## 1410012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "久しぶりだね、死の羽音。<line-break>いや、今はレイヴンと<line-break>呼んだ方がいいかな？<end>"
EN: "It's been a long time, Death's Wingbeat. Or should I call you Raven now?<end>"
KO: "오랜만이군, 죽음의 날갯소리. 아니, 지금은 레이븐이라고 부르는 게 좋을까?<end>"

## 1410013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひっ………！<end>"
EN: "Eek...!<end>"
KO: "히익………!<end>"

## 1410014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しのはおと、ですって？<line-break>…どういうこと？<end>"
EN: "Death's Wingbeat? What does that mean?<end>"
KO: "'시노하오토'라고? …무슨 뜻이지?<end>"

## 1410015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、君の後ろにいる<line-break>男はね、かつて…<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. You see, the man behind you was once...<end>"
KO: "후후, 네 뒤에 있는 남자는 말이지, 예전에….<end>"

## 1410016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめろ！　やめてくれ！<end>"
EN: "Stop! Please, stop!<end>"
KO: "그만둬! 그만해 줘!<end>"

## 1410017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その男は、施文院の暗殺組織<line-break>告死天使の一員だったんだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>それも死の羽音と呼ばれる<line-break>屈指の暗殺者…<line-break>…いや、殺人機械だった。<end>"
EN: "That man was once a member of the Angels of Death, the Inscription Order's assassination organization. And not just any member―he was one of its finest assassins, known as Death's Wingbeat. No, he was a killing machine.<end>"
KO: "그 남자는 시문원의 암살 조직 고사천사의 일원이었어. 그것도 죽음의 날갯소리라 불리던 손꼽히는 암살자… 아니, 살인 기계였지.<end>"

## 1410018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、彼は任務に失敗した。<line-break>殺人機械のはずの彼が、おびえという<line-break>不要な感情を持ってしまってね。<line-break>ほら、今も後ろでふるえている。<line-break>無様なものだね。フフフ。<line-break>こうなっては殺人機械も形なしだ。<end>"
EN: "But he failed his mission. Though meant to be a killing machine, he developed the useless emotion of fear. Look at him, still trembling back there. What a pathetic sight. Heh heh heh. So much for the killing machine.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그는 임무에 실패했지. 살인 기계여야 할 그가 두려움이라는 쓸데없는 감정을 품어 버렸거든. 봐, 지금도 뒤에서 떨고 있잖아. 참 꼴사납군. 후후후. 이래서는 살인 기계라는 이름도 무색하지.<end>"

## 1410019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、君たちも<line-break>彼の正体がわかったからには、<line-break>放っておくわけにはいかないだろう？<line-break>彼を渡してくれないか？<line-break>処分したいのでね。<end>"
EN: "Now that you know what he really is, you can hardly leave matters as they are, can you? Would you hand him over? I'd like to dispose of him.<end>"
KO: "자, 이제 너희도 그의 정체를 알았으니 그냥 둘 순 없겠지? 그를 넘겨주지 않겠나? 처리하고 싶어서 말이야.<end>"

## 1410020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファスを阻止する<end>"
EN: "Stop Elfas<end>"
KO: "엘파스를 저지한다<end>"

## 1410021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "黙って見ている<end>"
EN: "Watch in silence<end>"
KO: "말없이 지켜본다<end>"

## 1410022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　もしかして、<line-break>君は僕の邪魔をするのかい？<line-break>彼は暗殺者なんだよ？<end>"
EN: "Hm? Are you going to stand in my way? You do realize he's an assassin, don't you?<end>"
KO: "응? 혹시 네가 나를 방해하려는 거야? 그는 암살자라고?<end>"

## 1410023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レイヴンを<line-break>あなたに渡すわけにはいきません！<end>"
EN: "I won't let you take Raven!<end>"
KO: "레이븐을 당신에게 넘길 수는 없습니다!<end>"

## 1410024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ノ、ノエル…。<line-break>やめろ、君のかなう相手では…。<end>"
EN: "N-Noel... Don't. You can't defeat him...<end>"
KO: "…노, 노엘…. 그만둬, 네가 당해낼 상대가 아니야….<end>"

## 1410025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レイヴンは、<line-break>わたしの大切な仲間です！<line-break>あなたの好きにはさせません！<line-break>あなたが<line-break>どうしてもと言うのなら…！<end>"
EN: "Raven is a dear friend! I won't let you do whatever you please with him! If you insist...!<end>"
KO: "레이븐은 제 소중한 동료예요! 당신 마음대로 하게 두지 않겠습니다! 그래도 굳이 하겠다면…!<end>"

## 1410026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル…。<end>"
EN: "Noel...<end>"
KO: "노엘….<end>"

## 1410027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ。無限のソウルを持つ者を<line-break>ふたり同時に相手にしてまで<line-break>処分する価値は、彼にはないよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. He isn't worth disposing of if it means facing two Bearers of the Infinite Soul at once.<end>"
KO: "후후. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자 둘을 동시에 상대하면서까지 처리할 가치는 그에게 없어.<end>"

## 1410028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レイヴンをものみたいに言わないで！<line-break>彼には手を出させません！<end>"
EN: "Don't talk about Raven as if he were a thing! I won't let you touch him!<end>"
KO: "레이븐을 물건처럼 말하지 마! 그에게 손대게 두지 않겠습니다!<end>"

## 1410029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…彼には手を出さないで、か…。　　<line-break>フフ、姉さんもそんなことを<line-break>言っていたかな…。<line-break>今日のところは失礼させてもらうよ。<line-break>だが、死の羽音よ、やすらぎに<line-break>ひたる権利が、君にはあるのかな？<end>"
EN: "“Don't touch him,” is it? Heh. I seem to recall my sister saying much the same. I'll take my leave for today. But tell me, Death's Wingbeat: do you truly have the right to bask in peace?<end>"
KO: "…그에게 손대지 말라, 인가…. 후후, 누나도 그런 말을 했던가…. 오늘은 이만 실례하지. 하지만 죽음의 날갯소리여, 안식에 잠길 권리가 과연 네게 있을까?<end>"

## 1410030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…彼の言うとおりさ。<end>"
EN: "...He's right.<end>"
KO: "…그가 말한 대로야.<end>"

## 1410031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？<end>"
EN: "What?<end>"
KO: "어?<end>"

## 1410032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺には…殺人機械だった俺には<line-break>安らぎにひたる権利なんて<line-break>…ない。<end>"
EN: "I was a killing machine. I have no right... to live in peace.<end>"
KO: "나에게는… 살인기계였던 나에게는 평온에 잠길 권리 같은 건… 없어.<end>"

## 1410033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル、カフィン…。<line-break>世話になった。<line-break>許してくれとは言わない。<line-break>これで、告死天使たちが<line-break>君たちの前に現れることは<line-break>なくなるはずだ。<end>"
EN: "Noel, Coffin... Thank you for everything. I won't ask you to forgive me. This should keep the Angels of Death from appearing before you again.<end>"
KO: "노엘, 카핀… 신세 졌다. 용서해 달라는 말은 하지 않겠다. 이제 고사천사들이 너희 앞에 나타나는 일은 없을 거다.<end>"

## 1410034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待って！　待ってください！<end>"
EN: "Wait! Please, wait!<end>"
KO: "기다려! 기다려 주세요!<end>"

## 1410035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたしたちには…わたしには<line-break>レイヴンの力が必要なんです！<line-break>わたしたち、仲間なんですから！　　<line-break>昔のレイヴンが何をしていたのか、<line-break>わたしは知りません！　でも、<line-break>わたしには今のレイヴンが必要なの！<end>"
EN: "We... I need your strength, Raven! We're companions! I don't know what you did in the past, but I need the Raven you are now!<end>"
KO: "우리에게는… 나에게는 레이븐의 힘이 필요해요! 우리는 동료잖아요! 예전의 레이븐이 무슨 일을 했는지 저는 몰라요! 하지만 지금의 레이븐은 제게 필요해요!<end>"

## 1410036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ダメだ。俺がいると…<line-break>君に迷惑がかかる。<end>"
EN: "No. If I stay... I'll only bring you trouble.<end>"
KO: "안 돼. 내가 있으면… 네게 폐를 끼쳐.<end>"

## 1410037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レイヴン！<end>"
EN: "Raven!<end>"
KO: "레이븐!<end>"

## 1410038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ここまで<line-break>ノエルが言っているんですもの。<line-break>　<line-break>それでも出ていくって言うなら<line-break>あたしも黙っていないわ。<end>"
EN: "After everything Noel has said, if you still insist on leaving, I won't stand by and let you.<end>"
KO: "…노엘이 이렇게까지 말하고 있잖아. 그래도 나가겠다면 나도 가만있지 않을 거야.<end>"

## 1410039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このパーティのリーダーはお前だ。<line-break>お前がそれでいいというのなら<line-break>私はかまわんよ。<end>"
EN: "You're the leader of this party. If you're all right with that, I have no objection.<end>"
KO: "이 파티의 리더는 너다. 네가 그걸로 괜찮다면 나는 상관없어.<end>"

## 1410040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願いです、レイヴン。<line-break>行かないで。<end>"
EN: "Please, Raven. Don't go.<end>"
KO: "부탁이야, 레이븐. 가지 마.<end>"

## 1410041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ありがとう。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>君が俺を必要とする限り、<line-break>俺は君のそばに…いるよ。<end>"
EN: "...Thank you. As long as you need me, I'll... stay by your side.<end>"
KO: "…고마워. 네가 날 필요로 하는 한, 난 네 곁에… 있을게.<end>"

## 1410052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。あの子とともに、<line-break>この海の向こうを目指しますか。<end>"
EN: "I see. So you mean to venture beyond this sea with him?<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까. 그 아이와 함께 이 바다 너머를 향하시겠습니까.<end>"

## 1410053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ、あの子が、<line-break>自分の旅に他人を誘うなんて、<line-break>本当に珍しい。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. It is rare indeed for him to invite someone else on his journey.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 그 아이가 자기 여행에 남을 초대하다니 정말 드문 일이네.<end>"

## 1410054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、ひょっとしたら、　　　<line-break>あの子が<value:$28>の旅に<line-break>　<line-break>ついていくということなのかも<line-break>しれませんね。<end>"
EN: "Or perhaps he is the one joining <value:$28> on the journey.<end>"
KO: "아니, 어쩌면 그 아이가 <value:$28>의 여행에 따라간다는 뜻일지도 모르겠군요.<end>"

## 1410055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今だから言いますが、あの子は、<line-break>内心いつも悩んでいました。<line-break> <line-break>その、どこか近寄りがたいような<line-break>雰囲気のせいもありますが…。<end>"
EN: "I can tell you this now: deep down, he was always troubled. That rather unapproachable air of his was partly to blame...<end>"
KO: "이제 와 말하지만 그 아이는 속으로 늘 고민하고 있었어요. 어딘가 가까이하기 힘든 분위기 탓도 있었지만….<end>"

## 1410056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これまでネメアが要求されてきたものは、<line-break>その名の重みであるとか、<line-break>卓越した強さであるとか…<line-break>何にせよ、ネメアという人間<line-break>そのものではないことが多かった。<end>"
EN: "Until now, people have looked to Nemea for the weight his name carries, or for his extraordinary strength... Whatever they wanted, more often than not, it was not Nemea himself.<end>"
KO: "지금까지 네메아에게 요구된 것은 그 이름의 무게라든가, 탁월한 강함이라든가… 어쨌든 네메아라는 인간 그 자체가 아닌 경우가 많았어.<end>"

## 1410057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "強くあるということは、<line-break>同時につらいことでもあり、<line-break> <line-break>強いまま生き続けるというのは、<line-break>とても難しいことなのです。<end>"
EN: "To be strong is also to suffer, and it is very difficult to go on living while remaining strong.<end>"
KO: "강하다는 것은 동시에 괴로운 일이기도 하고, 강한 채로 살아간다는 건 아주 어려운 일입니다.<end>"

## 1410058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いや、それは、あなたが一番よく<line-break>知っていることですね、<line-break><value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "No... but you know that better than anyone, don't you, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "…아니, 그건 당신이 가장 잘 알고 있는 일이겠군요, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1410059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたと出会えて、あの子は、<line-break>心のくびきからようやく<line-break>解き放たれようとしているのでしょう。<line-break>この一連の戦いで何かを<line-break>得たというのは、<line-break>あの子も一緒だったというわけです。<end>"
EN: "Meeting you may finally be setting his heart free. It seems he, too, gained something from all these battles.<end>"
KO: "당신과 만나면서 그 아이는 마음의 굴레에서 드디어 풀려나려는 것이겠죠. 이 일련의 싸움에서 무언가를 얻은 건 그 아이도 마찬가지였다는 겁니다.<end>"

## 1410060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、そろそろだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, it's time.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 슬슬 때가 됐다.<end>"

## 1410061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…父よ、何と言おうが、<line-break>あの時死のうとしていた私を救い、<line-break>育ててくれたのは、あなただ。<line-break>そのことには感謝している。<end>"
EN: "Father... Whatever else I may say, you saved me when I was trying to die, and you raised me. For that, I am grateful.<end>"
KO: "…아버지여, 뭐라 하든 그때 죽으려던 나를 구하고 키워 준 것은 당신이다. 그 점은 감사하고 있다.<end>"

## 1410062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、この旅に出れば、<line-break>もうあなたには…。<end>"
EN: "But once I set out on this journey, I can no longer...<end>"
KO: "하지만 이 여행을 떠나면 이제 당신에게는….<end>"

## 1410063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、今度こそ、<line-break>お土産を待っていますよ、ネメア。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. This time, I shall expect a souvenir, Nemea.<end>"
KO: "후후, 이번에는 꼭 선물을 기다리고 있을게요, 네메아.<end>"

## 1410064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>フッ、あなたらしい別れの言葉だ。<end>"
EN: "... Heh. A farewell so very like you.<end>"
KO: "………………. 훗, 당신다운 작별 인사군.<end>"

## 1410065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では行こうか、<value:$28>。<line-break>行ける限り、地の果てまでも。<end>"
EN: "Then let us go, <value:$28>. As far as we can―even to the ends of the earth.<end>"
KO: "그럼 가자, <value:$28>. 갈 수 있는 데까지, 세상 끝까지.<end>"

## 1410066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、ゼネさんが<line-break>ファーロス家の御曹司だったとはねぇ。<line-break>スラムの住人一同びっくりだったよ。<end>"
EN: "Who would've thought Zene was the heir to House Pharos? Everyone in the Slums was stunned.<end>"
KO: "그런데 제네 씨가 파로스 가문의 도련님이었다니. 슬럼 주민들 모두 깜짝 놀랐어.<end>"

## 1410067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへ、悪かった。<line-break>だますつもりはなかったんだが、<line-break>ま、許してくれや。<end>"
EN: "Heh, sorry about that. I didn't mean to fool you. Forgive me, all right?<end>"
KO: "헤헤, 미안. 속일 생각은 없었는데, 뭐 용서해 줘.<end>"

## 1410068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そりゃ、もちろんですよ。<line-break>ゼネテスさんのおかげで、<line-break>この国は助かったんだから！<end>"
EN: "Of course! This country was saved thanks to you, Xenetes!<end>"
KO: "그야 물론이죠. 제네테스 씨 덕분에 이 나라가 살아났으니까요!<end>"

## 1410069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の力じゃないさ。<line-break>必死に国を守ろうとした<line-break>みんなのおかげだ。<end>"
EN: "It wasn't me. It was everyone who fought so hard to defend this country.<end>"
KO: "내 힘이 아니야. 필사적으로 나라를 지키려 한 모두의 덕분이지.<end>"

## 1410070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まったくだよ。<line-break>感謝しなきゃね。<line-break> <line-break>貴族にだって、<line-break>ゼネさんみたいなのがいるんだ。<line-break>この国は変わるかもしれないよ。<end>"
EN: "You're right. We owe them our thanks. If even the nobility has people like you, Zene, maybe this country really can change.<end>"
KO: "정말 그래. 감사해야겠네. 귀족 중에도 제네 씨 같은 사람이 있구나. 이 나라도 변할지 몰라.<end>"

## 1410071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにねー、ゼネテスには、<line-break>さいこーの相棒がいるんだもんね。<end>"
EN: "And Xenetes has the best partner ever!<end>"
KO: "그리고 말이지—, 제네테스에겐 최고의 동료가 있잖아.<end>"

## 1410072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。<line-break>ハンナはよくわかってるな。<end>"
EN: "That's right. Hannah knows what she's talking about.<end>"
KO: "그래. 한나는 잘 알고 있구나.<end>"

## 1410073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee.<end>"
KO: "후후후.<end>"

## 1410074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところで、ゼネさん、<line-break>あんたこんな所にいていいのかい？<end>"
EN: "By the way, Zene, should you really be hanging around a place like this?<end>"
KO: "그런데 제네 씨, 이런 데 있어도 괜찮아?<end>"

## 1410075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　ああ、まあ、なんだ、<line-break>宮廷は退屈なんでね。<line-break>ちっと相棒と遊びにでも行こうかと。<end>"
EN: "Hm? Oh, well, you know... Court's boring. Thought I'd go have a little fun with my partner.<end>"
KO: "응? 아, 뭐랄까, 궁정은 지루해서 말이야. 잠깐 파트너랑 놀러라도 갈까 해서.<end>"

## 1410076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>。<line-break>ギルドでいい仕事を　　　　　　　<line-break>見つけたんだわ。<line-break>どうだ、ちょっくら<line-break>やってみないか？<line-break>古代王国の秘宝探しなんだけどよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. I found a good job at the Guild. What do you say―want to give it a shot? We'd be hunting for treasure from an ancient kingdom.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 길드에서 괜찮은 일을 찾았어. 어때, 한번 해 보지 않을래? 고대 왕국의 비보 찾기야.<end>"

## 1410077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、決まりだ！<line-break>行こうぜ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Good, it's settled! Let's go, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 결정이다! 가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1410078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスも<value:$28>も<line-break>結局、冒険好きなのね。<line-break>子供ねー。<end>"
EN: "In the end, you and Xenetes both just love adventures, don't you, <value:$28>? You're both such kids.<end>"
KO: "제네테스도 <value:$28>도 결국 모험을 좋아하는구나. 어린애네~.<end>"

## 1410079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、来たか、<value:$28>。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>今日は、ちょいとお前さんに<line-break>頼みたいことがあってよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, you made it, <value:$28>. There's something I'd like your help with today.<end>"
KO: "오, 왔나, <value:$28>. 오늘은 네게 부탁할 게 좀 있어서 말이야.<end>"

## 1410080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…叔母貴が死んじまってから、<line-break>やっぱり何かしら変わっちまったよな。<end>"
EN: "Ever since my aunt died, things really haven't been the same.<end>"
KO: "…숙모가 죽고 나서 역시 뭔가 달라졌지.<end>"

## 1410081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "変わりたくないって言ったとこで、<line-break>変わらずにゃいられない…。<line-break> <line-break>それだけ叔母貴の存在が<line-break>大きかったってことだものな。<end>"
EN: "You may not want to change, but you can't stay the same... That just shows how much my aunt meant to me.<end>"
KO: "변하고 싶지 않다고 해도 변하지 않을 수는 없어…. 그만큼 숙모의 존재가 컸다는 뜻이니까.<end>"

## 1410082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、あらためて一杯、ってのは<line-break>俺があっちがわへ行くまで<line-break>待っててもらうとして、<line-break>それまで、<line-break>叔母貴のことはできるだけ鮮明に<line-break>記憶に留めておきたいって思ってな。<end>"
EN: "Well, she'll have to wait for that next drink until I cross over to the other side myself. Until then, I want to keep my memories of her as vivid as I can.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 다시 한잔하는 건 내가 저쪽 세상으로 갈 때까지 기다려 달라고 하고, 그때까지는 아주머니 모습을 가능한 선명하게 기억해 두고 싶어서 말이야.<end>"

## 1410083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺も、ひとつ、<line-break>料理でもやってみようと思うんだが…<line-break>どうかな？<end>"
EN: "So I thought I might try my hand at cooking... What do you think?<end>"
KO: "나도 한번 요리를 해 볼까 하는데… 어때?<end>"

## 1410084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なんだよ、<value:$28>？<line-break>その、けげんそうな顔は？<line-break>俺の腕が疑わしいってのか？<end>"
EN: "W-What is it, <value:$28>? What's with that doubtful look? You don't think I can cook?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐야, <value:$28>? 그 미심쩍은 얼굴은? 내 실력이 의심스러운 거냐?<end>"

## 1410085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、さすがにはっきり言ってくれるぜ。<line-break>…でも、ま、無理かどうか、<line-break>いっちょつきあってくれよ。<end>"
EN: "Y-You really don't mince words. Still, humor me and let's see whether I can pull it off.<end>"
KO: "역시 말은 확실하게 하는군…. 그래도 뭐, 무리인지 아닌지 한번 어울려 줘.<end>"

## 1410086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "外に飛び出さねえだけで、<line-break>これも大冒険ってことに<line-break>違いないだろ。<end>冒険には信頼できる仲間が必要だ。<line-break>そう思い出させてくれたのは、<line-break><value:$28>、お前さんだ。<end>"
EN: "We may not be venturing outside, but that doesn't make this any less of a grand adventure.<end>Every adventure needs a companion you can trust. You're the one who reminded me of that, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "밖으로 뛰쳐나가지 않았을 뿐, 이것도 대모험인 건 틀림없잖아.<end>모험에는 믿을 수 있는 동료가 필요하다. 그걸 다시 떠올리게 해 준 건 <value:$28>, 바로 너야.<end>"

## 1410089

## 1410090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1410091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦いを勝負と呼ぶ。<line-break>だが、我には<line-break>その意味は失われていた。<line-break>いつしか勝利のみが<line-break>運命づけられていた。<line-break>我は失うことを失った。<end>"
EN: "We call a battle a contest. Yet for me, that word had lost its meaning. Before I knew it, victory alone had been ordained for me. I had lost the ability to lose.<end>"
KO: "싸움을 승부라 부른다. 하지만 내게 그 의미는 사라져 있었다. 어느새 승리만이 운명지어졌고, 나는 패배하는 법을 잃었다.<end>"

## 1410092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "頂点には<line-break>ひとり分の場所しかない。<line-break>敵する者なき、孤独な場所だ。<end>"
EN: "At the summit, there is room for only one. It is a lonely place, where none can challenge you.<end>"
KO: "정상에는 한 사람 몫의 자리밖에 없다. 맞설 자 없는 고독한 자리다.<end>"

## 1410093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、うぬが現れた。<line-break>そして運命という檻を壊した。<line-break>我に戦いを取り戻した。<end>"
EN: "But then you appeared. You broke the cage called fate and gave battle back to me.<end>"
KO: "하지만 네가 나타났다. 그리고 운명이라는 우리를 부쉈지. 내게 싸움을 되돌려 줬다.<end>"

## 1410094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "返礼だ、頂点に立つ者よ。<line-break>こたびは、我がうぬに挑み、<line-break>うぬの孤独を壊そう。<end>"
EN: "Now I shall return the favor, you who stand at the summit. This time, I shall challenge you and shatter your solitude.<end>"
KO: "답례다, 정상에 선 자여. 이번에는 내가 네게 도전해 네 고독을 깨뜨리겠다.<end>"

## 1410095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゆくぞ。<end>"
EN: "...Here I come.<end>"
KO: "…간다.<end>"

## 1410096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゆくぞっ。<end>"
EN: "…Let's go!<end>"
KO: "…간다.<end>"

## 1410101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "女王様、はじめてのお務めは<line-break>いかがでしたか？<end>"
EN: "Your Majesty, how did your first official duty go?<end>"
KO: "여왕님, 첫 임무는 어떠셨습니까?<end>"

## 1410102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…とても緊張しました。<line-break>周りのみなさんが私のことを<line-break>見ているのですもの。<end>"
EN: "I was terribly nervous. Everyone was watching me.<end>"
KO: "…정말 긴장했어요. 주변 사람들이 모두 저를 보고 있었으니까요.<end>"

## 1410103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはきっと、女王様のことを<line-break>とても魅力的な方だと、<line-break>みなさまが思われたからですわ。<end>"
EN: "Surely that was because everyone found Your Majesty so very charming.<end>"
KO: "그건 분명 모두가 여왕님을 아주 매력적인 분이라고 생각했기 때문일 거예요.<end>"

## 1410104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私が…魅力的？<end>"
EN: "Me... charming?<end>"
KO: "내가… 매력적이라고?<end>"

## 1410105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。<end>"
EN: "Yes.<end>"
KO: "네.<end>"

## 1410106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（私のことを、そんな風に<line-break>　思ってくれる方が<line-break>　あの方以外にいるなんて…。）<line-break>（でも、私は、あの方だけが<line-break>　そう思ってくれれば十分…。）<end>"
EN: "(To think anyone besides that person could see me that way...) (But as long as that person does, that's enough for me...)<end>"
KO: "(나를 그렇게 생각해 주는 사람이 그분 말고도 있다니….) (하지만 나는 그분 한 사람만 그렇게 생각해 주면 충분해….)<end>"

## 1410107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（魅力的…？　私が…？）<end>"
EN: "(Charming...? Me...?)<end>"
KO: "(매력적…? 내가…?)<end>"

## 1410108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうされたのですか、女王様？<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Is something the matter, Your Majesty?<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이십니까, 여왕 폐하?<end>"

## 1410109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、いえ。なんでもありません。<line-break>少しぼうっとしてしまいました。<end>"
EN: "N-no. It's nothing. My mind wandered for a moment.<end>"
KO: "아, 아니요. 아무것도 아닙니다. 잠깐 멍하니 있었어요.<end>"

## 1410110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きっと、はじめてのご体験で<line-break>お疲れになったのでしょう。<line-break>今日はごゆっくりとお休みください。<end>"
EN: "Your first official duty must have left you tired. Please rest well today.<end>"
KO: "분명 처음 겪으신 일이라 피곤하셨을 겁니다. 오늘은 푹 쉬세요.<end>"

## 1410111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。<line-break>どうもありがとう。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Thank you very much.<end>"
KO: "응. 정말 고마워.<end>"

## 1410112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、失礼いたします。<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"

KO: "그럼, 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1410113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は本当に、疲れたわ…。<line-break>文書の最後に署名を書くだけだった<line-break>けれど、少し曲がってしまったし…。<line-break>もっと、がんばらなくては。<end>"
EN: "I'm truly exhausted today... All I had to do was sign my name at the bottom of a document, but even that came out a little crooked... I must try harder.<end>"
KO: "오늘은 정말 피곤하네…. 문서 끝에 서명만 하면 됐는데 조금 삐뚤어졌고…. 더 열심히 해야겠어.<end>"

## 1410114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様ですか？<line-break>どうぞ、お入りになって。<end>"
EN: "Oh, is that you, <value:$28>? Please, come in.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님이십니까? 들어오세요.<end>"

## 1410115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ、<value:$28>様も<line-break>今日は一日、ご苦労様でした。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>そうそう。<value:$28>様を<line-break>お呼びしたのは、これを<line-break>受け取ってもらいたかったからです。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. You've had a long day too, <value:$28>. Oh, that's right. I asked you here, <value:$28>, because I wanted you to have this.<end>"
KO: "우후후, <value:$28> 님도 오늘 하루 수고 많으셨어요. 그리고 <value:$28> 님을 부른 건 이걸 받아 주셨으면 해서예요.<end>"

## 1410116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私が初めて誰の助けも借りずに<line-break>焼いたクッキーです。<line-break>召しあがってください。<end>"

KO: "제가 처음으로 누구 도움도 받지 않고 구운 쿠키예요. 드셔 주세요.<end>"

## 1410117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、待ってください。<line-break>感想はあとで…、<line-break>ここでは食べないで…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, wait. Tell me what you think later... Please don't eat them here.<end>"
KO: "아, 기다려 주세요. 감상은 나중에… 여기서는 먹지 마세요….<end>"

## 1410118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの…。<line-break>どう、ですか？<end>"
EN: "Um... Well? How are they?<end>"
KO: "저, 저기…. 어때요?<end>"

## 1410119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…よかった。<end>"
EN: "Oh, good.<end>"
KO: "…다행이다.<end>"

## 1410120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、これからも、いろんなことを<line-break>ひとりでできるようになりたいのです。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様と一緒にいても<line-break>迷惑にならないように<line-break>なりたいのです。<end>"
EN: "I want to learn to do all sorts of things on my own. I want to be able to stay with you without becoming a burden, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "저, 앞으로도 여러 가지 일을 혼자 할 수 있게 되고 싶어요. <value:$28> 님과 함께 있어도 폐를 끼치지 않게 되고 싶어요.<end>"

## 1410121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですから、<value:$28>様。<line-break>どうか、いつまでも、私の近くに<line-break>いてください。<end>"
EN: "So please, <value:$28>... stay close to me, always.<end>"
KO: "그러니 <value:$28>님. 부디 언제까지나 제 곁에 있어 주세요.<end>"

## 1410122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "고마워요. …기뻐요, <value:$28> 님.<end>"

## 1410123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1410129

## 1410130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1410131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1410132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1410133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかげで、胸のつかえが取れました。<line-break>隣の間へ移りましょう。<line-break>お茶のしたくができている頃です。<end>"

## 1410134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこに用意しているクッキーは、<line-break>あの…私が今朝初めてひとりで<line-break>焼いたものなのです…。<line-break>うまくできたと思うので…<line-break>どうか、一緒に召し上がってください。<end>"

KO: "거기 준비해 둔 쿠키는… 제가 오늘 아침 처음 혼자 구운 거예요…. 잘된 것 같으니… 부디 함께 드셔 주세요.<end>"

## 1410135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1410136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml

JP: "あの、<value:$28>様。<end>"

KO: "저기, <value:$28> 님.<end>"

## 1410138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたがいらしてくれれば、私、<line-break>何事にも安心して挑戦できそうです。<end>"

## 1410139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "嬉しいです、<value:$28>様。<end>"

## 1410140

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1410141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おそいなぁ、<value:$28>。<line-break>まさか、忘れちゃったんじゃ<line-break>ないよなぁ。<end>"

KO: "늦네, <value:$28>. 설마 잊은 건 아니겠지.<end>"

## 1410142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう！　ようやく、みんなを説得して<line-break>自由に冒険できるようになったって<line-break>言うのにぃ！<line-break>何やってるんだよぉ、<line-break><value:$28>…。<line-break>　<end>"

KO: "정말! 겨우 모두를 설득해서 자유롭게 모험할 수 있게 됐는데! 뭐 하는 거야, <value:$28>….<end>"

## 1410143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>が来てくれないと<line-break>冒険なんて、できないんだぞ…。<end>"
EN: "I can't go adventuring if you don't come, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>이 와 주지 않으면 모험 같은 건 할 수 없다고….<end>"

## 1410144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれ？　ボク、はじめて冒険の旅に<line-break>出たときは、ひとりでも<line-break>やっていけるって思ってたのに…。<line-break>今では、<value:$28>が<line-break>いないと、旅に出るのが<line-break>なんだか、恐くなってる…。<end>"
EN: "That's strange. When I first set out on an adventure, I thought I'd be fine on my own... But now, the thought of setting off without you is a little frightening, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "어라? 난 처음 모험 여행을 떠났을 땐 혼자서도 잘할 수 있다고 생각했는데…. 지금은 <value:$28>가 없으면 여행을 떠나는 게 왠지 무서워졌어….<end>"

## 1410145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君に頼らせて、って言ったら、<line-break><value:$28>は、勝手だって<line-break>怒るかな？<end>"
EN: "If I asked to lean on you, would you be angry with me for being selfish, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "네게 의지하게 해 달라고 하면, <value:$28>는 제멋대로라고 화낼까?<end>"

## 1410146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、<value:$28>って、<line-break>とっても頼りがいがあるし、<line-break> <line-break><value:$28>に頼るのって、<line-break>とっても気持ちがいいし…。<end>"
EN: "But you're so dependable, <value:$28>, and it feels so good to be able to lean on you, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "하지만 <value:$28>은 정말 믿음직하고, <value:$28>에게 기대면 정말 마음이 편하고….<end>"

## 1410147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いっそ、ボクの姉さんになってよ！<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>…な、なんて言ったら、変かな？<line-break>やっぱり、変？<end>"
EN: "Why don't you just become my big sister? I-is that a strange thing to say? It is, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "차라리 내 누나가 되어 줘!   …라, 라고 하면 이상할까? 역시 이상해?<end>"

## 1410148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1410149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつのまにか、<line-break>ボクにとっての<line-break>冒険の旅っていうのは、<line-break><value:$28>と<line-break>一緒にいることに<line-break>なっちゃったんだな…。<end>"

## 1410150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "빨리 와 줘, <value:$28>….<end>"

## 1410151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1410152

## 1410153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1410154

## 1410156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、でも、元はと言えば<line-break><value:$28>が約束の時間に<line-break>来ないのが原因だったんだよ。<line-break>ボク、すっごく長い間、<line-break>ここで、待ってたんだから。<end>"

## 1410157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1410158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あは、あはははは…。<line-break>ゴメン、ボクが勘違いしてたみたい。<line-break>　<line-break>ボク、少しでも早く<line-break><value:$28>と冒険の旅に<line-break>出たくて、うずうずしてたからさ。<end>"
EN: "Ah... ahahaha... Sorry, I suppose I got the time wrong. I was just so eager to set off on an adventure with you as soon as possible, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아하, 아하하하…. 미안, 내가 착각했나 봐. 조금이라도 빨리 <value:$28>과 모험을 떠나고 싶어서 몸이 근질거렸거든.<end>"

## 1410159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1410160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだ、シェスター？<line-break>精霊は、<line-break>呼びかけに答えてくれているか？<end>"
EN: "How is it, Shester? Are the spirits answering your call?<end>"
KO: "어때, 셰스터? 정령은 부름에 응답하고 있나?<end>"

## 1410161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや～、はやいもんだよねぇ。<line-break>もう、バイアシオン大陸なんて<line-break>ちっちゃくて見えないわ。<line-break>あんな、ちっぽけな陸の上で<line-break>あたしたちは戦ってたんだよね。<line-break>ここからこっちは自分の国だって。<end>"
EN: "Well, that was quick, wasn't it? The Vyashion Continent's already so tiny I can't even see it. And to think we fought on that little scrap of land, saying, “Everything from here on is our country.”<end>"
KO: "이야~ 빠르네. 벌써 바이아시온 대륙은 작아서 안 보일 정도야. 저 조그만 땅 위에서 우리는 싸우고 있었던 거지. 여기서부터는 자기 나라라고 말하면서.<end>"

## 1410162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、ディンガルとロストールが<line-break>仲よくなっちゃったから、<line-break>戦争はもうおしまい。<line-break>これから、バイアシオンは<line-break>ひとつになっていくんだろうね。<end>"
EN: "But now that Dyneskal and Rostorl have made peace, the war's over. I suppose Vyashion will gradually become one.<end>"
KO: "하지만 딘갈과 로스톨이 사이좋아졌으니 전쟁은 이제 끝이야. 앞으로 바이아시온은 하나가 되어 가겠지.<end>"

## 1410163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ってなると、あたしたちみたいな<line-break>戦うことが仕事っていう物騒な連中は<line-break>出て行くしかないよねぇ、やっぱ。<end>"
EN: "Which means dangerous sorts like us, the ones who fight for a living, have no choice but to move on.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 우리처럼 싸우는 게 직업인 위험한 녀석들은 결국 떠날 수밖에 없겠네.<end>"

## 1410164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや～、力を持ちすぎた者の宿命って<line-break>やつですか？　しょうがないよね。<end>"
EN: "Ah, the fate of those with too much power. Is that what this is? Well, it can't be helped.<end>"
KO: "이야~, 힘을 너무 많이 가진 자의 숙명이라는 건가요? 어쩔 수 없죠.<end>"

## 1410165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすくす。<line-break>あたしと一緒にするなって<line-break>顔したって、ダメだよん。<line-break>なんてったって、<value:$28>は<line-break>世界を救った最強の勇者様だもんね。<line-break>要するに、一番物騒だってこと。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. Don't give me that look, as if you're nothing like me. It won't work. After all, <value:$28> is the mightiest Hero, savior of the world. In other words, the most dangerous one of all.<end>"
KO: "킥킥. 나랑 같은 취급 하지 말라는 표정을 지어도 소용없어. 어쨌든 <value:$28>는 세계를 구한 최강의 용사님이잖아. 한마디로 제일 위험한 사람이란 뜻이지.<end>"

## 1410166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、いいじゃないですか？<line-break>英雄って言われてたネメア様だって<line-break>またどっかに行っちゃったし。<line-break>こうなったら、あたしたちも<line-break>次の大陸に渡って、国のひとつや<line-break>ふたつ手に入れてみようじゃん？<end>"
EN: "What's wrong with that? Even Lord Nemea, who was hailed as a hero, has gone off somewhere again. So why don't we cross over to the next continent and take a country or two for ourselves?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 괜찮지 않아요? 영웅이라 불리던 네메아 님도 또 어디론가 가 버렸고. 이왕 이렇게 된 거 우리도 다음 대륙으로 건너가 나라 하나둘쯤 손에 넣어 보자고요?<end>"

## 1410167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なになに？　そんなこっちゃ、<line-break>戦争が目的になっちゃう、　<line-break>って…？<line-break>そ、ね。<line-break>わかってる。<line-break>あたしも、そうだって思うよ。<end>"
EN: "What's that? If we did that, war itself would become the goal? Yes, I know. I think so too.<end>"
KO: "뭐라고? 그러면 전쟁 자체가 목적이 돼 버린다고…? 그래. 알아. 나도 그렇다고 생각해.<end>"

## 1410168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦争は国を手に入れるための<line-break>目的であって、戦争をするのが<line-break>目的ってんじゃダメだよね。<end>"
EN: "War should be a means of taking a country. It shouldn't be waged for its own sake.<end>"
KO: "전쟁은 나라를 얻기 위한 수단이지, 전쟁 자체가 목적이 되어선 안 되지.<end>"

## 1410169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "するにしたってさ、<line-break>それじゃいい戦いはできないでしょ、<line-break>ってね…。<end>"
EN: "Even if we do fight, we couldn't have a good fight that way... right?<end>"
KO: "한다고 해도, 그러면 좋은 싸움은 못 하잖아… 그런 얘기지.<end>"

## 1410170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しっかし！<line-break>あー、このだだっ広い海原ってやつ、<line-break>最高！<line-break>神速の戦術家、青い死神も、<line-break>最強の英雄も、もう、<line-break>どっかあっちの波間の向こう。<end>"
EN: "But wow! This huge open sea is the best! The lightning-fast tactician―the Blue Reaper―and the mightiest hero are already somewhere out there, beyond the waves.<end>"
KO: "그나저나! 아, 이 끝없이 넓은 바다라는 거 최고다! 신속의 전술가도, 푸른 사신도, 최강의 영웅도 이제 저 파도 너머 어딘가야.<end>"

## 1410171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしもあんたも、ここでは<line-break>ただ１人の人間でしかないんだあね。<line-break> <line-break>ねえ、<value:$28>、<line-break>これってさ、<line-break>最高にワクワクすることじゃん？<end>"
EN: "Out here, you and I are just people. Hey, <value:$28>, isn't that incredibly exciting?<end>"
KO: "나도 당신도 여기서는 그저 한 사람의 인간일 뿐이야. 있잖아, <value:$28>, 이거 정말 최고로 두근거리지 않아?<end>"

## 1410172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたと来てよかったよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break> <line-break>さあこの調子で、どこまでも<line-break>行ってやりましょうかね！<end>"
EN: "I'm glad I came with you, <value:$28>. Now let's keep going like this, as far as we possibly can!<end>"
KO: "너랑 와서 다행이야, <value:$28>. 자, 이 기세로 어디까지든 가 보자고!<end>"

## 1410173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "全速・前進～！<end>"
EN: "Full speed ahead!<end>"
KO: "전속력・전진~!<end>"

## 1410174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ホントのこと言うとさ、<line-break>国だとか領地だとか、そんなもん、<line-break>結構どうでもいいんだよね～。<end>"
EN: "Oh, if I'm being honest, I don't really care much about countries or territory or any of that.<end>"
KO: "아, 솔직히 말하면 나라니 영지니 하는 건 사실 꽤 아무래도 좋아~.<end>"

## 1410175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしは<value:$28>と<line-break>一緒に行くだけだから。<line-break> <line-break>あたしはただ、<value:$28>と<line-break>一緒にいれば、ずっと笑って<line-break>いられるんじゃないかって思っただけ。<end>"
EN: "I'm just going with <value:$28>. I only thought that if I stayed with <value:$28>, maybe I could go on laughing forever.<end>"
KO: "난 <value:$28>와 같이 갈 뿐이야. 난 그냥 <value:$28>와 함께 있으면 계속 웃을 수 있을 것 같다고 생각했을 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1410176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心から笑うことができるあたしを<line-break>見つけられるんじゃないかってね。<end>"
EN: "I thought maybe I'd find the me who can laugh from the heart.<end>"
KO: "진심으로 웃을 수 있는 나를 찾을 수 있지 않을까 해서.<end>"

## 1410177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、元気出していこう～！<line-break>次の大陸があたしたちをよんでる～！<end>"
EN: "Come on, let's keep our spirits up! The next continent is calling us!<end>"
KO: "자, 기운 내서 가자~! 다음 대륙이 우리를 부르고 있어~!<end>"

## 1410178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は今まで、そんな当たり前のことに<line-break>気付きはしなかった…。<end>"
EN: "Until now, I had never noticed something so obvious.<end>"
KO: "나는 지금까지 그런 당연한 사실조차 깨닫지 못했어….<end>"

## 1410179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰かのために生きること、<line-break>誰かを愛するということとは、<line-break>どういうことなのかを、<line-break>君が教えてくれたおかげだ…。<end>"
EN: "You taught me what it means to live for someone, and what it means to love someone.<end>"
KO: "누군가를 위해 살아가는 것, 누군가를 사랑한다는 게 무엇인지 네가 가르쳐 준 덕분이야….<end>"

## 1410180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君と、もっと親しくなりたかった…。<line-break>だけど、僕はもう旅立たねばならない…。<line-break>最後まで言えなかったことを、<line-break>今、言うよ。<end>"
EN: "I wanted us to grow closer... But now I must depart. There's something I couldn't say until the very end. I'll say it now.<end>"
KO: "너와 좀 더 친해지고 싶었어…. 하지만 나는 이제 떠나야 해…. 끝까지 말하지 못했던 걸 지금 말할게.<end>"

## 1410181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えと、誰にも気づかれなかったよね。<line-break>よかった。昨日のうちから<line-break>準備しておいて。<end>"
EN: "Um, no one noticed me, right? Good. I'm glad I got everything ready yesterday.<end>"
KO: "저기, 아무도 눈치 못 챘지? 다행이다. 어제부터 준비해 둬.<end>"

## 1410182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたしの生まれた町。<line-break>わたしとカフィンとレイヴンと<line-break>そして、あの人とで、取り戻した町。<line-break>リベルダムやロストールみたいに<line-break>おっきくて、にぎやかじゃないけど、<line-break>わたしの大好きなシルヴァ。<end>"
EN: "The town where I was born. The town Coffin, Raven, that person, and I won back together. It isn't as big or bustling as Liberdam or Rostorl, but Silva is the town I love.<end>"
KO: "내가 태어난 마을. 나와 카핀과 레이븐, 그리고 그 사람과 함께 되찾은 마을. 리벨덤이나 로스톨처럼 크고 번화하진 않지만 내가 정말 좋아하는 실바.<end>"

## 1410183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、みんな知らない。<line-break>カフィンとレイヴンが、命をかけて<line-break>ソウルを取り戻してくれたことを。<line-break>わたしだけが命の恩人で、<line-break>カフィンたちは記憶の外…。<line-break>そんなの、もう耐えられない。<end>"
EN: "But no one knows. No one knows that Coffin and Raven risked their lives to recover the Soul. I'm the only one remembered as the savior, while Coffin and the others have been forgotten... I can't bear it anymore.<end>"
KO: "하지만 아무도 몰라. 카핀과 레이븐이 목숨을 걸고 소울을 되찾아 준 걸. 나만 생명의 은인이고 카핀들은 기억 밖에 있어…. 그런 건 이제 못 견디겠어.<end>"

## 1410184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "急に旅に出るなんて<line-break>書き置きを見たら<line-break>びっくりするだろうな。<line-break>ごめんなさい、<line-break>お父様、お母様。<line-break>でも、わたしは…。<end>"
EN: "They'll be so surprised when they find my note saying I've suddenly left on a journey. I'm sorry, Father. Mother. But I...<end>"
KO: "갑자기 여행을 떠난다는 메모를 보면 놀라시겠지. 죄송해요, 아버지, 어머니. 그래도 저는….<end>"

## 1410185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>おはようございます！<end>"
EN: "Oh, good morning, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 좋은 아침이에요!<end>"

## 1410187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ、あの、<value:$28>さんが<line-break>わたしの意見に賛成してくれて、<line-break>とってもうれしいんです。　　　　　　<line-break>カフィンもレイヴンも、<line-break><value:$28>さんと一緒だったら<line-break>安心して見守ってくれると思いますし。<end>"
EN: "No, I mean, I'm so happy you agreed with me, <value:$28>. And if I'm traveling with you, <value:$28>, I think Coffin and Raven can watch over me without worrying.<end>"
KO: "아니, 그게, <value:$28> 님이 제 의견에 찬성해 줘서 정말 기뻐요. 카핀도 레이븐도 <value:$28> 님과 함께라면 안심하고 지켜봐 줄 것 같고요.<end>"

## 1410188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、お父様とお母様にも内緒で<line-break>こうして出てきちゃったんです。<line-break>　<line-break>その、本当にわたしなんかで<line-break>いいんでしたら、お願いします。<line-break>一緒に連れていってください！<end>"
EN: "That's why I slipped away without even telling Father and Mother. So, if you're really sure you want someone like me... please, take me with you!<end>"
KO: "그래서 아버님과 어머님께도 비밀로 하고 이렇게 나와 버렸어요. 그, 정말 저 같은 사람도 괜찮다면 부탁드릴게요. 함께 데려가 주세요!<end>"

## 1410189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたし、がんばりますから！<end>"
EN: "I'll do my very best!<end>"
KO: "저, 열심히 할 테니까요!<end>"

## 1410190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ええ。実はお父様とお母様の　　<line-break>ことは心残りなんです。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、旅に出るって言っちゃったら<line-break>絶対ダメって言われるから…！<end>"
EN: "...Yes. I do feel bad about leaving Father and Mother behind. But if I told them I was going on a journey, they'd never let me go!<end>"
KO: "…네. 사실 아버지와 어머니가 마음에 걸려요. 하지만 여행을 떠난다고 말하면 절대 안 된다고 하실 테니까…!<end>"

## 1410191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、お父様…お母様。<end>"
EN: "F-Father... Mother.<end>"
KO: "아, 아버님… 어머님.<end>"

## 1410192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1410193

## 1410194

## 1410195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1410196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml

## 1410197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "天動地鳴をお前が抜き、<line-break>冒険の旅へと出る…。<line-break> <line-break>いつかその後ろ姿を<line-break>見送る日が来るだろうとは<line-break>思っていたよ。<end>"
EN: "You've drawn Heaven Moves, Earth Roars, and now you're setting off on an adventure... I knew the day would come when I'd watch you go.<end>"
KO: "천동지명을 네가 뽑고 모험 여행을 떠난다…. 언젠가 그 뒷모습을 배웅할 날이 올 거라 생각했지.<end>"

## 1410198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前には旅に出る理由と資格がある。<line-break>父が言うのだ、間違いない。<line-break> <line-break>行ってきなさい、ノエル。<line-break>お前が信頼している<line-break>この方と一緒に。<end>"
EN: "You have both the reason and the right to make this journey. Your father says so, and you may be sure of it. Go, Noel. Go with the person you trust.<end>"
KO: "너에겐 여행을 떠날 이유와 자격이 있다. 아버지가 하는 말이니 틀림없다. 다녀오거라, 노엘. 네가 믿는 이분과 함께.<end>"

## 1410199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml

## 1410200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、これだけは約束しなさい。<line-break>いつでもいい、必ず一度は<line-break>元気な姿でこの村へ帰ってくると。<end>"
EN: "But you must promise me one thing. No matter when it may be, you will return to this village at least once, safe and well.<end>"
KO: "하지만 이것만은 약속해. 언제라도 좋으니 반드시 한 번은 건강한 모습으로 이 마을에 돌아오겠다고.<end>"

## 1410201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は、はい！<end>"
EN: "Y-yes!<end>"
KO: "네, 네!<end>"

## 1410202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、お父様、お母様。<line-break>行ってきます！<end>"
EN: "Then, Father, Mother... I'm off!<end>"
KO: "그럼 아버님, 어머님. 다녀오겠습니다!<end>"

## 1410203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、<line-break>行きましょう！<end>"
EN: "Let's go, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨, 갑시다!<end>"

## 1410204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ…我が息子にも感謝をせねばな。<line-break>この身体とお前という愛しい存在を<line-break>与えてくれたのだからな…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... I must thank my son as well. He gave me this body―and gave me you, my beloved...<end>"
KO: "후후후… 내 아들에게도 감사해야겠군. 이 몸과 너라는 사랑스러운 존재를 내게 주었으니….<end>"

## 1410205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、いくぞ！<line-break>この竜を手始めに、全ての魔、<line-break>全ての神、すべての愚民、<line-break>我に逆らう者、全てを屠りさり、<line-break>人類の革新を成し遂げるのだ！<line-break>フフフフフ、ハハハハハハ！<end>"
EN: "Come, let us begin! This dragon shall be the first. Every demon, every god, every witless commoner, and all who defy me―I shall slaughter them all and bring about Humanity's Evolution! Heh heh heh heh... Ha ha ha ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "자, 간다! 이 용을 시작으로 모든 마물, 모든 신, 모든 어리석은 백성, 나에게 거역하는 자 전부를 도륙하고 인류의 혁신을 이루는 것이다! 후후후후후, 하하하하하하!<end>"

## 1410206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さーん！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨~!<end>"

## 1410207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかった。<line-break><value:$28>さんと<line-break>すぐに会うことができて。<line-break>あの、実は大変なことに<line-break>なってしまって。<line-break>話を聞いてもらえますか？<end>"
EN: "Thank goodness I found you so quickly, <value:$28>. Um, something terrible has happened. Would you hear me out?<end>"
KO: "다행이에요. <value:$28>님과 바로 만날 수 있어서. 저기, 사실 큰일이 생겼어요. 이야기 좀 들어 주실래요?<end>"

## 1410208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　ノエル、あたしとレイヴンは　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　このあたりで、あなたと<line-break>　　　　別行動を取ることにしたわ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Noel, Raven and I have decided it's time for us to part ways with you here.<end>"
KO: "노엘, 나와 레이븐은 이쯤에서 너와 따로 움직이기로 했어.<end>"

## 1410209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　いつまでも、あなたと一緒に　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　いては、お互いのために<line-break>　　　　　　よくないと思うの。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I don't think staying together forever would be good for any of us.<end>"
KO: "언제까지나 당신과 함께 있는 건 서로에게 좋지 않다고 생각해.<end>"

## 1410210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　ナーシェスもいなくなって、　　　　　<line-break>　　　　パーティーを解散するのに<line-break>　　　今がちょうどいい頃合いでしょ？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Now that Narscess is gone too, this seems like the right time to disband the party, don't you think?<end>"
KO: "나셰스도 없어졌고, 파티를 해산하기엔 지금이 딱 좋은 때잖아?<end>"

## 1410211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　そうそう、偶然だけど、　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　同じ宿に<value:$28>が<line-break>　　　　　　泊まっているみたいよ。<line-break>　　　　一度、話してみたらどうかしら。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and as luck would have it, <value:$28> seems to be staying at the same inn. Why don't you go and have a word?<end>"
KO: "아, 참. 우연이지만 같은 여관에 <value:$28>가 묵고 있는 것 같아. 한번 이야기해 보는 게 어때?<end>"

## 1410212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　もしかしたら、一緒に冒険の旅に<line-break>　　連れていってくれるかもしれないわね。<line-break>　　　　　　それじゃ、またね。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　カフィンより　<end>"
EN: "You might even get to go adventuring together. Well, see you around. Coffin<end>"
KO: "어쩌면 함께 모험 여행에 데려가 줄지도 모르겠네. 그럼 또 봐. 카핀으로부터<end>"

## 1410213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの、わたし、どうして<line-break>こんな事になってしまったのか　　<line-break>全然わからなくって…。<line-break>最初にいなくなっちゃったのは<line-break>ナーシェスなんです。わたしたちの<line-break>役目は終わったとか言って…。<end>"
EN: "I... I don't understand how any of this happened. Narscess was the first to leave. He said something about our work being done...<end>"
KO: "저, 저는 왜 이런 일이 됐는지 전혀 모르겠어요…. 처음 사라진 건 나셰스예요. 우리 역할은 끝났다면서….<end>"

## 1410214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それで、今朝起きてみたら<line-break>今度はカフィンもレイヴンも<line-break>いなかったんです。<end>"
EN: "Then I woke up this morning, and Coffin and Raven were gone too.<end>"
KO: "그래서 오늘 아침 일어나 보니 이번엔 카핀도 레이븐도 없었어요.<end>"

## 1410215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしてなんだろう…。<line-break>やっぱり、わたしがドジだから<line-break>みんな嫌いになっちゃったのかな…。<line-break>わたし、これから<line-break>どうしたらいいんだろう…。<end>"
EN: "I wonder why... Maybe they all hate me because I'm so clumsy. What am I going to do now...?<end>"
KO: "왜 그런 걸까…. 역시 내가 덤벙대서 다들 날 싫어하게 된 걸까…. 나, 이제 어떻게 해야 하지….<end>"

## 1410216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　わたしを<line-break>連れていってくれるんですか？<end>"
EN: "What? You'll take me with you?<end>"
KO: "네? 저를 데려가 주시는 건가요?<end>"

## 1410217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…で、でも、いいんですか？<line-break>わたし、<value:$28>さんに<line-break>迷惑をかけちゃうかも…。<end>"
EN: "B-but are you sure? I might only be a burden to you, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "…그, 그래도 괜찮아요? 제가 <value:$28>님께 폐를 끼칠지도….<end>"

## 1410218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え、いえ、その、嫌じゃないですよ！<line-break>すっごく、嬉しいんです。<line-break>嬉しいんですけど…<line-break>本当に、わたしが一緒で<line-break>いいんですか？<end>"
EN: "Oh, no, I don't mean I don't want to! I'm so happy. I really am, but... are you sure you want me to come?<end>"
KO: "아, 아니, 싫은 건 아니에요! 정말 기뻐요. 기쁘긴 한데… 정말 제가 함께해도 괜찮나요?<end>"

## 1410219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、それでは、<line-break>お言葉に甘えさせて<line-break>もらっちゃおうかな…。　　　　　<line-break>わたしがもっとしっかりするように<line-break>なれば、カフィンもレイヴンも<line-break>また会ってくれるかもしれないし。<end>"
EN: "W-well then, maybe I will take you up on your offer... And if I can learn to be more dependable, maybe Coffin and Raven will want to see me again.<end>"
KO: "그, 그럼 말씀에 기대 볼까…. 내가 좀 더 제대로 하게 되면 카핀도 레이븐도 다시 만나 줄지도 모르니까.<end>"

## 1410220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、<line-break>わたし、一生懸命がんばりますから<line-break>これから、よろしくお願いします！<end>"
EN: "I'll do my very best, <value:$28>. I hope we'll get along!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 저 열심히 할 테니 앞으로 잘 부탁드려요!<end>"

## 1410221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんだか、夢みたいです。<line-break>憧れの<value:$28>さんと一緒に<line-break>冒険の旅ができるなんて…。<end>"
EN: "It almost feels like a dream. I can't believe I get to go adventuring with someone I've always admired, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "…왠지 꿈만 같아요. 동경하던 <value:$28>님과 함께 모험할 수 있다니….<end>"

## 1410222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、行きましょうか、<line-break><value:$28>さん！<end>"
EN: "Well then, shall we be on our way, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "그럼 가 볼까요, <value:$28>님!<end>"

## 1410223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行ってしまったな。<end>"
EN: "She's gone.<end>"
KO: "가버렸군.<end>"

## 1410224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何よ、未練でもあるの？　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ノエルと<value:$28>を<line-break>くっつけるっていう、あたしの<line-break>計画に賛成だったんじゃないの？<end>"
EN: "What, having second thoughts? I thought you agreed with my plan to get Noel and <value:$28> together.<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 미련이라도 있어? 노엘과 <value:$28>을 이어 주겠다는 내 계획에 찬성했던 거 아니야?<end>"

## 1410225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "反対ではない。<line-break>それが、ノエルのためだと　<line-break>思うからな。<line-break>お前の方こそ、<line-break>いつまでもノエルと一緒に<line-break>いたかったのではないのか？<end>"
EN: "I was not opposed. I believe it is best for Noel. But were you not the one who wanted to stay with her forever?<end>"
KO: "반대하는 건 아니다. 그게 노엘을 위한 일이라 생각하니까. 오히려 너야말로 언제까지나 노엘과 함께 있고 싶었던 것 아닌가?<end>"

## 1410226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、あの子のそばにいると<line-break>目が離せないのは確かだけどね。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、あたしだってまだ若いんだから、<line-break>いつまでも子守ばかりしてられないわ。<line-break>ここらが潮時なのよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, it's true that you can't take your eyes off that girl for a moment. But I'm still young too. I can't spend the rest of my life looking after her. It was time.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그 아이 곁에 있으면 눈을 뗄 수 없는 건 사실이지만. 그래도 나도 아직 젊은데 언제까지나 아이만 돌볼 순 없잖아. 이쯤이 그만둘 때야.<end>"

## 1410227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どう？　ひとり者どうし、<line-break>しばらく、ふたりで旅を続けてみる？<end>"
EN: "So, what do you say? We're both unattached. Want to travel together for a while?<end>"
KO: "어때? 둘 다 혼자인데, 당분간 둘이서 여행을 계속해 볼래?<end>"

## 1410228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…遠慮する。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>俺にも、俺の人生を<line-break>自由に生きる権利がある。<end>"
EN: "No, thank you. I, too, have the right to live my own life as I please.<end>"
KO: "…사양하지. 나도 내 인생을 자유롭게 살 권리가 있어.<end>"

## 1410229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なによ！　失礼ね！<line-break>あとで、後悔しても知らないわよ！<end>"
EN: "What! How rude! Don't come crying to me when you regret it!<end>"
KO: "뭐야! 실례네! 나중에 후회해도 난 몰라!<end>"

## 1410230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ちなさい！<line-break>ちょっとは振り返ったりしなさいよ！<end>"
EN: "Wait! You could at least look back at me!<end>"
KO: "기다려! 조금은 뒤돌아보기도 해!<end>"

## 1410231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか…。<line-break>あれだけの大事件が<line-break>起こったのですものね…。<end>"
EN: "I see... After such a momentous event, I suppose that is only natural.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요… 그만한 큰 사건이 일어났으니까요…<end>"

## 1410232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、<value:$28>じゃないか。<line-break>久々だな。いつものお茶でいいかい？<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't <value:$28>. It's been a while. The usual tea?<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28> 아닌가. 오랜만이군. 늘 마시던 차면 되겠나?<end>"

## 1410233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ…いよいよ、<line-break>次の旅に出かけるのか。<end>"
EN: "Ah... so the time has finally come for your next journey.<end>"
KO: "아아… 드디어 다음 여행을 떠나는 건가.<end>"

## 1410234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、寂しくなるな。<line-break>俺はいいが、きっとあの娘は…。<end>"
EN: "I see. It'll be lonely without you. I'll be fine, but I'm sure she...<end>"
KO: "그런가, 쓸쓸해지겠군. 난 괜찮지만 분명 그 아이는….<end>"

## 1410235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほとぼりが冷めてみりゃ、<line-break>ふびんでな。<line-break> <line-break>蝶よ花よと育てられたお嬢様に、<line-break>解放軍の首領なんて務まるもんか…。<line-break>そうは思わないか？<end>"
EN: "Once things settled down, I couldn't help feeling sorry for her. How could a sheltered young lady, raised like a precious flower, ever lead the Liberation Army? Don't you think?<end>"
KO: "시간이 지나고 보니 안쓰럽더군. 귀하게 자란 아가씨가 해방군의 수장을 맡을 수 있겠나… 자네도 그렇게 생각하지 않나?<end>"

## 1410236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの娘…クリュセイスの世界は、<line-break>まるきり変わっちまったんだろうな。<line-break>これから、いったい…。<end>"
EN: "That girl's... Khryuseis's whole world must have changed completely. And now... what?<end>"
KO: "그 아이… 크류세이스의 세계는 완전히 바뀌어 버렸겠지. 앞으로 대체….<end>"

## 1410237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと見つけたわ、<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "I finally found you, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "드디어 찾았어, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1410238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたくしから逃げて、<line-break>ヤケ酒でもしているかと思えば…<line-break>またお茶？<end>"
EN: "I thought you'd fled from me to drown your sorrows... but tea again?<end>"
KO: "나를 피해 자포자기해서 술이라도 마시는 줄 알았더니… 또 차야?<end>"

## 1410239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、私に断りもなく勝手に<line-break>宿舎からいなくなったのはなぜ？<end>"
EN: "Then why did you leave the quarters without so much as a word to me?<end>"
KO: "그럼 내 허락도 없이 마음대로 숙소에서 사라진 건 왜지?<end>"

## 1410240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それに…その旅装束は何よ？<end>"
EN: "And... what are you doing dressed for a journey?<end>"
KO: "게다가… 그 여행 복장은 뭐야?<end>"

## 1410241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>わたくしにだって、<line-break>それくらい分かります。<line-break>また旅に出るつもりなのでしょう？<line-break>新しい土地、新しい世界へ。<line-break>わたくしを置き去りにして、ね。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, even I can tell that much. You're planning to set out again, aren't you? Off to new lands, a new world, leaving me behind.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 저도 그 정도는 알아요. 또 여행을 떠날 생각이죠? 새로운 땅, 새로운 세계로. 저를 두고서 말이에요.<end>"

## 1410242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたくしも行きます。<end>"
EN: "I'm coming with you.<end>"
KO: "저도 가겠습니다.<end>"

## 1410243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう言われるだろうと思ってたわ。<line-break>でもね、思い出して、<line-break><value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "I knew you would say that. But please, remember, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "그렇게 말할 줄 알았어. 하지만 기억해 봐, <value:$28>….<end>"

## 1410244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの戦乱の日々を。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>わたくしに違う世界を<line-break>見せてくれたのは、<line-break>あなたじゃなくって？<end>"
EN: "Those days of war. Weren't you the one who showed me a different world?<end>"
KO: "그 전란의 나날을…. 나에게 다른 세계를 보여 준 건 당신 아니었나요?<end>"

## 1410245
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…結局、わたくしはこの世界の一部分、<line-break>ほんのちいさな世界しか知らない。<line-break> <line-break>もっともっと世界を見て回り、<line-break>知らなければ…。そのために、<line-break>旅に出たいと思うのです。<end>"
EN: "In the end, I'm only one small part of this world, and I know only a tiny world of my own. I need to travel farther, see more of the world, and learn more about it. That's why I want to set out on a journey.<end>"
KO: "…결국 저는 이 세계의 일부, 아주 작은 세계밖에 몰라요. 더 많이 세상을 보고 다니며 알아야 해요…. 그러기 위해 여행을 떠나고 싶습니다.<end>"

## 1410246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ、もう決めました。<line-break>仕事の引継ぎも終わってるわ。<end>"
EN: "No. I've made up my mind. I've already handed over all my duties.<end>"
KO: "아니요, 이미 결정했어요. 업무 인계도 끝났고요.<end>"

## 1410247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…一緒に…来てくれない？<end>"
EN: "Will you... come with me?<end>"
KO: "…같이… 와 주지 않을래?<end>"

## 1410248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう<value:$28>…<line-break>とてもうれしいわ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>... I'm so happy.<end>"
KO: "고마워, <value:$28>… 정말 기뻐.<end>"

## 1410249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実はもうわたくしの<line-break>旅支度も済んでるの。<line-break>外に置いてあるわ。<end>"
EN: "The truth is, I've already packed for the journey. My things are waiting outside.<end>"
KO: "사실 제 여행 준비도 벌써 끝났어요. 밖에 두었답니다.<end>"

## 1410250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、行きましょ、<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Come, let's go, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "자, 가자, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1410251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "封士の心得と一緒に、<line-break>何度も語り聞かせたろうが、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>あきれた奴じゃ、もう忘れたか？<end>"
EN: "I've told you about it time and again, right alongside the Sealer's code, <value:$28>. Honestly, have you forgotten already?<end>"
KO: "봉사의 마음가짐과 함께, 몇 번이나 말해 줬잖느냐, <value:$28>. 한심한 녀석, 벌써 잊었나?<end>"

## 1410252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…質問がなければ、<line-break>これにて閣議は終了とする。<end>"
EN: "If there are no questions, this cabinet meeting is adjourned.<end>"
KO: "…질문이 없다면 이것으로 각의를 마치겠습니다.<end>"

## 1410253
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご苦労であった。さがってよい。<end>"
EN: "You have done well. You may go.<end>"
KO: "수고했다. 물러가도 좋다.<end>"

## 1410254
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ザキヴがディンガルを支配する。<end>"
EN: "Zagiv rules Dyneskal.<end>"
KO: "…자기브가 딘갈을 지배한다.<end>"

## 1410255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気がつけば、<line-break>ゾフォルの予言通りというわけね…。<end>"
EN: "And before I knew it, Zofor's prophecy had come true.<end>"
KO: "정신 차리고 보니, 조포르의 예언대로 된 셈이네….<end>"

## 1410256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は予言などには負けまいと…<line-break>信じまいと、戦ってきたつもりなのに…。<end>"
EN: "I thought I had fought all this time, refusing to believe in prophecy, refusing to let it defeat me.<end>"
KO: "나는 예언 따위에는 지지 않겠다고… 믿지 않겠다고 싸워왔다고 생각했는데….<end>"

## 1410257
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気がつけばこのザマよ。<line-break>おかしいでしょう？<line-break> <line-break>私はやっぱり呪われた予言から<line-break>逃げられなかった…そう思うわよね、<line-break><value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "And look where I am now. It's almost funny, isn't it? I couldn't escape that cursed prophecy after all. That's what you think too, don't you, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "정신을 차려 보니 이 꼴이야. 우습지? 난 역시 저주받은 예언에서 벗어나지 못했어… 그렇게 생각하지, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1410258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "２人きりの時に、そんなこと<line-break>しなくていいって言ったでしょう！？<end>"
EN: "I told you, you don't have to do that when we're alone!<end>"
KO: "둘만 있을 때는 그런 거 하지 않아도 된다고 했잖아!?<end>"

## 1410259
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ…ごめんなさい…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "앗… 미안해요….<end>"

## 1410260
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、どうしても人に対して<line-break>高圧的な物言いになってしまうの。<line-break>いけないとは思っているのだけれど…。<end>"
EN: "Lately, I can't seem to stop taking such an overbearing tone with people. I know I shouldn't...<end>"
KO: "요즘은 자꾸 사람에게 고압적으로 말하게 돼. 그러면 안 된다고 생각은 하는데….<end>"

## 1410261
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<line-break>こんなことを打ち明けられるのは、<line-break><value:$28>だけなの。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. You're the only one I can confide in like this, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "고마워. 이런 이야기를 털어놓을 수 있는 건 <value:$28>뿐이야.<end>"

## 1410262
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…毎日、眠りに落ちるとき<line-break>今でもすごく怖くなる。<end>"
EN: "Even now, I get terribly frightened every night as I'm falling asleep.<end>"
KO: "…매일 잠들 때면 지금도 너무 무서워져.<end>"

## 1410263
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、あの闇に…<line-break>すべてを壊してしまうあの闇に、<line-break>落ちてしまうんじゃないかって…。<line-break>でも、そんな時…<end>"
EN: "I'm afraid I'll fall into that darkness again... the darkness that destroys everything. But at times like that...<end>"
KO: "또 그 어둠에… 모든 것을 부숴 버리는 그 어둠에 빠져 버릴까 봐…. 하지만 그럴 때….<end>"

## 1410264
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "必ず絶望の闇の中から、<line-break>それを打ち砕いて<line-break>私を助け出してくれる騎士がいるの。<end>"
EN: "There is always a knight who shatters that darkness of despair and rescues me from it.<end>"
KO: "반드시 절망의 어둠 속에서 그것을 깨부수고 나를 구해 줄 기사가 있어.<end>"

## 1410265
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "女だてらに、<line-break>勇ましい甲冑をまとってね。<line-break> <line-break>私は、その騎士のおかげで<line-break>安心して眠りにつくことができる…。<end>"
EN: "A woman in gallant armor, no less. Thanks to that knight, I can fall asleep in peace.<end>"
KO: "여자인데도 용맹한 갑옷을 두르고 있었지. 나는 그 기사 덕분에 안심하고 잠들 수 있었어….<end>"

## 1410266
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今わかった。ゾフォルの予言も、<line-break>決して完全なものではなかったわ。<end>"
EN: "Now I understand. Even Zofor's prophecy was never infallible.<end>"
KO: "지금 알았어. 조포르의 예언도 결코 완전한 건 아니었어.<end>"

## 1410267
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルは、あなたのことを…<line-break>私にとって、こんなに大きな存在を<line-break>見ることができなかったのですもの。<end>"
EN: "Zofor failed to foresee you―someone who would come to mean so much to me.<end>"
KO: "조포르는 당신을… 제게 이렇게 큰 존재가 된 당신을 보지 못했던 거예요.<end>"

## 1410268
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう私は、予言などには惑わされない。<line-break>自分の未来を、自分で作る。<line-break>それでいいのよね？<end>"
EN: "I will no longer let prophecy lead me astray. I will shape my own future. That's all right, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "이제 나는 예언 따위에 휘둘리지 않아. 내 미래는 내가 만든다. 그걸로 되는 거지?<end>"

## 1410269
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…あなたがいれば、<line-break>私はもっと強くなれる気がする。<line-break>ずっと、そばにいてくれますね？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... with you beside me, I feel I can become stronger. You will stay with me, won't you?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>… 당신이 있으면 저는 더 강해질 수 있을 것 같아요. 계속 곁에 있어 주실 거죠?<end>"

## 1410270
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "二人の時は<line-break>それはなしだって言ったでしょう？<line-break>わざとなの？<end>"
EN: "I told you, none of that when we're alone. Are you doing it on purpose?<end>"
KO: "둘만 있을 땐 그건 하지 말자고 했잖아? 일부러 그래?<end>"

## 1410271
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、<value:$28>ったら…。<line-break>それではこれから、<line-break>帝都の巡察に出かけます。<line-break>道中の護衛、しっかり頼みますよ、<line-break>近衛将軍<value:$28>殿。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>... Well then, I shall now set out to inspect the imperial capital. I trust you to guard me well along the way, Commander of the Imperial Guard <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "후후, <value:$28>도 참…. 그럼 이제 제도 순찰을 나가겠습니다. 가는 길 호위, 잘 부탁합니다. 근위장군 <value:$28> 공.<end>"

## 1410272
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml

JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
