## 1410276
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…以上、指示したとおり<line-break>ロセン及びリベルダムへの援助は、<line-break>それぞれの要望どおりに実施します。<line-break>よしなに…。<end>"
EN: "That is all. As I instructed, Rocen and Liberdam will each receive the aid they requested. See to it.<end>"
KO: "…이상, 지시한 대로 로센과 리벨덤에 대한 지원은 각각의 요청대로 실시합니다. 잘 부탁합니다….<end>"

## 1410277
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かしこまりました。女王様。<end>"
EN: "As you command, Your Majesty.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 여왕님.<end>"

## 1410278
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ様。お待ちを！<line-break>ロストールの財政は火の車です。<line-break> <line-break>今は国内の復興を<line-break>優先的に行うべきかと…。<end>"
EN: "Lady Tiana, please wait! Rostorl's finances are already strained to the breaking point. Surely we should give priority to rebuilding our own country for now.<end>"
KO: "티아나 님, 기다려 주십시오! 로스톨 재정은 매우 어렵습니다. 지금은 국내 복구를 우선해야 할 것으로….<end>"

## 1410279
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "財務長官殿！<end>"
EN: "Minister of Finance!<end>"
KO: "재무장관님!<end>"

## 1410280
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "良いのです、<line-break>財務長官の心配はもっともなこと。<end>"
EN: "It is all right. The finance minister's concerns are entirely reasonable.<end>"
KO: "괜찮습니다. 재무장관의 걱정은 지당합니다.<end>"

## 1410281
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "度重なる戦乱、内乱…<line-break>このロストールも荒れてしまいました。<line-break>国の財政が逼迫している事も事実です。<end>"
EN: "Repeated wars and civil strife have left Rostorl in ruins as well. It is true that the kingdom's finances are under severe strain.<end>"
KO: "거듭된 전란과 내란으로 이 로스톨도 황폐해졌습니다. 국가 재정이 궁핍해진 것도 사실입니다.<end>"

## 1410282
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも…だからこそ<line-break>国内の利益だけを見ていては<line-break>ならないのです。<line-break>ロセンもリベルダムも、<line-break>この援助によって<line-break>いずれ復興するでしょう。<end>"
EN: "But that is precisely why we must look beyond our own interests. With this aid, Rocen and Liberdam will both recover in time.<end>"
KO: "하지만… 그렇기 때문에 국내의 이익만 보고 있어선 안 됩니다. 로센도 리벨덤도 이 원조로 언젠가는 부흥하겠지요.<end>"

## 1410283
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうなった折、<line-break>出来上がる協調的な国際世界において、<line-break>このロストールが<line-break>強い発言権を持つための、<line-break>いわばこれは布石なのです。<end>"
EN: "When they do, a cooperative international order will emerge. This aid is, so to speak, laying the groundwork for Rostorl to have a strong voice within it.<end>"
KO: "그때 형성될 협력적인 국제사회에서 이 로스톨이 강한 발언권을 갖기 위한, 말하자면 포석입니다.<end>"

## 1410284
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし！<line-break>いや…分かりました。<line-break> <line-break>そこまでお考えなら<line-break>申し上げることはございません。<end>ティアナ様のお心に沿うよう努力いたします。<end>"
EN: "But... No, I understand. If you have considered the matter that far, I have nothing more to say.<end>I will do all I can to carry out your wishes, Lady Tiana.<end>"
KO: "하지만! 아니… 알겠습니다. 그렇게까지 생각하셨다면 더 드릴 말씀은 없습니다.<end>티아나 님의 뜻에 따르도록 노력하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1410285
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ様のお心に沿うよう<line-break>努力いたします。<end>"
EN: "I will do all I can to carry out your wishes, Lady Tiana.<end>"
KO: "티아나 님의 뜻에 따를 수 있도록 노력하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1410286
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ様…。<end>"
EN: "Lady Tiana...<end>"
KO: "티아나 님….<end>"

## 1410287
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "分かっています。<line-break>監査の方よろしくお願いします。<end>"
EN: "I know. Please see to the audit.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 감사 잘 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 1410288
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。<end>"
EN: "Yes.<end>"
KO: "네.<end>"

## 1410289
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふう、…雌狐の子は雌狐。<end>"
EN: "Well... a vixen's daughter is a vixen.<end>"
KO: "후우, …암여우의 자식은 암여우지.<end>"

## 1410291
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "分かっています。<line-break>この道を選んだ時から<line-break>そう言われるのは覚悟しています。<line-break>どんなことを言われてもやり遂げるしか<line-break>ないのですから…。<end>"
EN: "I know. From the moment I chose this path, I knew people would call me that. Whatever anyone says, I have no choice but to see it through.<end>"
KO: "알고 있습니다. 이 길을 선택했을 때부터 그런 말을 들을 각오는 했어요. 무슨 말을 듣더라도 끝까지 해낼 수밖에 없으니까요….<end>"

## 1410292
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いろいろな事があって<line-break>…たくさんの方がわたしの周りから<line-break>姿を消してしまった…。<line-break>私ひとりで、<line-break>この荒れた世界を建て直すことなんて…。<end>"
EN: "So much has happened, and so many people have vanished from my life. How could I ever rebuild this ravaged world alone?<end>"
KO: "…여러 일이 있었고… 많은 분이 제 곁에서 사라져 버렸어요…. 저 혼자 이 황폐한 세상을 다시 세울 수 있을까요….<end>"

## 1410293
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…せめて…そう、<line-break>せめてあの方が側に<line-break>いてくれたら…。<line-break>そしたら私、きっと…。<end>"
EN: "If only... Yes, if only she were here beside me. Then I... I'm sure I could...<end>"
KO: "…적어도… 그래, 적어도 그분이 곁에 있어 준다면…. 그러면 나, 분명….<end>"

## 1410294
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…でもあの方も、<line-break>もう長いこと姿を<line-break>見せてくださらない。<line-break>ふふ、きっと…あきれられたのね。<end>"
EN: "But she hasn't come to see me in such a long time. Heh... I suppose she's grown tired of me.<end>"
KO: "…하지만 그분도 오랫동안 모습을 보여 주지 않으셨어. 후후, 분명… 질리신 거겠지.<end>"

## 1410295
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはそうだわ。<line-break>私は…同じ女性だというのに<line-break>…あの方の強さに頼ってばかりだった！<line-break>いつでもどこでも守ってもらえると<line-break>都合のいいことばかり考えていた。<end>"
EN: "Of course she has. Even though I'm a woman too, I did nothing but depend on her strength! I kept telling myself she would always be there to protect me, wherever I went.<end>"
KO: "그 말이 맞아. 나는… 같은 여자인데도…그분의 강함에 의지하기만 했어! 언제 어디서든 지켜 주겠지 하고내 편한 생각만 했어.<end>"

## 1410296
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当なら、あの方の強さの半分、<line-break>いいえ百分の一の勇気でも<line-break>身に着けるべきだったのに…。<line-break>私は、現実を見ようとはしていなかった。<end>"
EN: "I should have acquired half her strength―or no, even one hundredth of her courage. Instead, I refused to face reality.<end>"
KO: "정말이라면 그분 강함의 절반, 아니 백분의 일만큼의 용기라도 익혔어야 했는데…. 나는 현실을 보려 하지 않았어.<end>"

## 1410297
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから今、<line-break>私はその責任から<line-break>逃げることはできない。<line-break>もしも、<line-break>もう一度あの方と出会えた時に、私は…。<end>"
EN: "That is why I cannot run from my responsibility now. If I should ever meet her again, I...<end>"
KO: "그래서 지금 나는 그 책임에서 도망칠 수 없어. 만약 다시 그분을 만날 수 있다면 나는….<end>"

## 1410298
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…もしもう一度お会いできたら、<line-break>今度は…今度こそはパートナーとして<line-break>胸を張って…<line-break>一緒に歩きたいと…<line-break>思っていました…。<line-break>勇気を持って…。<end>"
EN: "If I could see her once more, then this time... this time, as her partner, I wanted to hold my head high and walk beside her. With courage of my own...<end>"
KO: "…다시 한번 만날 수 있다면, 그때는… 그때야말로 파트너로서 당당하게… 함께 걷고 싶다고… 생각했어요…. 용기를 내서….<end>"

## 1410299
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 님!<end>"

## 1410300
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1410301
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだ、シェスター？<line-break>精霊は、<line-break>呼びかけに答えてくれているか？<end>"
EN: "How is it, Shester? Are the spirits answering your call?<end>"
KO: "어때, 셰스터? 정령은 부름에 응답하고 있나?<end>"

## 1410302
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ…まだとても弱々しいけれど、<line-break>回復のきざしはあるわ。<line-break> <line-break>これ以上傷つかないように<line-break>手入れを怠らなければ、きっと…。<end>"
EN: "Yes... They're still very weak, but there are signs of recovery. If we keep tending to them and make sure they come to no further harm, I'm sure...<end>"
KO: "그래… 아직 아주 약하지만 회복의 기미는 있어. 더 다치지 않게 꾸준히 돌본다면 분명….<end>"

## 1410303
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そっちはどうだった、<value:$28>？<line-break>精霊の息吹を<line-break>感じられそうな場所は見つかったか？<end>"
EN: "How did you fare, <value:$28>? Did you find anywhere you could sense the breath of the spirits?<end>"
KO: "그쪽은 어땠어, <value:$28>? 정령의 숨결을 느낄 만한 곳은 찾았어?<end>"

## 1410304
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…。<line-break>前の戦乱で、被害を受けたのは、<line-break>人々ばかりではない。<line-break>魔法の乱発によって、幾千万の<line-break>精霊がこの世から消滅してしまった。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I see... People were not the only ones harmed by the last war. The reckless use of magic wiped tens of millions of spirits from this world.<end>"
KO: "그런가…. 지난 전란에서 피해를 입은 건 사람들뿐이 아니다. 마법의 남발로 수천만의 정령이 이 세상에서 사라졌다.<end>"

## 1410305
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊たちの数が減れば、<line-break>じき大地はうるおいを失い、<line-break>樹木は枯れてゆく…。<line-break>そうだったな、シェスター？<end>"
EN: "As the spirits dwindle, the land will soon lose its moisture and the trees will wither... Isn't that right, Shester?<end>"
KO: "정령들의 수가 줄면 머지않아 대지는 수분을 잃고 나무는 말라 죽는다…. 그랬지, 셰스터?<end>"

## 1410306
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、ロイ。<line-break>一見すこやかに見えるこの森も、<line-break>私には、以前と同じ場所とは思えない。<line-break>精霊たちが、弱りきっているのね…。<end>"
EN: "Yes, Roy. This forest may look healthy, but to me, it no longer feels like the same place. The spirits have grown so weak...<end>"
KO: "그래, 로이. 겉보기엔 건강해 보이는 이 숲도 내게는 예전과 같은 곳으로 느껴지지 않아. 정령들이 완전히 약해졌어….<end>"

## 1410308
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほどな…。<line-break>誰がつぐないをするべきかといえば、<line-break> <line-break>戦乱を戦い抜いた<line-break>私たちの他にはいないだろうな…。<end>"
EN: "I understand... If anyone should make amends, it can only be those of us who fought through the war.<end>"
KO: "그렇군…. 누가 속죄해야 하느냐고 한다면 전란을 끝까지 싸운 우리 말고는 없겠지….<end>"

## 1410309
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、<value:$28>。<line-break>つぐなうというのは、<line-break>この場合、どうすることなのだろうか？<end>"
EN: "But, <value:$28>, what can we do to make amends in this case?<end>"
KO: "하지만 <value:$28>. 속죄한다는 건 이 경우 어떻게 해야 하는 걸까?<end>"

## 1410310
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロイ…。<end>"
EN: "Roy...<end>"
KO: "로이….<end>"

## 1410311
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊の死をいたみ、<line-break>嘆き悲しみ、<line-break>謝るばかりが方法ではない。<line-break>私は、シェスターに、<line-break>そのことを教わったつもりだ。<end>"
EN: "Mourning the spirits' deaths, grieving for them, and apologizing are not the only things we can do. At least, that is what I believe Shester taught me.<end>"
KO: "정령의 죽음을 슬퍼하고 한탄하며 사과만 하는 게 방법은 아니야. 나는 셰스터에게 그걸 배웠다고 생각해.<end>"

## 1410312
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊たちを傷つけたのは、<line-break>間違いなく、私たちのこの手だ。<line-break> <line-break>しかしまた、この手は、<line-break>精霊たちを癒す手伝いもできる…。<end>"
EN: "There is no doubt that it was by our own hands that the spirits were harmed. But these same hands can also help them heal...<end>"
KO: "정령들을 상처 입힌 건 틀림없이 우리 손이다. 하지만 이 손으로 정령들의 상처를 치유하는 일도 도울 수 있어….<end>"

## 1410313
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうすることが、彼らに対して、<line-break>何よりのつぐないに<line-break>なるのではないだろうか？<end>"
EN: "Wouldn't that be the best way to make amends to them?<end>"
KO: "그렇게 하는 것이 그들에게 무엇보다 큰 속죄가 되지 않을까?<end>"

## 1410314
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう、<value:$28>。<line-break>あなたが私たちの考えに<line-break>賛同してくれて、とても頼もしい。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. It's very heartening to know that you support what we're trying to do.<end>"
KO: "고마워, <value:$28>. 네가 우리 생각에 동의해 줘서 정말 든든해.<end>"

## 1410315
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、そうだな。<line-break>私たちには希望がある。<line-break> <line-break>そう信じられることは、<line-break>うれしいことだな…。<end>"
EN: "Yes... that's right. We have hope. I'm glad we can believe that...<end>"
KO: "그래, 맞아. 우리에겐 희망이 있어. 그렇게 믿을 수 있다는 건 기쁜 일이군….<end>"

## 1410316
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "続けましょう、ロイ、<value:$28>。<line-break>世界のために、<line-break>私たちの未来のために、ね。<end>"
EN: "Let's keep going, Roy, <value:$28>. For the world, and for our future.<end>"
KO: "계속하자, 로이, <value:$28>. 세상을 위해, 우리의 미래를 위해.<end>"

## 1410317
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ！<end>"
EN: "Yes!<end>"
KO: "아아!<end>"

## 1410321
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いよいよ、<line-break>行ってしまうんですね、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "So the time has finally come for you to leave, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…드디어 떠나시는군요, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1410322
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さびしくなりますね。<line-break>今度の旅の目的は…。<end>"
EN: "It will be lonely without you. And the purpose of this journey...<end>"
KO: "쓸쓸해지겠네요. 이번 여행의 목적은….<end>"

## 1410323
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら！　早くしなって、ヴァイ！<line-break>もうお兄ちゃん出発しちゃってる<line-break>かもしれないよ！<end>"
EN: "Come on, Vai! Hurry up! Big brother might have left already!<end>"
KO: "자! 빨리 하라니까, 바이! 벌써 오빠가 출발했을지도 몰라!<end>"

## 1410324
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫よ。<line-break>大体の時間は調べがついてるんだから。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry. I've found out roughly when he's due to leave.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아. 대략적인 시간은 이미 알아봤으니까.<end>"

## 1410325
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だって、もしも気が変わって<line-break>早く出発してたらどうするのよ！<line-break>　<line-break>お兄ちゃん、ああ見えても<line-break>もの覚え悪いんだから。<end>"
EN: "But what if he changed his mind and left early? Big brother may not look it, but he's terribly forgetful.<end>"
KO: "마음이 바뀌어서 일찍 출발했으면 어쩌려고! 오빠는 보기와 달리 기억력이 나쁘단 말이야.<end>"

## 1410326
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なに言ってるの。ヴィアがあわてて<line-break>旅の装備をそっくり忘れたのが<line-break>いけないんでしょう？<line-break>それとね、ヴィア、<line-break>お兄ちゃんじゃなくて<line-break>ちゃんと<value:$28>様とお呼びな…<end>"
EN: "What are you talking about? It's your own fault for getting flustered and forgetting all the traveling gear, isn't it? And another thing, Via: call him <value:$28>, not “big brother”...<end>"
KO: "무슨 소리야. 비아가 허둥대다가 여행 장비를 몽땅 두고 온 게 잘못이잖아? 그리고 비아, 오빠가 아니라 제대로 <value:$28> 님이라고 불러야…<end>"

## 1410327
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml

## 1410328
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お兄ちゃん！<end>"
EN: "Big brother!<end>"
KO: "오빠!<end>"

## 1410329
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの…これはその、なんと申しますか…。<end>"
EN: "Ah... well, this is... how shall I put it...<end>"
KO: "저… 이건 그, 뭐라고 해야 할지….<end>"

## 1410330
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違うの、お兄ちゃん…じゃなくって<line-break><value:$28>様…。<end>"
EN: "No, big brother... I mean, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "아니야, 오빠… 아니, <value:$28>님….<end>"

## 1410331
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご安心ください、<value:$28>様、<line-break>陛下のお許しは<line-break>いただいております。<end>"
EN: "Please rest assured, <value:$28>. We have the monarch's permission.<end>"
KO: "안심하세요, <value:$28> 님. 폐하의 허락은 받았습니다.<end>"

## 1410332
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お兄ちゃんが…じゃない、<line-break><value:$28>様について<line-break>戦乱で荒廃した各地を見て回り、<line-break>報告するのが私たちの仕事なの。<line-break>で、ついでに…<end>"
EN: "Our job is to accompany big brother... I mean, <value:$28>, through the lands ravaged by war, see how things stand, and report on what we find. And while we're at it...<end>"
KO: "오빠가… 아니, <value:$28>님을 따라 전란으로 황폐해진 각지를 돌아보고 보고하는 게 우리 일이야. 그리고 겸사겸사….<end>"

## 1410333
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どこか埋まらない心のすきまを、<line-break>その欠片のようなものを<line-break>見つけられたらいいと…。<end>"
EN: "We hope we might find something to fill that hollow place inside us―something like the missing piece.<end>"
KO: "어딘가 채워지지 않는 마음의 틈, 그 조각 같은 것을 찾을 수 있으면 좋겠다고….<end>"

## 1410334
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。<line-break>厳しい旅になるだろう事は<line-break>承知しております。<line-break>それでも私は<value:$28>様<line-break>について行きたいのです。<end>"
EN: "Yes. I understand that the journey will be a difficult one. Even so, I wish to go with you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "네. 힘든 여행이 될 거라는 건 알고 있습니다. 그래도 저는 <value:$28>님을 따라가고 싶습니다.<end>"

## 1410335
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様とならば、<line-break>見つかる気がするのです。<line-break>忘れてきた心の破片が…。<end>"
EN: "With you, <value:$28>, I feel I may find it... the piece of my heart I left behind.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님과 함께라면 찾을 수 있을 것 같아요. 잃어버린 마음의 조각을….<end>"

## 1410336
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、またヴァイだけ<line-break>お兄ちゃんに媚売ってる！<end>"
EN: "Hey! Vai's sucking up to big brother again!<end>"
KO: "앗, 또 바이만 오빠한테 아부해!<end>"

## 1410337
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "媚って…。いい、ヴィア、あのね、<line-break>私たちは遊びに行くわけじゃないの。<end>"
EN: "Sucking up...? Listen, Via. We aren't going on this journey for fun.<end>"
KO: "아첨이라니…. 잘 들어, 비아. 우리는 놀러 가는 게 아니야.<end>"

## 1410338
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなのわかってるわよ！<line-break>でも、だいたいヴァイは…。<end>"
EN: "I know that! But you always...<end>"
KO: "그런 건 알고 있어! 하지만 애초에 바이는….<end>"

## 1410339
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "退屈しない旅になりそうですねえ、<line-break><value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "It seems you won't be bored on this journey, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "심심하지 않은 여행이 되겠네요, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1410340
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "忘れるは、人の常。<line-break>忘れられぬは、人の業…そう言います。<line-break>　<line-break>見つかるかどうか、わかりませんよ？<line-break>それでも行くんですね？<end>"
EN: "To forget is human nature. To be unable to forget is the burden of being human... or so they say. There is no telling whether you will find what you seek. Will you still go?<end>"
KO: "잊는 것은 사람의 상사, 잊지 못하는 것은 사람의 업이라고 합니다. 찾을 수 있을지 모릅니다. 그래도 가시겠습니까?<end>"

## 1410341
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今さら、わかりきったことでしたね。<end>"
EN: "But I already knew your answer, didn't I?<end>"
KO: "이제 와선 너무 당연한 일이었네요.<end>"

## 1410342
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、ではお行きなさい。<line-break>忘却のかなたへ。<end>"
EN: "Go, then. Beyond the reaches of oblivion.<end>"
KO: "자, 그럼 가세요. 망각의 저편으로.<end>"

## 1410343
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お嬢さん方、<line-break><value:$28>はもう出発しましたよ？<end>"
EN: "Young ladies, <value:$28> has already left, you know?<end>"
KO: "아가씨들, <value:$28>은 벌써 출발했습니다만?<end>"

## 1410348

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "오라버니!<end>"

## 1410350
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、いってらっしゃい…。<line-break>若き真実の狩人たちよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... Safe travels, young hunters of truth.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 다녀오세요…. 젊은 진실의 사냥꾼들이여.<end>"

## 1410351
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、来てくれたんだ！<line-break>待ってたよ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, you came! I've been waiting for you!<end>"
KO: "아, 와 줬구나! 기다렸어!<end>"

## 1410352
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァイ？　えっとね、いろいろあって、<line-break>ちょっと向こう行ってもらってるの。<line-break>　<line-break>どうしても、私から<value:$28>に<line-break>言っときたいことがあってね。<end>"
EN: "Vai? Well, a lot happened, so I had Vai step away for a bit. There's something I just had to tell you myself, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "바이? 음, 여러 일이 있어서 잠깐 저쪽에 가 있게 했어. 꼭 내가 직접 <value:$28>에게 말해 두고 싶은 게 있어서.<end>"

## 1410353
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、<value:$28>。<line-break><value:$28>の中じゃ、<line-break>もう、全部終わっちゃった？<end>"
EN: "Tell me, <value:$28>. Deep down, do you feel like everything is already over?<end>"
KO: "저기, <value:$28>. <value:$28>한테는 이제 다 끝난 거야?<end>"

## 1410354
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんごめん、えっと…その…<line-break>戦争は、もうおしまいに<line-break>なったよね？<end>"
EN: "Sorry, sorry. Um... What I mean is... the war's over now, right?<end>"
KO: "미안, 미안. 그러니까… 그… 전쟁은 이제 끝났지?<end>"

## 1410355
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだね。私もヴァイも、<line-break>もうあんな風に戦うことは<line-break>ないって思ってるんだ。<line-break>でもね…。<end>"
EN: "Right. Vai and I both think we won't ever fight like that again. But...<end>"
KO: "그래. 나도 바이도 이제 그런 식으로 싸울 일은 없을 거라 생각해. 하지만….<end>"

## 1410356
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私ね、<line-break>戦いすぎちゃったみたいなの。<line-break>　<line-break>戦いすぎて、大事なものを、<line-break>どっかに落として<line-break>きちゃったみたいなの…。<end>"
EN: "I think... maybe I fought too much. I fought so much that I must have lost something important along the way...<end>"
KO: "…나, 너무 많이 싸운 것 같아. 너무 싸워서 소중한 걸 어딘가에 떨어뜨리고 온 것 같아….<end>"

## 1410357
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何だかおかしいの！<line-break>ヴァイと調べれば調べるほど、<line-break>わからないことが増えていくの！<line-break>ねえ、信じられる？<line-break>起きるはずのないことが起きて、<line-break>知らない間に勝手に消えていって…。<end>"
EN: "Something's wrong! The more Vai and I look into it, the more questions we find! Can you believe it? Things that shouldn't happen are happening, then disappearing on their own before anyone even notices...<end>"
KO: "뭔가 이상해! 바이와 조사하면 할수록 모르는 게 더 늘어나! 있잖아, 믿을 수 있어? 일어날 리 없는 일이 일어나고, 모르는 사이에 멋대로 사라져 버리고….<end>"

## 1410358
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わからない…<line-break>おかしくなりそうなの！<line-break>　<line-break>私、どうしても、それを探しに<line-break>行かなくちゃならない…！<end>"
EN: "I don't understand... I feel like I'm losing my mind! But no matter what, I have to go and find it!<end>"
KO: "모르겠어… 미쳐 버릴 것 같아! 나, 무슨 일이 있어도 그걸 찾으러 가야 해…!<end>"

## 1410359
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう一度戦いの中へ<line-break>戻ることになっても、<line-break>それを取り戻すためなら…！<end>"
EN: "Even if it means going back into battle, I'll do it to get that back...!<end>"
KO: "다시 싸움 속으로 돌아가게 되더라도, 그것을 되찾을 수 있다면…!<end>"

## 1410360
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心のカケラ…あの時なくした、<line-break>大切な、私の、何か…。<end>"
EN: "A piece of my heart... something precious, something of mine that I lost back then...<end>"
KO: "마음의 조각… 그때 잃어버린, 소중한 내 무언가….<end>"

## 1410362
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、<value:$28>、お願い！<line-break>手伝って！　<value:$28>の力で、<line-break>私たちを助けて！　お願い！<end>"
EN: "Please, <value:$28>! Help me! Use your strength to help us! Please!<end>"
KO: "있잖아, <value:$28>, 부탁이야! 도와줘! <value:$28>의 힘으로 우리를 도와줘! 부탁해!<end>"

## 1410363
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ありがとう…うれしいよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>あなたとなら、<line-break>きっと見つけられる。<line-break>私の心の欠片が…。<end>"
EN: "Ah, thank you... I'm so glad, <value:$28>. With you, I'm sure we can find it. The piece of my heart...<end>"
KO: "고, 고마워… 기뻐, <value:$28>. 당신과 함께라면 분명 찾을 수 있을 거야. 내 마음의 조각을….<end>"

## 1410364
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そういうことだったのね、ヴィア。<end>"
EN: "So that's what this was about, Via.<end>"
KO: "…그런 일이었구나, 비아.<end>"

## 1410365
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァイ！　き、聞いてたの？<end>"
EN: "Vai! W-were you listening?<end>"
KO: "바이! 드, 들었어?<end>"

## 1410366
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。…よく言ってくれたわ、ヴィア。<line-break>そして、引き受けてくださったこと…。<line-break>　<line-break>私からも、お礼を言わせてください、<line-break><value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "Yes. I'm glad you said it, Via. And for agreeing to take this on... allow me to thank you as well, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "네. …잘 말해 줬어, 비아. 그리고 맡아 주신 것에 대해 저도 감사드리겠습니다, <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 1410367
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "も、もう！<line-break>だめだめ！<line-break>そんな顔しちゃ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, come on! No, no! Don't look like that!<end>"
KO: "정말! 안 돼, 안 돼! 그런 표정 짓지 마!<end>"

## 1410368
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴィア…？<end>"
EN: "Via...?<end>"
KO: "비아…?<end>"

## 1410369
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これから出発なんだよ？<line-break>どうせ行くなら、楽しく行こうよ！<line-break>ね、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "We're about to set out, aren't we? If we're going anyway, let's enjoy it! Right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "이제 출발하는 거잖아? 어차피 갈 거면 즐겁게 가자! 그렇지, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1410370
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだよね！　それじゃ、行こうよ！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>私たちの、新しい旅に！<end>"
EN: "That's right! Then let's go―on our new journey!<end>"
KO: "맞아! 그럼 가자! 우리의 새로운 여행으로!<end>"

## 1410371

## 1410372
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>お待ちしていました。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I've been waiting for you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 기다리고 있었습니다.<end>"

## 1410373
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴィアですか？　ヴィアには、<line-break>一歩先に行ってもらっています。<line-break> <line-break>きっと今頃、私のことを探しているとは<line-break>思いますが…。<end>"
EN: "Via? I had Via go on ahead. I expect Via's looking for me by now...<end>"
KO: "비아요? 비아는 한발 먼저 가게 했습니다. 아마 지금쯤 저를 찾고 있겠지만….<end>"

## 1410374
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしても<value:$28>様に、<line-break>お話ししておきたいことがあったのです。<line-break>旅立ちの前に…。<end>"
EN: "There was something I simply had to tell you before I left, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "꼭 <value:$28>님께 말씀드리고 싶은 게 있었습니다. 떠나시기 전에….<end>"

## 1410375
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦いの日々は、終わりました。<line-break>でも、まだ何かが、心の中で<line-break>くすぶってはいないでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "The days of fighting are over. But isn't there still something smoldering inside you?<end>"
KO: "싸움의 나날은 끝났습니다. 하지만 아직도 마음속에 무언가 타다 남은 것은 없습니까?<end>"

## 1410376
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "不思議なのです。<line-break>情報を整理すればするほど、<line-break>そのくすぶりは赤々と燃え上がる…。<line-break>…起きるはずのない事件が起き、<line-break>解決するはずのない事変が解決していて、<line-break>しかもそれが誰のせいでもないのです。<end>"
EN: "It's strange. The more I sort through the information, the more fiercely that ember burns. Events that should never have happened have happened, crises that should never have been resolved have been resolved, and none of it is anyone's fault.<end>"
KO: "이상합니다. 정보를 정리하면 할수록 그 불씨는 더욱 붉게 타올라요…. 일어날 수 없는 사건이 일어나고, 해결될 수 없는 일이 해결되어 있는데, 게다가 그게 누구의 탓도 아닙니다.<end>"

## 1410377
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様達のご活躍により、<line-break>戦乱は終結し、<line-break>世界は復興しようとしています。<line-break>でも、私の心に、まだ本当の終わりは<line-break>訪れていないのです。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to all that you and your companions have done, <value:$28>, the wars are over and the world is beginning to rebuild. But in my heart, the true end has yet to come.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님들의 활약으로 전란은 끝났고 세계는 재건을 향하고 있습니다. 하지만 제 마음에는 아직 진정한 끝이 찾아오지 않았습니다.<end>"

## 1410378
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのために…その答えを探し出すために、<line-break>私は、あてもない旅へ…<line-break>出ようと思います。<end>"
EN: "And so, to find that answer, I mean to set out on a journey with no destination.<end>"
KO: "그걸 위해… 그 답을 찾아내기 위해 저는 목적지도 없는 여행을… 떠나려고 합니다.<end>"

## 1410379
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心…心のカケラ…。<line-break>あの時なくした…たいせつな…。<end>"
EN: "A heart... a fragment of a heart... Something precious, lost back then...<end>"
KO: "마음… 마음의 조각…. 그때 잃어버린… 소중한….<end>"

## 1410380
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい、<line-break>自分でもわからないんです…。<line-break> <line-break>あの時っていつなのか…。<line-break>心のカケラって、何なのか…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. I don't understand it myself... I don't know when “back then” was, or what this fragment of a heart might be...<end>"
KO: "미안해요, 저도 잘 모르겠어요…. 그때가 언제인지…, 마음의 조각이 무엇인지….<end>"

## 1410381
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、それがあるということだけは、<line-break>なぜかわかるんです。<line-break>わかるから、探さなくてはいけないの。<end>"
EN: "But somehow, I know it exists. And because I know, I have to search for it.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그것이 존재한다는 것만은 왠지 알아요. 알기 때문에 찾아야 해요.<end>"

## 1410382
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ…。<line-break>手伝って…くれるのですか？<end>"
EN: "What...? You'll help me?<end>"
KO: "네…? 도와… 주시는 건가요?<end>"

## 1410383
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう…ありがとうございます、<line-break><value:$28>様。<line-break> <line-break><value:$28>様と一緒なら、<line-break>きっと…きっと…！<end>"
EN: "Thank you... Thank you so much, <value:$28>. If I'm with <value:$28>, then surely... surely...!<end>"
KO: "고마워요… 감사합니다, <value:$28>님. <value:$28>님과 함께라면 분명… 분명…!<end>"

## 1410384
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、ヴァイ、探したよ！<line-break>こんなところで、何して…<line-break>あっ！<end>"
EN: "Vai! I've been looking for you! What are you doing in a place like this...? Oh!<end>"
KO: "정말, 바이, 찾았잖아! 이런 데서 뭐 하… 앗!<end>"

## 1410385
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい、ヴィア。<line-break>改めて出発としましょう。<line-break>私たちの旅を。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, Via. Let's set out again on our journey.<end>"
KO: "미안해, 비아. 다시 출발하자. 우리의 여행을.<end>"

## 1410386
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きっと、見つかるわ。<line-break>私たちの心のカケラ…。<line-break>忘れてしまった、大切な何かが…。<end>"
EN: "We'll find it. That piece of our hearts... the precious something we've forgotten...<end>"
KO: "분명 찾을 수 있을 거야. 우리 마음의 조각…. 잊어버린 소중한 무언가를…<end>"

KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1410391
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "終わりだ…。<line-break>古き神…竜王よ…。<line-break>お前の役目は終わった。<end>"
EN: "It is over... Ancient god... Dragon King. Your role is at an end.<end>"
KO: "끝이다…. 오래된 신… 용왕이여…. 네 역할은 끝났다.<end>"

## 1410392
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　我は、うつつの身を持つ最後の神…<line-break>　　　　人の子よ…、すべての神を殺し、<line-break>　　　人はおのが足で歩めると言えるのか？<end>"
EN: "I am the last god to bear a body in the waking world... O child of man... after slaying every god, can humankind claim to walk on its own feet?<end>"
KO: "나는 현세에 몸을 가진 마지막 신…. 인간의 아이여… 모든 신을 죽이고도 인간은 자기 발로 걸을 수 있다고 말할 수 있느냐?<end>"

## 1410393
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神に手を借りねば歩けぬほど<line-break>我らは弱くは無い。<line-break> <line-break>神々の揺り篭を捨て、<line-break>我らが自らの足で歩く時がきたのだ。<end>"
EN: "We are not so weak that we must rely on a god's aid to walk. The time has come to leave the cradle of the gods and stand on our own two feet.<end>"
KO: "신의 도움 없이는 걷지 못할 만큼 우리는 약하지 않다. 신들의 요람을 버리고 스스로의 발로 걸을 때가 왔다.<end>"

## 1410394
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人類の革新は今、<line-break>これより始まるのだ！<end>"
EN: "Humanity's Evolution begins here and now!<end>"
KO: "인류의 혁신은 지금, 여기서 시작된다!<end>"

## 1410395
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　傲慢なるものよ…<line-break>　 それは破滅へと通じる道だ…<line-break>　 　愚かなり…、愚かなり…<end>"
EN: "Arrogant one... That road leads to ruin... What folly... what folly...<end>"
KO: "오만한 자여… 그것은 파멸로 이어지는 길이다… 어리석구나…, 어리석구나….<end>"

## 1410396
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>終わったな…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... It is over.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 끝났군….<end>"

## 1410397
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうだな。<line-break>これはまだ始まりにすぎない…。<line-break> <line-break>それは苦難に満ちた<line-break>道のりとなるだろう。<end>"
EN: "Yes. But this is only the beginning. The road ahead will be fraught with hardship.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군. 이건 아직 시작에 불과해…. 고난으로 가득한 길이 되겠지.<end>"

## 1410398
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、私は信じている。<line-break>人類の未来を…そして、<line-break>君が見せてくれた無限の可能性を…。<end>"
EN: "Even so, I have faith in humanity's future―and in the limitless potential you have shown me.<end>"
KO: "하지만 나는 믿고 있어. 인류의 미래를… 그리고 네가 보여 준 무한한 가능성을….<end>"

## 1410399
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、戻ろう。<line-break>我々には為さねばならぬことが<line-break>数多くあるのだから…。<end>"
EN: "Come. Let us return. There is still much we must do.<end>"
KO: "자, 돌아가자. 우리에게는 해야 할 일이 많으니까….<end>"

## 1410400
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1410401
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ…、<line-break>世界を守る願い。どうやら、<line-break>今回は君の勝ちのようだね。<end>"
EN: "Ah... the wish to protect the world. It seems this round is yours.<end>"
KO: "이런… 세계를 지키려는 소원. 아무래도 이번엔 네 승리인 것 같네.<end>"

## 1410402
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねぇ？<line-break>もう一度、僕を殺してみないかい？<end>"
EN: "Tell me, won't you try killing me once more?<end>"
KO: "있잖아? 다시 한 번 나를 죽여 볼래?<end>"

## 1410403
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君なら僕を殺せるかもしれないよ？<line-break>無限のソウルである君なら。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps you could kill me. You, the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "자네라면 나를 죽일 수 있을지도 몰라. 무한의 소울인 자네라면.<end>"

## 1410404
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ…<line-break>僕は全ての悲しみを終わらせる。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... I will put an end to all sorrow.<end>"
KO: "후후후… 나는 모든 슬픔을 끝내겠어.<end>"

## 1410405
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それがどんなに大きな<line-break>悲しみをもたらそうとも…<end>"
EN: "No matter how much sorrow it may bring...<end>"
KO: "그것이 아무리 큰 슬픔을 가져온다 해도….<end>"

## 1410406
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それがどんなに大きな<line-break>苦しみをもたらそうとも…<end>"
EN: "No matter how much suffering it may bring...<end>"
KO: "그것이 아무리 큰 고통을 가져온다 해도….<end>"

## 1410407
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かなえられなかった願いに<line-break>安らぎを与えるために。<line-break>それが幾千万の意思さ。<end>"
EN: "So that unfulfilled wishes may find peace. Such is the will of tens of millions.<end>"
KO: "이루지 못한 소원에안식을 주기 위해. 그것이 수천만의 의지야.<end>"

## 1410408
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして君は全ての悲しみを紡ぎ続ける。<end>"
EN: "And you will go on weaving every sorrow together.<end>"
KO: "그리고 너는 모든 슬픔을 계속 엮어 간다.<end>"

## 1410409
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それがどんなに大きな<line-break>悲しみをもたらそうとも…<end>"
EN: "No matter how much sorrow it may bring...<end>"
KO: "그것이 아무리 큰 슬픔을 가져온다 해도….<end>"

## 1410410
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それがどんなに大きな<line-break>苦しみをもたらそうとも…<end>"
EN: "No matter how much suffering it may bring...<end>"
KO: "그것이 아무리 큰 고통을 가져온다 해도….<end>"

## 1410411
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この世に生きる全ての者に<line-break>希望を与えるために。<line-break>それも幾千万の意思さ。<end>"
EN: "So that all who live in this world may have hope. That, too, is the will of tens of millions.<end>"
KO: "이 세상에 사는 모든 이에게 희망을 주기 위해. 그것도 수천만의 의지 중 하나다.<end>"

## 1410412
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君が愛しいよ。<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>僕らが交わすのは口付けではなく刃。<line-break>愛の言葉ではなく呪いの言葉。<end>"
EN: "I adore you, <value:$28>. We trade blades instead of kisses, curses instead of words of love.<end>"
KO: "네가 사랑스럽다. <value:$28>. 우리가 주고받는 건 입맞춤이 아니라 칼날. 사랑의 말이 아니라 저주의 말이지.<end>"

## 1410413
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでも、<line-break>君だけが僕の心を捕らえつづける。<line-break>僕と君、この二人が<line-break>創造という輪を回し続けるんだ。<line-break>フフフ、アハハハハハ。<end>"
EN: "And still, you alone hold my heart captive. You and I―the two of us will keep the wheel of creation turning. Heh heh heh... Ahahahahaha!<end>"
KO: "그래도 너만은 계속 내 마음을 붙잡고 있어. 나와 너, 이 둘이 창조라는 수레바퀴를 계속 돌리는 거야. 후후후, 아하하하하.<end>"

## 1410414
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、僕に死の口付けを！<line-break>君には愛の抱擁を！<line-break>幾千万の願いを賭けて、<line-break>果てなきダンスを踊り続けよう！<end>"
EN: "Now, give me the kiss of death! And I shall give you love's embrace! With the wishes of tens of millions at stake, let us dance on without end!<end>"
KO: "자, 나에게 죽음의 입맞춤을! 너에게는 사랑의 포옹을! 수천수만의 소원을 걸고 끝없는 춤을 계속 추자!<end>"

## 1410415
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1410417
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…、<line-break>僕だ…エルファスだ…。　<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... It's me. Elfas.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>… 나야… 엘파스야….<end>"

## 1410418
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は世界中のソウルと一体になって、<line-break>色々なことを知った…。<line-break>悲しみ…、苦しみ…。<line-break>そして、人の抱える痛みについて…。　<end>"
EN: "I became one with Souls throughout the world, and learned so much... Sadness. Suffering. The pain people carry within them...<end>"
KO: "나는 전 세계의 소울과 하나가 되어 많은 것을 알게 됐어…. 슬픔… 고통…. 그리고 사람이 품고 사는 아픔에 대해서….<end>"

## 1410419
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして何より、生きる喜びや…<line-break>誰かを愛する心の大事さについて…<line-break>僕は…知ることができた。　<end>"
EN: "Above all, I came to understand the joy of being alive... and how precious it is to love someone.<end>"
KO: "그리고 무엇보다 살아가는 기쁨과… 누군가를 사랑하는 마음의 소중함을… 나는 알게 됐어.<end>"

## 1410420
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…人の心の色は、<line-break>無垢な純白ではない。<line-break>けれど、それらに触れて僕は感じた…。<line-break>美しい、と…。<end>"
EN: "Human hearts are not pure, unblemished white. But when I touched them, I felt it... They were beautiful.<end>"
KO: "…사람 마음의 색은 티 없는 순백이 아니야. 하지만 그 모든 것에 닿고 나는 느꼈어…. 아름답다고….<end>"

## 1410421
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、世界は、人の心は、<line-break>こんなにも美しいものだったんだ…。<end>"
EN: "Yes... The world, the human heart... both were so beautiful.<end>"
KO: "그래, 세계는, 사람의 마음은 이렇게나 아름다운 것이었구나…<end>"

## 1410422
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は今まで、そんな当たり前のことに<line-break>気付きはしなかった…。<end>"
EN: "Until now, I had never noticed something so obvious.<end>"
KO: "나는 지금까지 그런 당연한 사실조차 깨닫지 못했어….<end>"

## 1410423
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰かのために生きること、<line-break>誰かを愛するということとは、<line-break>どういうことなのかを、<line-break>君が教えてくれたおかげだ…。<end>"
EN: "You taught me what it means to live for someone, and what it means to love someone.<end>"
KO: "누군가를 위해 살아가는 것, 누군가를 사랑한다는 게 무엇인지 네가 가르쳐 준 덕분이야….<end>"

## 1410424
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君と、もっと親しくなりたかった…。<line-break>だけど、僕はもう旅立たねばならない…。<line-break>最後まで言えなかったことを、<line-break>今、言うよ。<end>"
EN: "I wanted us to grow closer... But now I must depart. There's something I couldn't say until the very end. I'll say it now.<end>"
KO: "너와 좀 더 친해지고 싶었어…. 하지만 나는 이제 떠나야 해…. 끝까지 말하지 못했던 걸 지금 말할게.<end>"

## 1410425
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう…。<line-break><value:$28>…<line-break>そして…僕は、君のことを愛している。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>... And... I love you.<end>"
KO: "고마워…. <value:$28>… 그리고… 나는 널 사랑해.<end>"

JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1410431
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様。<line-break>ようこそ、水の神殿へ。<line-break>　<line-break>お久しぶりです。<line-break>何かあれから<line-break>お変わりはありませんでしたか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, welcome to the Water Temple. It has been a long time. Has anything changed for you since then?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님. 물의 신전에 오신 것을 환영합니다. 오랜만입니다. 그동안 별일 없으셨나요?<end>"

## 1410432
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか…。<line-break>あれだけの大事件が<line-break>起こったのですものね…。<end>"
EN: "I see... After such a momentous event, I suppose that is only natural.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요… 그만한 큰 사건이 일어났으니까요…<end>"

## 1410433
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "色々なことが変わり、<line-break>大勢の人たちの<line-break>生き方が変わるというのも、<line-break>仕方のないことなんでしょう。<end>"
EN: "So much has changed, and I suppose it was inevitable that the way so many people live would change as well.<end>"
KO: "여러 가지가 변하고 많은 사람들의 삶이 달라지는 것도 어쩔 수 없는 일이겠죠.<end>"

## 1410434
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、私もそう思います。<line-break>変わったと言っても、<line-break>今、世界には、<line-break>明るい兆しが見えています。<line-break>ここからでも、<line-break>それがはっきりとわかります。<end>"
EN: "Yes, I think so too. But for all that has changed, there are signs of hope in the world now. I can see them clearly, even from here.<end>"
KO: "네, 저도 그렇게 생각합니다. 변했다고 해도 지금 세계에는 밝은 조짐이 보입니다. 여기서도 분명히 알 수 있어요.<end>"

## 1410435
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここから見る世界は、<line-break>まるで水面に映じて<line-break>ゆらめく光のようです。<line-break>私は、ずっとここで<line-break>それを眺めている。<end>"
EN: "Seen from here, the world is like light shimmering on the surface of the water. I have always remained here, watching it.<end>"
KO: "여기서 보는 세계는 마치 수면에 비쳐 흔들리는 빛과 같습니다. 저는 줄곧 여기서 그것을 바라보고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1410436
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様は、<line-break>そんな私を、変わらず<line-break>この神殿に訪ねてくださいました。<end>"
EN: "And through it all, <value:$28>, you have continued to visit me at this temple.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님은 그런 저를 변함없이 이 신전까지 찾아와 주셨습니다.<end>"

## 1410437
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界は変わっても、<line-break>私たちは、<line-break>あまり変わってはいないのですね。<line-break>フフフ…。<end>"
EN: "The world may have changed, but we have not changed very much, have we? Hee hee...<end>"
KO: "세상이 변해도 우리는 별로 변하지 않았네요. 후후후….<end>"

## 1410438
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですね。<line-break>時々、声をかけてくださる方も<line-break>いらっしゃいます。<line-break>こんなところで閉じこもって、<line-break>外の世界の広さを<line-break>見ないのはもったいないことだ、と…。<end>"
EN: "Yes. From time to time, someone will say that it is a shame to shut myself away in a place like this without seeing how vast the outside world is...<end>"
KO: "그렇네요. 가끔 말을 걸어 주시는 분도 계세요. 이런 곳에 틀어박혀 바깥세상의 넓음을 보지 않는 건 아깝다고….<end>"

## 1410439
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、私、最近特に思うんです。<line-break>変わることも大切ならば、<line-break>また、変わらぬことも、<line-break>同じくらい<line-break>大切なことじゃないのかって。<end>"
EN: "But lately, I have often found myself thinking: if change is important, might it not be just as important for some things to remain unchanged?<end>"
KO: "하지만 전 요즘 특히 이런 생각을 해요. 변하는 것도 중요하다면, 변하지 않는 것도 그만큼 중요한 게 아닐까 하고요.<end>"

## 1410440
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "昔の方々が、<line-break>一生懸命私たちに伝えようとしたこと…<line-break>　<line-break>長い歳月が流れ、<line-break>世の中の風景がどんなに変わっても、<line-break>それにはきっと意味がある。<end>"
EN: "The things those who came before us tried so earnestly to pass down... However many years go by, and however much the world changes, surely they still have meaning.<end>"
KO: "옛사람들이 필사적으로 우리에게 전하려 했던 것… 긴 세월이 흐르고 세상의 풍경이 아무리 달라져도 거기엔 분명 의미가 있습니다.<end>"

## 1410441
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その思いを無駄にしてはならないと…<line-break>そう信じて、ここにいるのです。<line-break> <line-break>ちょっとおばあさんのようだと<line-break>笑われてしまいそうですけれど。<end>"
EN: "I remain here because I believe we must not let their hopes go to waste... Though I suppose people might laugh and say I sound a little like an old woman.<end>"
KO: "그 마음을 헛되게 해서는 안 된다고… 그렇게 믿고 여기 있는 겁니다. 조금 할머니 같다고 웃음 받을지도 모르겠지만요.<end>"

## 1410442
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからも、私はずっとここにいます。<line-break>ミズチの魂と語らい、<line-break>水のせせらぐ音を聞いて、<line-break>ゆっくり、生きていくことでしょう。<end>"
EN: "I will remain here from now on, speaking with the soul of Mizuchi, listening to the murmur of the water, and living out my days at an unhurried pace.<end>"
KO: "앞으로도 저는 계속 여기 있을 겁니다. 미즈치의 영혼과 이야기하고 물 흐르는 소리를 들으며 천천히 살아가겠지요.<end>"

## 1410443
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "旅の途中、<line-break>いつでも立ち寄ってください。<line-break>たくさん、お話をしましょう。<line-break>いつでもここで、イークレムンは、<line-break><value:$28>様のことを<line-break>お待ちしております…。<end>"
EN: "Please stop by whenever your travels bring you this way. We can talk to our hearts' content. Iiklmn will always be here, waiting for you, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "여행 중 언제든 들러 주세요. 많은 이야기를 나눠요. 언제나 여기서 이크레문은 <value:$28>님을 기다리고 있겠습니다….<end>"

## 1410444
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1410445
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1410446
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…許せよ。<line-break>今のわしらは、<line-break>これ以上に良い方法を持たん。<end>"
EN: "...Forgive us. For now, we have no better way.<end>"
KO: "…용서해라. 지금의 우리는 이보다 나은 방법이 없다.<end>"

## 1410447
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつか、良い方法を編み出し、再び、<line-break>そして必ず、お前の元を訪れよう。<line-break>だから、それまで…静かに眠れ。<line-break>眠っていてくれ…。<end>"
EN: "Someday, we'll find a better way, and we'll come back to you without fail. Until then... rest quietly. Please, keep sleeping...<end>"
KO: "언젠가 좋은 방법을 찾아 다시, 반드시 네 곁으로 오겠다. 그러니 그때까지… 조용히 잠들어라. 잠들어 있어 줘….<end>"

## 1410448
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…終わったな。<line-break>これでまたひとつ。<end>"
EN: "It's done. That's one more.<end>"
KO: "…끝났군. 이걸로 또 하나.<end>"

## 1410449
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ…気の若さだけなら、<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>おぬしと同世代と思ってはおるが、<line-break>さすがに体は正直だわい。<end>"
EN: "Good grief... At heart, I still think of myself as young as you, <value:$28>, but the body never lies.<end>"
KO: "허허… 마음만 젊다면 <value:$28>, 나도 자네와 동갑이라고 생각하지만 몸은 역시 솔직하구먼.<end>"

## 1410450
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無理はするな、と？<line-break>ふん、何が無理じゃ、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>できぬことはハナからせぬよ、<line-break>わしという男は。<end>"
EN: "Don't overdo it, you say? Hmph. What do you mean, “overdo it,” <value:$28>? I never attempt anything I can't do in the first place. That's the sort of man I am.<end>"
KO: "무리하지 말라고? 흥, 뭐가 무리냐, <value:$28>. 애초에 못 할 일은 하지 않는다, 나는.<end>"

## 1410451
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "できるからする。<line-break>そう、これが、彼らに対して<line-break>できることだから、わしはしておる。<line-break>しかし…まあ…<end>"
EN: "I do it because I can. Yes, because this is what I can do for them. Still... well...<end>"
KO: "할 수 있으니 한다. 그래, 그들에게 내가 할 수 있는 일이기에 하고 있는 거다. 하지만… 뭐….<end>"

## 1410452
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日、は、ちいっと、<line-break>無茶を、したもんだ、わな。<line-break> <line-break>色気を出して、途中でおぬしの<line-break>手助けを断ったのが悪かった。<line-break>首と腰が痛くなっちまったわい。<end>"
EN: "Today, I may have pushed myself... a little too hard. I got ambitious and turned down your help halfway through. That was a mistake. Now my neck and lower back ache.<end>"
KO: "오늘은 좀 무리를 했구먼. 욕심내서 중간에 네 도움을 거절한 게 잘못이었어. 목이랑 허리가 아파졌네.<end>"

## 1410453
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なに？　助けがいらんのか、とな？<line-break>…ふん、そんなわけあるかい。<line-break>　<line-break>…わしが、こう、年がいもなく<line-break>でしゃばってるのは、<line-break>全てが全て、奴との約束のためじゃ。<end>"
EN: "What? You're asking if I don't need help? Hmph. Of course I do. The only reason I keep butting in like this at my age is a promise I made to someone.<end>"
KO: "뭐? 도움이 필요 없냐고? …흥, 그럴 리가 있나. …내가 이렇게 나잇값도 못 하고 나서는 건 전부 그 녀석과의 약속 때문이야.<end>"

## 1410454
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "封士の心得と一緒に、<line-break>何度も語り聞かせたろうが、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>あきれた奴じゃ、もう忘れたか？<end>"
EN: "I've told you about it time and again, right alongside the Sealer's code, <value:$28>. Honestly, have you forgotten already?<end>"
KO: "봉사의 마음가짐과 함께, 몇 번이나 말해 줬잖느냐, <value:$28>. 한심한 녀석, 벌써 잊었나?<end>"

## 1410455
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしは、<line-break>その約束を果たさねばならぬのじゃ。<line-break>是が非でも、わしの、この手でな…。<line-break>しかし、<value:$28>、<line-break>おぬしの手助けも要る。<line-break>なくてはならん。<end>"
EN: "I must keep that promise, whatever it takes, and by my own hand... But, <value:$28>, I need your help too. I can't do it without you.<end>"
KO: "나는 그 약속을 지켜야 한다. 무슨 일이 있어도 내 손으로 말이다…. 하지만 <value:$28>, 네 도움이 필요하다. 반드시 필요해.<end>"

## 1410456
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "封ぜねばならん子らがため。<line-break>その強大な力を前に<line-break>怖気づかんがため。<line-break>何より…いつか、わしが、<line-break>立ち上がれなくなった時が…ため…。<line-break>うっ…ゴ、ゴホッ……！<end>"
EN: "For the sake of the children who must be sealed. To keep from losing courage before their immense power. Above all... for the day when I can no longer... stand... Ugh... G-gah... Kgh...!<end>"
KO: "봉인해야 할 아이들을 위해. 그 강대한 힘 앞에서도 겁먹지 않기 위해. 무엇보다… 언젠가 내가 일어설 수 없게 되었을 때를… 위해…. 윽… 코, 콜록……!<end>"

## 1410458
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、とは、頼む…約束を…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>………………。<end>"
EN: "The rest... is in your hands... the promise...<end>"
KO: "뒤, 뒤는 부탁해… 약속을…. ……………….<end>"

## 1410459
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なあんてな。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>ちょいとは、心配したか？<line-break>ん？<end>"
EN: "...Just kidding. Did I have you worried for a moment? Hm?<end>"
KO: "…농담이야. 조금은 걱정했나? 응?<end>"

## 1410460
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっはっは！<line-break>世界最強となったはいいが、<line-break>心の面はまだまだ青いようだのう！<end>"
EN: "Ha ha ha! You may have become the strongest in the world, but at heart you're still green!<end>"
KO: "하하하! 세계 최강이 된 건 좋지만 마음은 아직도 풋내기구나!<end>"

## 1410461
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ついてこい、<value:$28>！<line-break>封士イオンズ様が、<line-break>みっちり鍛えてやるぞい！<line-break>わーはっはっはっ！<end>"
EN: "Come with me, <value:$28>! The great Sealer Ions will put you through your paces! Wah-ha-ha-ha!<end>"
KO: "따라와라, <value:$28>! 봉사 이온즈 님이 제대로 단련시켜 주마! 와하하하하!<end>"

## 1410466
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "必殺技だゴブー！<end>"
EN: "Finishing move, gob!<end>"
KO: "필살기다 고브ー!<end>"

## 1410467
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むにゅー…。<end>"
EN: "Munyuu...<end>"
KO: "우뉴우….<end>"

## 1410468
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>むにゅー、ですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho, ho, ho! Munyuu indeed.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 말랑말랑하군요~.<end>"

## 1410469
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お見事です。<line-break>フゴー様、例のお言葉を…。<end>"
EN: "Splendidly done. Lord Hugo, if you would say the words...<end>"
KO: "훌륭합니다. 휴고 님, 그 말씀을….<end>"

## 1410470
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たくさん珍しい品を<line-break>持ってきてくれたのぉ。<end>"
EN: "You've brought us a great many rare items.<end>"
KO: "희귀한 물건을 많이 가져왔구나.<end>"

## 1410471
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは、私たちに<line-break>英知を示してくれました。<end>"
EN: "You have shown us your wisdom.<end>"
KO: "당신은 우리에게 지혜를 보여 주었습니다.<end>"

## 1410472
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールの復興、<line-break>リベルダムの復興に<line-break>大きな貢献をしてくれたのぉ。<end>"
EN: "You have contributed greatly to the restoration of both Rostorl and Liberdam.<end>"
KO: "로스톨과 리벨덤의 복흥에 큰 공헌을 해 주었구나.<end>"

## 1410473
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、博愛心とその強さを<line-break>示してくれました。<end>"
EN: "You have also shown us your compassion and strength.<end>"
KO: "그리고 박애심과 그 강함을 보여 주었습니다.<end>"

## 1410474
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた方こそ、<line-break>ストラスエッジの所有者に<line-break>ふさわしい。<end>"
EN: "You are the ones worthy of possessing Stra's Edge.<end>"
KO: "당신들이야말로 스트라스엣지의 소유자에 어울립니다.<end>"

## 1410475
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴブゴブ団はストラスエッジを手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "The Gob-Gob Gang obtained Stra's Edge.<end>"
KO: "고브고브단은 스트라스 엣지를 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 1410476
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、団長、ついにやったゴブ。<end>"
EN: "C-Captain, we finally did it, gob.<end>"
KO: "다, 단장, 드디어 해냈고블.<end>"

## 1410477
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我ら誇り高いゴブゴブ団は<line-break>ついにその悲願をかなえたゴブ。<line-break> <line-break>我らゴブゴブ団こそ、<line-break>世界一のパーティだゴブ！<end>"
EN: "At last, our proud Gob-Gob Gang has fulfilled its long-cherished dream, gob! The Gob-Gob Gang is the greatest party in the world, gob!<end>"
KO: "우리 자랑스러운 고브고브단은 마침내 소원을 이뤘다 고브. 우리 고브고브단이야말로 세계 최고의 파티다 고브!<end>"

## 1410478
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長！　お祝いするゴブ！<line-break>ごちそうゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Captain! Let's celebrate, gob! A feast, gob!<end>"
KO: "단장! 축하한다 고브! 잔치다 고브.<end>"

## 1410479
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "雨の日も風の日も<line-break>ゴブリン質になった日も<line-break>諦めずにがんばってきてよかったゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Through rain and wind, even when we were taken Goblin hostage, we never gave up―and it was all worth it, gob!<end>"
KO: "비 오는 날도 바람 부는 날도 고블린 인질이 된 날도 포기하지 않고 버텨 온 보람이 있는 고브!<end>"

## 1410480
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マルーン！<line-break>さっそく宿屋の手配を命じるゴブ！<line-break>最高の食事を揃えるゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Maroon! I order you to make arrangements with the inn at once, gob! Have them prepare the finest meal they can!<end>"
KO: "마룬! 당장 여관 준비를 명령하는 고브! 최고의 음식을 마련하는 고브!<end>"

## 1410481
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長！　いってくるゴ…<end>"
EN: "Captain! I'm off, go...<end>"
KO: "단장! 다녀올게고….<end>"

## 1410482
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Not yet, gob.<end>"
KO: "아직 고브.<end>"

## 1410483
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なにをするゴブ？<line-break>急がないと晩ごはんに<line-break>間に合わないゴブ。<end>"
EN: "What are you doing, gob? Hurry, or it'll be too late for dinner, gob.<end>"
KO: "뭐 하는 고브? 서두르지 않으면 저녁밥에 늦는다 고브.<end>"

## 1410484
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごちそうは食べたいゴブ。<line-break>でも、リベルダムにはおなかを<line-break>すかせた人が一杯いるゴブ。<end>"
EN: "I want a feast too, gob. But there are lots of hungry people in Liberdam.<end>"
KO: "맛있는 건 먹고 싶은 고브. 하지만 리벨덤에는 배고픈 사람이 잔뜩 있는 고브.<end>"

## 1410485
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごちそうでなくても、<line-break>おなかは一杯にできるゴブ。<line-break> <line-break>ごちそうを食べるのに使うお金を<line-break>分ければ、おなかをすかせた人が<line-break>何人か救えるゴブ。<end>"
EN: "It doesn't have to be a feast to fill a belly, gob. If we shared the money we'd spend on one, we could save some of those hungry people.<end>"
KO: "진수성찬이 아니어도, 배는 채울 수 있다 고브. 진수성찬에 쓸 돈을나눠 주면, 굶주린 사람을몇 명은 구할 수 있다 고브.<end>"

## 1410486
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おなかをすかせた人が<line-break>いなくなってからでも<line-break>ごちそうはきっと食べれるゴブ。<end>"
EN: "We can still have a feast once no one is going hungry, gob.<end>"
KO: "배고픈 사람이 없어지고 나서도 진수성찬은 분명 먹을 수 있다 고브.<end>"

## 1410487
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…す、凄いゴブ。<line-break>オルナットがごちそうを<line-break>あきらめたゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Th-That's incredible, gob. Ornut gave up a feast!<end>"
KO: "…대, 대단하다 고브. 오르나트가 잔치를 포기했다 고브!<end>"

## 1410488
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…オルナットの言うことが正しいゴブ。<line-break>我ら誇り高いゴブゴブ団は、<line-break>飢えと貧困と戦ってきたゴブが、<line-break>まだ、完全に勝利していないゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Ornut is right, gob. Our proud Gob-Gob Gang has fought hunger and poverty, but our victory is not yet complete, gob!<end>"
KO: "…오르나트 말이 맞다 고브. 우리 자랑스러운 고브고브단은 굶주림과 가난에 맞서 싸워 왔지만 아직 완전히 이기진 못했다 고브!<end>"

## 1410489
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マルーン！　ごちそうを変更して<line-break>炊き出しの準備をするゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Maroon! Change of plans for the feast―prepare food to hand out instead, gob!<end>"
KO: "마룬! 잔치를 변경해서 배식 준비를 하는 고브!<end>"

## 1410490
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、団長…<line-break>自分は今ほどゴブゴブ団に入って<line-break>誇らしいと思ったことはないゴブ！<line-break>行ってくるゴブ。<end>"
EN: "C-Captain... I've never been prouder to belong to the Gob-Gob Gang, gob! I'm on my way!<end>"
KO: "다, 단장… 지금만큼 고브고브단에 들어온 걸 자랑스럽게 생각한 적이 없다고브! 다녀오겠다고브.<end>"

## 1410491
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フゴー殿、ダイダロ殿。<line-break>というワケで、<line-break>ゴブゴブ団は前進するゴブ。<line-break>ゴブゴブ団を認めてくれたことは<line-break>嬉しかったゴブ。<line-break>では、さらばゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Lord Hugo, Lord Daedalo. And so, the Gob-Gob Gang presses onward, gob. We were glad to have your recognition. Farewell, gob!<end>"
KO: "휴고 님, 다이다로 님. 그런 이유로 고브고브단은 전진한다 고브. 고브고브단을 인정해 줘서 기뻤다 고브. 그럼 안녕이다 고브!<end>"

## 1410492
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど…。<line-break>あなたが彼らを推薦した理由が<line-break>わかるような気がします。<line-break>種族や国を超えて助け合う彼らこそ、<line-break>あらたなる世界に相応しい人材<line-break>なのかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "I think I understand now why you recommended them. Perhaps people like these, who help one another beyond the bounds of race and nation, are precisely what the new world needs.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요…. 당신이 그들을 추천한 이유를 알 것 같습니다. 종족과 국가를 넘어 서로 돕는 그들이야말로 새로운 세계에 어울리는 인재일지도 모르겠군요.<end>"

## 1410493
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかもしれんのぅ。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps so.<end>"
KO: "그럴지도 모르겠구나.<end>"

## 1410494
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>そうですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho, ho, ho! I believe you're right.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇네요.<end>"

## 1410495
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼らにはまだまだ苦難の道が<line-break>待っているでしょう。<line-break> <line-break>あなたのような人が彼らの力に<line-break>なってくれることを望みます。<end>"
EN: "Many hardships still await them. I hope someone like you will lend them strength.<end>"
KO: "그들 앞에는 아직도 고난의 길이 기다리고 있겠지요. 당신 같은 사람이 그들에게 힘이 되어 주길 바랍니다.<end>"

## 1410496
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！<line-break>鍋の用意ができたゴブよー。<line-break> <line-break>野菜を切るの<line-break>手伝って欲しいゴブー！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! The pot's ready, gob! Come help chop the vegetables, gob!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 냄비 준비가 됐다 고브. 채소 써는 것 좀 도와줬으면 한다 고브!<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1410501
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは、実に素晴らしい！<line-break>この尋常でない色あい、デザイン…<line-break>まさにガルドの頭飾り、<line-break>本物に間違いありません。<line-break>良い値で買い取らせていただきます。<end>"
EN: "This is magnificent! Such extraordinary coloring, such design... It is unmistakably an authentic Gald headdress. I would be happy to pay a good price for it.<end>"
KO: "이건 정말 훌륭하군요! 이 평범하지 않은 색감과 디자인… 틀림없는 진짜 갈드의 머리장식입니다. 좋은 값에 사겠습니다.<end>"

## 1410502
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつもながら<line-break><value:$28>の手際は見事じゃのぉ。<end>"
EN: "As always, <value:$28>, masterfully done.<end>"
KO: "언제 봐도 <value:$28>의 솜씨는 훌륭하구먼.<end>"

## 1410503
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>そーですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho, ho, ho! Quite so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇군요~.<end>"

## 1410504
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私などが言うまでもなく、<line-break><value:$28>様は、世界でも<line-break>超一流の冒険家でいらっしゃいます。<line-break>…フゴー様、よい機会、<line-break>例のものも依頼なさってみては？<end>"
EN: "Not that it needs saying, but <value:$28> ranks among the finest adventurers in the world. Hugo, this is an excellent opportunity. Why not ask for that item as well?<end>"
KO: "제가 말씀드릴 것도 없이 <value:$28>님은 세계에서도 초일류 모험가이십니다. …휴고 님, 좋은 기회인데 그 물건도 의뢰해 보시는 게 어떻습니까?<end>"

## 1410505
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほぉぉ、例のものをのぉ。<line-break>しかし、あれはのぉ。<line-break>無理じゃのぉ。<end>"
EN: "Ohhh, that matter. But that would be... No, it's impossible.<end>"
KO: "호오오, 그 물건 말인가. 하지만 그건 말이지. 무리라네.<end>"

## 1410506
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>そーですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho, ho, ho! Quite so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇군요~.<end>"

## 1410507
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼いたしました。<line-break><value:$28>様、では、お礼を…。<end>"
EN: "My apologies. Now then, <value:$28>, your payment...<end>"
KO: "실례했습니다. <value:$28>님, 그럼 사례를….<end>"

## 1410508
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例のもの、でございますか？<line-break>いえ、それは…。<end>"
EN: "That matter, you mean? No, that...<end>"
KO: "그 물건 말씀이십니까? 아니, 그건….<end>"

## 1410509
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>が聞きたいのなら、<line-break>仕方がないのぉ。<end>"
EN: "Well, if <value:$28> wants to hear it, I suppose we have no choice.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>이 듣고 싶다면 어쩔 수 없구먼.<end>"

## 1410510
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>そーですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho, ho, ho! Quite so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇군요~.<end>"

## 1410511
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかりました、<line-break>お話しいたしましょう。<line-break> <line-break>竜の牙というものを、<line-break>ご存知ですか？<end>"
EN: "Very well. I shall tell you. Have you ever heard of the Dragon's Fang?<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 말씀드리죠. '용의 송곳니'라는 것을 알고 계신가요?<end>"

## 1410512
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いかにも、世界にまたとない、<line-break>知られざる宝物です。<line-break>どんな劫火にも焼かれることなく、<line-break>この世のどんな物質をもってしても<line-break>傷ひとつつけることができない<line-break>という代物…。<end>"
EN: "Indeed. It is a treasure the world has never known, and one without equal. No inferno can burn it, and no substance in existence can leave so much as a scratch upon it...<end>"
KO: "과연, 세계에 둘도 없는 알려지지 않은 보물입니다. 어떤 겁화에도 타지 않고 이 세상 어떤 물질로도 흠집 하나 낼 수 없다는 물건….<end>"

## 1410513
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "古代、<line-break>王たちは国を売ってでも<line-break>手に入れたいと言われたとか。<end>"
EN: "It is said that in ancient times, kings would have sold their kingdoms to possess it.<end>"
KO: "고대에는 왕들이 나라를 팔아서라도 손에 넣고 싶어 했다고 하지.<end>"

## 1410514
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "欲しいのぉ。<line-break>竜の牙、手に入れてみたいのぉ。<end>"
EN: "I want it. I'd certainly like to get my hands on the Dragon's Fang.<end>"
KO: "갖고 싶구먼. 용의 송곳니, 한번 손에 넣어 보고 싶구먼.<end>"

## 1410515
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>そーですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho, ho, ho! Quite so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇군요~.<end>"

## 1410516
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、それがどこにあるか、<line-break>どんなものなのかすらも<line-break>伝わってはいません。<line-break>大変なご苦労になるでしょうし、<line-break>おそらく…。<end>"
EN: "But no account survives of where it lies, or even what sort of thing it is. The search would be terribly arduous, and most likely...<end>"
KO: "하지만 그것이 어디 있는지, 어떤 것인지조차 전해지지 않았습니다. 큰 고생이 되겠고, 아마도….<end>"

## 1410517
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでもお探しになりますか？<end>"
EN: "Knowing that, will you still search for it?<end>"
KO: "그래도 계속 찾으시겠습니까?<end>"

## 1410518
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがは<value:$28>だのぉ。<line-break>世界一の冒険家だのぉ。<end>"
EN: "Just what I'd expect from <value:$28>. The greatest adventurer in the world.<end>"
KO: "역시 <value:$28>이구나. 세계 제일의 모험가로구나.<end>"

## 1410519
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>そーですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho, ho, ho! Quite so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇군요~.<end>"

## 1410520
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フゴー様の<line-break>おっしゃるとおりでございます。<line-break>危険な旅…<line-break><value:$28>様、どうかご無事で。<end>"
EN: "Hugo is quite right. It will be a dangerous journey... Please return safely, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "휴고 님 말씀대로입니다. 위험한 여행… <value:$28>님, 부디 무사하시길.<end>"

## 1410521
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フゴー様、<line-break><value:$28>様は、<line-break>無事に竜の牙を手に入れ、<line-break>戻られるでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "Hugo, do you think <value:$28> will obtain the Dragon's Fang and return safely?<end>"
KO: "휴고 님, <value:$28>님은 무사히 용의 송곳니를 얻어 돌아오실까요?<end>"

## 1410522
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まったくわからんのぉ。<line-break>今まで、何十人も行ったがのぉ、<line-break>生きて帰ってきた者が、<line-break>ひとりもおらんからのぉ。<end>"
EN: "I haven't the slightest idea. Dozens have gone looking for it, and not one has ever returned alive.<end>"
KO: "전혀 모르겠구나. 지금까지 수십 명이 갔지만 살아 돌아온 자가 한 명도 없어서 말이야.<end>"

## 1410523
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>そーですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho, ho, ho! Quite so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇군요~.<end>"

## 1410524
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど。<line-break>しかし、それでも冒険へとおもむく。<line-break>それが世界一の冒険者…。<end>"
EN: "I understand. And yet, knowing all that, an adventurer still sets forth. Such is the world's greatest adventurer...<end>"
KO: "그렇군. 하지만 그래도 모험을 떠난다. 그게 세계 최고의 모험가….<end>"

## 1410525
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どえらいのぉ。<line-break>たいしたもんじゃのぉ。<end>"
EN: "That's incredible. Quite a feat.<end>"
KO: "대단하구나. 정말 훌륭하구나.<end>"

## 1410531
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml

## 1410532
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1410533
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1410534
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1410535
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪熱く燃える　世界一の魂～<line-break>♪煮えたぎる　悪党への怒り～<end>"
EN: "♪The world's greatest soul, blazing hot― ♪My rage against villains, boiling over―<end>"
KO: "♪뜨겁게 타오르는 세계 제일의 영혼~ ♪끓어오르는 악당을 향한 분노~<end>"

## 1410536
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "♪이 세상의 사랑을 위협하는 ♪달갑지 않은 녀석의 그림자 ♪내가 모조리 집어던져 주마~!<end>"

## 1410550
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様の魂の輝きで悪は滅んだ。<line-break>安心するがいい！<end>"
EN: "The radiance of my soul has vanquished evil. You may rest easy!<end>"
KO: "이 몸의 영혼의 빛으로 악은 멸망했다. 안심해라!<end>"

## 1410551
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "Ah, thank you very much.<end>"
KO: "아, 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1410552
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様の熱き魂が<line-break>また一つの悪を滅ぼした。<line-break> <line-break>俺様伝説にあらたな１ページが<line-break>加えられた！<end>"
EN: "My burning soul has vanquished yet another evil. A new page has been added to my legend!<end>"
KO: "나의 뜨거운 혼이 또 하나의 악을 멸했다. 내 전설에 새로운 한 페이지가 더해졌다!<end>"

## 1410553
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪熱く燃える　正しい魂～<end>"
EN: "♪A righteous soul, blazing hot―<end>"
KO: "♪뜨겁게 타오르는 올바른 영혼~<end>"

## 1410554
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冗談じゃないぜ…。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>いくらギルドの依頼とはいえ<line-break>なんで、俺が<line-break>ガルドランの護衛なんか…。<end>"
EN: "You've got to be kidding me... Guild job or not, why do I have to escort Girldlan...?<end>"
KO: "농담하지 마…. 아무리 길드 의뢰라 해도 왜 내가 갈드란의 호위 같은 걸….<end>"

## 1410555
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、いいんじゃないかな。<line-break>ガルドランも改心したみたいだし。<line-break>少しでも悪い事する人が減るなら、<line-break>目立つガルドランを<line-break>防犯のマスコットに<line-break>するのも一つの手だと思うよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, maybe it's not such a bad idea. Girldlan does seem to have changed his ways. If it means fewer people doing wrong, I think using someone as conspicuous as Girldlan as a crime-prevention mascot is one way to go.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 괜찮지 않을까. 갈드란도 개심한 것 같고. 조금이라도 나쁜 일을 하는 사람이 줄어든다면 눈에 띄는 갈드란을 방범 마스코트로 쓰는 것도 한 방법이라고 생각해.<end>"

## 1410556

KO: "있잖아. <value:$28>.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
