## 1410562
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは…また妙な質感の石だね。<line-break>小さいけど、ずいぶん重いし…。<line-break> <line-break>…ん？<line-break>あれっ…？<end>"
EN: "This is... another stone with a strange texture. It's small, but remarkably heavy... Hm? What's this...?<end>"
KO: "이건… 또 묘한 질감의 돌이네. 작은데도 꽤 무겁고…. …응? 어라…?<end>"

## 1410563
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…すごい。<line-break>これは、すごい大発見かもしれないよ、<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Incredible. This could be a tremendous discovery, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "…대단해. 이건 엄청난 발견일지도 몰라, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1410564
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いい？　これは明らかに岩石だ。<line-break>でも、ここ見て。<line-break>ガラスみたいにすべすべしてるよね？<end>"
EN: "Now, this is clearly a rock. But look here. It's smooth as glass, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "잘 봐. 이건 분명 바위야. 그런데 여기 봐. 유리처럼 매끈하지?<end>"

## 1410565
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "火山性の鉱物じゃないかって？　<line-break>うん、確かにそういうことはあるよね。<line-break> <line-break>だけど、ここは火山じゃないし、<line-break>岩自体、火山とは無縁の種類さ。<end>"
EN: "You think it might be a volcanic mineral? Yes, that's certainly possible. But this isn't a volcanic area, and this kind of rock has nothing to do with volcanoes.<end>"
KO: "화산성 광물 아니냐고? 응, 그런 경우도 있지. 하지만 여긴 화산이 아니고 암석 자체도 화산과 무관한 종류야.<end>"

## 1410566
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、岩をも溶かすような<line-break>超高熱がここで発生しないかぎり、<line-break>こんな岩はできないはずだ…<end>"
EN: "But this rock couldn't have formed unless something here produced enough heat to melt stone itself...<end>"
KO: "하지만 바위조차 녹일 초고열이 여기서 발생하지 않는 한 이런 바위가 생길 리 없어….<end>"

## 1410567
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱり君もそう思うかい？<line-break>…僕とおんなじだ！<end>"
EN: "So you think so too? That's exactly what I thought!<end>"
KO: "역시 너도 그렇게 생각해? …나랑 똑같네!<end>"

## 1410568
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この辺りには、超古代…<line-break>今の文明が生まれるより<line-break>はるか昔に、<line-break>華やかな謎の王朝があったという<line-break>伝説がある。<end>"
EN: "Legend says that in the remote past, long before our present civilization arose, a magnificent and mysterious dynasty flourished in this region.<end>"
KO: "이 근처에는 초고대… 지금의 문명이 생기기 훨씬 이전에 화려한 수수께끼의 왕조가 있었다는 전설이 있다.<end>"

## 1410569
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、今は、ご覧のとおり。<line-break>王朝はある時、<line-break>跡形もなく滅んでしまったらしい…。<line-break>どうしてなのか？<line-break>どうやってなのか？<end>"
EN: "But now, as you can see, nothing remains. Apparently, at some point, the dynasty was wiped out without a trace... Why? And how?<end>"
KO: "하지만 지금은 보는 그대로다. 왕조는 어느 순간 흔적도 없이 멸망한 모양이야…. 왜였을까? 어떻게였을까?<end>"

## 1410570
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいかい、驚かないでおくれよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>僕はね、この王朝が、<line-break>ものすごく高度な兵器を用いた戦争で<line-break>滅んだんじゃないかって、<line-break>にらんでるんだ！<end>"
EN: "Now, don't be too shocked, <value:$28>. I suspect this dynasty was destroyed in a war fought with extraordinarily advanced weapons!<end>"
KO: "잘 들어, 놀라지 마, <value:$28>. 나는 이 왕조가 엄청나게 고도화된 병기를 사용한 전쟁 때문에 멸망한 게 아닐까 보고 있어!<end>"

## 1410572
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エスト様、<line-break>お約束の日時となりましたので、<line-break>お迎えに参りました。<line-break>そろそろ、ロストールへ…<end>"
EN: "Lord Est, the appointed hour has arrived, so I have come to escort you. It is time we returned to Rostorl...<end>"
KO: "에스트 님, 약속한 시간이 되어 모시러 왔습니다. 이제 로스톨로….<end>"

## 1410573
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん。せっかくだけど、<line-break>それどころじゃないんだ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. I appreciate it, but I'm far too busy for that now.<end>"
KO: "음. 모처럼이지만 지금은 그럴 때가 아니야.<end>"

## 1410574
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "し、しかし…<line-break>そこを何とかなりませんか。<line-break>貴族院のお集まりが…<end>"
EN: "B-but surely some arrangement can be made. The Council of Nobles is convening...<end>"
KO: "하, 하지만… 어떻게든 안 되겠습니까. 귀족원 모임이…<end>"

## 1410575
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、セバスチャンも、<line-break>僕たちの発掘調査を<line-break>手伝ってくれるってのはどうかな？<line-break>そうすれば、早く帰れるかもね。<end>"
EN: "Then how about helping us with the excavation, Sebastian? We might be able to head back sooner that way.<end>"
KO: "그럼 세바스찬도 우리 발굴 조사를 도와주는 건 어때? 그러면 더 빨리 돌아갈 수 있을지도 모르잖아.<end>"

## 1410576
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "手伝うとは申しましても…<end>"
EN: "When you say \"help\"...<end>"
KO: "돕는다고 해도…<end>"

## 1410577
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まさか、湖に潜るなどとは…<end>"
EN: "Surely you cannot mean diving into the lake...<end>"
KO: "설마 호수에 잠수할 줄은…<end>"

## 1410578
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん、もちろんそのつもりだけど。<end>"
EN: "Yes, of course that's what I mean.<end>"
KO: "응, 물론 그럴 생각이지만.<end>"

## 1410579
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ…。<end>"
EN: "Oh...<end>"
KO: "아아….<end>"

## 1410580
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、こんな説はどうかな。<line-break>この湖は、太古の超兵器が<line-break>炸裂した爆心地じゃないのか…？<line-break>ひょっとすると、この湖底には、<line-break>僕の新学説を裏付ける鍵が<line-break>眠ってるかもしれないよね。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, how about this theory? Could this lake be ground zero for the detonation of an ancient superweapon...? There may be something on the lake bed that holds the key to substantiating my new theory.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 이런 가설은 어때? 이 호수가 태고의 초병기가 폭발한 폭심지가 아닐까…? 어쩌면 이 호수 밑에는 내 새 학설을 뒷받침할 단서가 잠들어 있을지도 모르지.<end>"

## 1410581
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だとしたら、凄いよ！<line-break>世界の歴史が<line-break>ひっくり返るかもしれない！<line-break>これは刺激的だっ！<end>"
EN: "If so, this is astonishing! It could overturn the history of the entire world! How exciting!<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 대단해! 세계의 역사가 뒤집힐지도 몰라! 정말 흥미진진해!<end>"

## 1410582
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はあ…。<line-break>リューガ家のあるじになられる方が、<line-break>あんなに泥だらけで…<line-break>私は心配です。<end>"
EN: "Oh dear... To think the future head of House Ryuga would get so covered in mud... I cannot help but worry.<end>"
KO: "하아…. 류가 가문의 주인이 되실 분이 저렇게 흙투성이가 되시다니… 걱정입니다.<end>"

## 1410583
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>も、準備して。<line-break>日が暮れるまでには、<line-break>調査を終わらせなくちゃ！<end>"
EN: "Get ready too, <value:$28>. We have to finish the investigation before sunset!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>도 준비해. 해가 지기 전까지 조사를 끝내야 해!<end>"

## 1410584
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml

EN: "I feel dizzy... Ah...<end>"
KO: "현기증이 납니다…. 아아….<end>"

## 1410586
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ついにここまで来たか…。<line-break>人類を縛る神代の遺物…竜王。<line-break>今日こそ、貴様の命を断ってくれよう。<end>"
EN: "At last, it has come to this... Dragon King―a relic of the Age of the Gods that has kept humanity in chains. Today, I shall end your life.<end>"
KO: "마침내 여기까지 왔군…. 인류를 구속하는 신대의 유물… 용왕. 오늘이야말로 네 목숨을 끊어 주마.<end>"

## 1410587
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これもお前の協力があったからだ。<line-break><value:$28>よ。<end>"
EN: "And it is all thanks to your help, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "이것도 네 협력이 있었기 때문이다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1410588
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "凡庸な人間やできそこないの<line-break>魔道兵など不要だったのだ。<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>お前さえいれば事は足りたのだ。<end>"
EN: "I never needed mediocre humans or defective magic soldiers. <value:$28>, you alone were enough.<end>"
KO: "평범한 인간이나 실패작인 마도병 따윈 필요 없었다. <value:$28>. 너만 있으면 충분했던 거다.<end>"

## 1410589
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ…我が息子にも感謝をせねばな。<line-break>この身体とお前という愛しい存在を<line-break>与えてくれたのだからな…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... I must thank my son as well. He gave me this body―and gave me you, my beloved...<end>"
KO: "후후후… 내 아들에게도 감사해야겠군. 이 몸과 너라는 사랑스러운 존재를 내게 주었으니….<end>"

## 1410590
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、いくぞ！<line-break>この竜を手始めに、全ての魔、<line-break>全ての神、すべての愚民、<line-break>我に逆らう者、全てを屠りさり、<line-break>人類の革新を成し遂げるのだ！<line-break>フフフフフ、ハハハハハハ！<end>"
EN: "Come, let us begin! This dragon shall be the first. Every demon, every god, every witless commoner, and all who defy me―I shall slaughter them all and bring about Humanity's Evolution! Heh heh heh heh... Ha ha ha ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "자, 간다! 이 용을 시작으로 모든 마물, 모든 신, 모든 어리석은 백성, 나에게 거역하는 자 전부를 도륙하고 인류의 혁신을 이루는 것이다! 후후후후후, 하하하하하하!<end>"

## 1420000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml

EN: ""
KO: "노엘2용 메시지 V4 시작<end>"

## 1420001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしてあんたは<line-break>ムリな仕事ばっかりやりたがるの？<end>"
EN: "Why do you always insist on taking jobs that are beyond us?<end>"
KO: "왜 당신은 무리한 일만 자꾸 하려는 거야?<end>"

## 1420002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ムリじゃない。それに、<line-break>こっちの方が人の助けになる。<line-break>ノエルはそれを望んでいる。<end>"
EN: "They're not beyond us. Besides, this one will do more to help people. That's what Noel wants.<end>"
KO: "무리가 아니야. 게다가 이쪽이 사람들에게 더 도움이 돼. 노엘도 그걸 바라고 있어.<end>"

## 1420003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失敗したら元も子もないじゃない！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>失敗するだけなら、まだいいわ。<line-break>この子が危険な目にあったら<line-break>どうするの？<end>"
EN: "If we fail, it'll all have been for nothing! And merely failing would be the least of it. What if this child gets hurt?<end>"
KO: "실패하면 아무 소용 없잖아! 실패만 한다면 그나마 괜찮아. 이 아이가 위험한 일을 당하면 어쩔 거야?<end>"

## 1420004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あのふたりとも<line-break>落ち着いて…<end>"
EN: "P-Please, both of you, calm down...<end>"
KO: "저, 두 분 다 진정하세요….<end>"

## 1420005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいこと？<line-break>確実な仕事をこなすのよ！<end>"
EN: "Listen to me. We should take jobs we know we can handle!<end>"
KO: "알겠지? 확실하게 일을 끝내는 거야!<end>"

## 1420006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…やれやれ。<line-break>格好の大胆さに比べて<line-break>考えることは地味なんだな。<end>"
EN: "Good grief. For someone who dresses so boldly, your thinking is remarkably conservative.<end>"
KO: "…이런 이런. 옷차림의 대담함에 비해 생각은 수수하군.<end>"

## 1420007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "堅実と言いなさい！　堅実と！<end>"
EN: "It's called being prudent! Prudent!<end>"
KO: "착실하다고 해! 착실하다고!<end>"

## 1420008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの、その…。<end>"
EN: "Um, well...<end>"
KO: "저기, 그게….<end>"

## 1420009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>さん！<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28> 씨!<end>"

## 1420010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、久しぶりだね。<end>"
EN: "Hello. It's been a while.<end>"
KO: "이야, 오랜만이군.<end>"

## 1420011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0あ、あら、<line-break>やあね、ボウヤじゃないの。<line-break>…オホホホ、元気そうね。<end>あ、あら、<line-break>やあね、子猫ちゃんじゃないの。<line-break>…オホホホ、元気そうね。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Oh, my! Well, look who it is, young man. Ohohoho... You look well.<end>Oh, my! Well, look who it is, my little kitten. Ohohoho... You look well.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0어, 어머, 이게 누구야, 꼬마잖아.…오호호호, 건강해 보이네.<end>어, 어머, 이게 누구야, 아기 고양이잖아.…오호호호, 건강해 보이네.<end>"

## 1420012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お久しぶりです、<line-break><value:$28>さん！<line-break>わたし、元気にやってますから！<end>"
EN: "It's been so long, <value:$28>! I've been doing just fine!<end>"
KO: "오랜만이에요, <value:$28> 씨! 저, 잘 지내고 있으니까요!<end>"

## 1420013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエルが元気そうで安心した<end>"
EN: "I'm relieved to see Noel doing well.<end>"
KO: "노엘이 건강해 보여서 안심했어.<end>"

## 1420014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエルの元気を分けてほしいよ<end>"
EN: "I could use some of Noel's energy.<end>"
KO: "노엘의 기운을 나눠 줬으면 해<end>"

## 1420015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい、ありがとうございます！<line-break>わたしも、<value:$28>さんが<line-break>お元気そうでうれしいです！<line-break><value:$28>さんが<line-break>がんばっているっていうお話、<line-break>旅先で聞くことがあるんですよ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you! I'm glad to see you're doing well too, <value:$28>! Sometimes, while I'm traveling, I hear stories about how hard <value:$28> has been working.<end>"
KO: "네, 감사합니다! 저도 <value:$28> 씨가 건강해 보여서 기뻐요! <value:$28> 씨가 열심히 하고 있다는 이야기를 여행지에서 듣기도 해요.<end>"

## 1420016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのたびに、わたしも<line-break>がんばらなくっちゃって<line-break>思うんです！<end>"
EN: "Whenever I do, it makes me want to work harder too!<end>"
KO: "그럴 때마다 저도 더 열심히 해야겠다고 생각해요!<end>"

## 1420017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　あの、その、<line-break>…<value:$28>さん、<line-break>何かあったんですか？　　　　　　　<line-break>あ、ごめんなさい。<line-break>立ち入ったことを聞いてしまって…。<line-break>あの、えと…。<end>"
EN: "What? Um, well... <value:$28>, did something happen? Oh, I'm sorry. I shouldn't have pried... Um...<end>"
KO: "어? 저, 그게… <value:$28> 씨, 무슨 일 있었나요? 아, 죄송해요. 너무 캐물었네요…. 저, 그러니까….<end>"

## 1420018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わたし、<value:$28>さんが<line-break>いつまでも、いつまでも<line-break>私の目標であってほしいなって…。<end>"
EN: "I just... I hope you'll always be someone I can look up to, <value:$28>. Always.<end>"
KO: "…저, <value:$28>님이 언제까지나 제 목표였으면 좋겠어요….<end>"

## 1420019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつまで待たせる。<line-break>もう、次の仕事は決めたのか？<line-break>…うん？<line-break>フフ、またお前か。<line-break>よく会うことだな。<end>"
EN: "How long do you intend to keep me waiting? Have you chosen the next job yet? Hmm? Heh. You again. We do seem to run into each other often.<end>"
KO: "언제까지 기다리게 할 거지. 다음 일은 정했나? …응? 후후, 또 너냐. 자주 마주치는군.<end>"

## 1420021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　あの、えと…<end>"
EN: "What? Um, well...<end>"
KO: "어? 저기, 그게….<end>"

## 1420022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先ほど、手続きはすませた。<line-break>多少難度は高いが、<line-break>俺たちには無理ではない仕事だ。<end>"
EN: "I completed the formalities earlier. It will be somewhat difficult, but it isn't beyond us.<end>"
KO: "아까 절차는 끝냈다. 난도는 조금 높지만 우리에게 불가능한 일은 아니야.<end>"

## 1420023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル、さっき、<line-break>引き受けた依頼でいいね？<end>"
EN: "Noel, you're happy with the job we accepted earlier, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "노엘, 아까 받은 의뢰로 괜찮지?<end>"

## 1420024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、はい。<line-break>わたし、それでいいです！<end>"
EN: "Oh, yes. I'm happy with that one!<end>"
KO: "아, 네. 전 그걸로 괜찮아요!<end>"

## 1420025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょ、ちょっと、あんたたち、<line-break>勝手に決めないで…<end>"
EN: "W-Wait a minute! Don't just decide without me...<end>"
KO: "자, 잠깐, 너희들 멋대로 정하지 마….<end>"

## 1420026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。それなら、<line-break>そろそろ出発したいものだな。<line-break>先に行っている。<end>"
EN: "Very well. In that case, we should be leaving soon. I'll go on ahead.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 그렇다면 슬슬 출발하고 싶군. 먼저 가 있겠다.<end>"

## 1420027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、ノエル、<line-break>俺たちもそろそろ行こうか。<end>"
EN: "Well then, Noel, shall we get going too?<end>"
KO: "그럼 노엘, 우리도 슬슬 가자.<end>"

## 1420028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼するよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>君もしっかりな。<end>"
EN: "We'll be off, <value:$28>. You keep at it too.<end>"
KO: "실례하지, <value:$28>. 자네도 힘내게.<end>"

## 1420029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、待ちなさい！<line-break>あたしはまだ納得してないわよ！<end>"
EN: "Wait! I'm still not convinced!<end>"
KO: "아, 잠깐! 난 아직 납득 못 했어!<end>"

## 1420030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ボウヤ、今日は<line-break>かまってあげられなくて　　<line-break>ごめんなさい。<line-break>また、今度<line-break>ゆっくりお話ししましょう。<line-break>じゃあね。<end>子猫ちゃん、今日は<line-break>かまってあげられなくて　　<line-break>ごめんなさい。<line-break>また、今度<line-break>ゆっくりお話ししましょう。<line-break>じゃあね。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Sorry I can't give you any attention today, young man. Let's have a nice, long talk next time. Bye for now.<end>Sorry I can't give you any attention today, my little kitten. Let's have a nice, long talk next time. Bye for now.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0꼬마야, 오늘은 상대해 주지 못해서 미안해. 다음에 천천히 이야기하자. 그럼 안녕.<end>아가씨, 오늘은 상대해 주지 못해서 미안해. 다음에 천천히 이야기하자. 그럼 안녕.<end>"

## 1420031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、カフィン、待って！<end>"
EN: "Oh, Coffin, wait!<end>"
KO: "아, 카핀, 기다려!<end>"

## 1420032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>それじゃ、わたし、失礼します！<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>さんも<line-break>がんばってくださいね！<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. I should be going now! Keep up the good work, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 그럼 저는 이만 실례할게요! <value:$28>님도 힘내세요!<end>"

## 1420033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…、<line-break>最近はずいぶんと<line-break>活躍しているそうだな？<end>"
EN: "I hear you've been quite active lately, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…, 요즘 꽤 활약하고 있다면서?<end>"

## 1420034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、何事もやりすぎないことだ。<line-break>人間はすぐに図に乗るところがある。<line-break>お前もよく肝に銘じておくといい。<line-break>己に課せられた役割は何か。<line-break>己に許された領域はどこまでか。<line-break>よく考えてみるのだな。<end>"
EN: "But do not overextend yourself in anything. Humans are quick to get carried away. You would do well to remember this. What role has been assigned to you? What are the limits of what you are permitted? Think carefully on these things.<end>"
KO: "하지만 뭐든 지나치면 안 된다. 인간은 금세 우쭐대는 면이 있으니까. 너도 잘 명심해 둬라. 자신에게 주어진 역할은 무엇인지, 허락된 영역은 어디까지인지 잘 생각해 봐.<end>"

## 1420035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今はまだいい。許容範囲だ。　　　<line-break>だが、それを逸脱するときは<line-break>…お前を消さねばならない。<line-break>その準備はできている。<line-break>そのために、町をひとつ消してまで<line-break>あの娘を鍛えているのだからな。<end>"
EN: "For now, you are within acceptable bounds. But should you overstep them... I will have to erase you. I am prepared to do so. That is why I went so far as to destroy an entire town to train that girl.<end>"
KO: "지금은 아직 괜찮다. 허용 범위다. 하지만 그걸 벗어나는 순간에는… 널 없애야 한다. 준비는 되어 있어. 그 때문에 마을 하나를 지워 가며 그 아가씨를 단련시키고 있으니까.<end>"

## 1420036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナーシェス。<end>"
EN: "Narscess.<end>"
KO: "나셰스.<end>"

## 1420037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どういうことだ？　　　　　　　<line-break>貴様、ノエルに<line-break>何をさせようとしている？<line-break>ノエルが<value:$28>を<line-break>どのように思っているのか、<line-break>知らないわけではないだろう？<end>"
EN: "What are you talking about? What are you trying to make Noel do? You know full well how Noel feels about <value:$28>, don't you?<end>"
KO: "무슨 뜻이지? 네놈, 노엘에게 뭘 시키려는 거냐? 노엘이 <value:$28>을 어떻게 생각하는지 모르는 건 아니겠지?<end>"

## 1420038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエルは貴様の道具ではない。<end>"
EN: "Noel is not your tool.<end>"
KO: "노엘은 네놈의 도구가 아니다.<end>"

## 1420039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、言うではないか、レイヴン。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ノエルの無限のソウルを呼び起こす<line-break>ために、ひとつの町を消した男の<line-break>言葉とは思えないな。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Big words, Raven, coming from the man who erased an entire town in order to awaken Noel's Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "후후, 제법 말하는군, 레이븐. 노엘의 무한의 소울을 깨우기 위해 한 마을을 없앤 남자의 말이라고는 생각되지 않는군.<end>"

## 1420040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…。<end>"
EN: "Damn...<end>"
KO: "큭….<end>"

## 1420041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、エルファスから助けられて以来<line-break>お前もずいぶんと感情が表に出るように<line-break>なったではないか。<line-break>私が告死天使どもの手から<line-break>助け出したときは、<line-break>人形のようだったのにな。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Ever since Elfas saved you, you've become quite expressive, haven't you? When I saved you from the hands of the Angels of Death, you were like a doll.<end>"
KO: "후후, 엘파스에게 구원받은 뒤로 너도 꽤 감정을 드러내게 됐군. 내가 고사천사들 손에서 구해 냈을 때는 인형 같았는데 말이지.<end>"

## 1420042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、いい。<line-break>いずれにしても、<value:$28>。<line-break>お前がおとなしくしていればすむ話だ。<line-break>この世界に必要なのは均衡だ。<line-break>平和とはその上にのみ成り立つ。<line-break>個人の感情が優先されるべきではない。<end>"
EN: "Well, never mind. In any case, <value:$28>, this will all be settled if you simply behave yourself. What this world needs is balance. Peace can exist only upon it. Individual feelings must not take precedence.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 됐다. 어쨌든 <value:$28>. 네가 얌전히 있으면 끝날 일이다. 이 세계에 필요한 건 균형이다. 평화는 그 위에서만 성립한다. 개인 감정이 우선되어선 안 돼.<end>"

## 1420043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、失礼する。<end>"
EN: "Then, if you'll excuse me.<end>"
KO: "그럼 실례하지.<end>"

## 1420044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>俺はノエルが悲しむ姿を　　　　　　<line-break>見たくはない。<line-break>俺はノエルのそばにいる。<line-break>ノエルが、それを望んでいる限り…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I do not want to see Noel sad. I will stay by Noel's side for as long as she wants me there...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 난 노엘이 슬퍼하는 모습을 보고 싶지 않아. 난 노엘 곁에 있을 거야. 노엘이 그걸 원하는 동안은….<end>"

## 1420045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君が、いつまでも<line-break>ノエルの憧れの対象であることを<line-break>願っているよ。<end>"
EN: "I hope you will always be someone Noel can look up to.<end>"
KO: "네가 언제까지나 노엘이 동경하는 대상이길 바라고 있어.<end>"

## 1420046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破滅の日は近い。<line-break>その日、神の力は<line-break>すべてを打ち砕くであろう。<end>"
EN: "The day of ruin draws near. On that day, the power of God shall shatter all things.<end>"
KO: "파멸의 날은 가깝다. 그날 신의 힘은 모든 것을 부술 것이다.<end>"

## 1420047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すべてを投げ捨てよ。<line-break>真の神にのみすがれ。<line-break>神の他に力はない。<line-break>神を信じ、<line-break>神が遣わしたる我を信ずる者こそ<line-break>救われるのだ。<end>"
EN: "Cast everything aside. Cling only to the true God. There is no power but God's. Only those who believe in God, and in me whom God has sent, shall be saved.<end>"
KO: "모든 것을 내던져라. 참된 신에게만 의지하라. 신 외에는 힘이 없다. 신을 믿고, 신이 보낸 나를 믿는 자만이 구원받으리라.<end>"

## 1420048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やぁ、君か。<end>"
EN: "Ah, it's you.<end>"
KO: "여어, 너구나.<end>"

## 1420049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "目の光が険しくなったね。<line-break>死の羽音の一件が<line-break>気に入らなかったのかい？　<line-break>気にすることはない。<line-break>僕だって彼にかまってばかり<line-break>いられないからね。<end>"
EN: "There's a harder look in your eyes now. Did that business with Death's Wingbeat not sit well with you? Don't let it trouble you. I can't spend all my time attending to him either.<end>"
KO: "눈빛이 험해졌군. 죽음의 날갯소리 건이 마음에 안 들었나? 신경 쓰지 마. 나도 그에게만 매달려 있을 수는 없으니까.<end>"

## 1420050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、そういえばあのとき、<line-break>ネメアが姉さんをさらっていこうと<line-break>するときも、そんなことを言っていた。<line-break>お前にかまっている時間はない、<line-break>ってね。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Come to think of it, Nemea said much the same thing when he came to abduct my sister. “I have no time to waste on you,” he said.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그러고 보니 그때 네메아가 누님을 납치하려 할 때도 그런 말을 했었지. 너한테 신경 쓸 시간은 없다고.<end>"

## 1420051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉さんは、美しかった。<line-break>世界中のどんなものより。<line-break>　<line-break>姉さんの前に出た者は、<line-break>誰もが自分の醜さを恥じて<line-break>うつむいたほどだ。<end>"
EN: "My sister was beautiful. More beautiful than anything in the world. All who stood before her lowered their eyes, ashamed of their own ugliness.<end>"
KO: "누나는 아름다웠다. 세상의 그 무엇보다도. 누나 앞에 선 자는 누구나 자신의 추함을 부끄러워하며 고개를 숙일 정도였지.<end>"

## 1420052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "けれど、卑しい人間どもは違った。<line-break>しかも権力におぼれた人間は、<line-break>一層たちが悪い。<line-break>ディンガル皇帝エリュマルク…<line-break>奴は、姉さんを己のものに<line-break>しようとした。<end>"
EN: "But base human beings were different. Those drunk on power were worse still. Elmark, Emperor of Dyneskal... He tried to make my sister his own.<end>"
KO: "하지만 천한 인간들은 달랐어. 게다가 권력에 빠진 인간은 한층 더 악질이지. 딩갈 황제 에류마르크… 놈은 누나를 자기 것으로 만들려 했어.<end>"

## 1420053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "配下のネメアに姉さんを<line-break>さらうように命じたんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>聞いたことがあるだろう？<line-break>皇帝エリュマルクがネメアに課した<line-break>１２の難事を。<end>"
EN: "He ordered his subordinate Nemea to abduct her. You've heard of the Twelve Labors Emperor Elmark imposed on Nemea, haven't you?<end>"
KO: "부하 네메아에게 누님을 납치하라고 명령했지. 들어본 적 있겠지? 황제 에류마르크가 네메아에게 내린 열두 가지 난제를.<end>"

## 1420054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのひとつに、美女イズをさらえ<line-break>というのがある。<line-break>そのイズこそ、僕の姉さんだ。<line-break>姉さんはネメアを前にして言った。<line-break>自分はどうなってもかまわない。<line-break>だから、弟には手を出さないでと。<end>"
EN: "One of them was to abduct the beautiful Iz. That Iz was my sister. When Nemea came for her, she told him she didn't care what happened to her, so long as he left her younger brother alone.<end>"
KO: "그중 하나가 미녀 이즈를 납치하라는 것이었다. 그 이즈가 바로 내 누나야. 누나는 네메아 앞에서 말했어. 자기는 어떻게 돼도 좋으니 동생에게는 손대지 말라고.<end>"

## 1420055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉さんはネメアの手に抱かれ<line-break>僕の前から消えた。<line-break>　<line-break>…僕は姉さんを守れなかった。<end>"
EN: "Held in Nemea's arms, my sister vanished from my sight. I... couldn't protect her.<end>"
KO: "누나는 네메아의 품에 안겨 내 앞에서 사라졌어. …나는 누나를 지키지 못했어.<end>"

## 1420056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉さんは僕のすべてだった。<line-break>僕にとって、姉さん以上に<line-break>価値のあるものなんて、なかった。<line-break>すべてを奪われた僕にとって、<line-break>世界がどうなろうと、<line-break>大したことじゃないんだ。<end>"
EN: "My sister was everything to me. Nothing in the world mattered more. Once everything had been taken from me, what happened to the world hardly seemed important.<end>"
KO: "누나는 내 전부였어. 내게 누나보다 가치 있는 건 아무것도 없었지. 모든 걸 빼앗긴 내게 세상이 어떻게 되든 대단한 일은 아니야.<end>"

## 1420057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すべてに空しくなった僕は<line-break>気がついたら、施文院の暗殺組織　　<line-break>告死天使の長に納まっていた。<line-break>僕の家は代々施文院の大神官を<line-break>務めていたからね。空虚になった僕に<line-break>おあつらえ向きのポジションだ。<end>"
EN: "Everything had become meaningless to me, and before I knew it, I had settled into the role of leader of the Angels of Death, the Inscription Order's assassination organization. My family had served as the Inscription Order's high priests for generations, you see. It was the perfect position for someone as empty as I was.<end>"
KO: "모든 게 허무해진 나는 정신을 차려 보니 시문원의 암살 조직 고사천사의 우두머리가 되어 있었어. 우리 집안은 대대로 시문원의 대신관을 맡아 왔거든. 공허해진 나한테 딱 맞는 자리였지.<end>"

## 1420058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…君たち冒険者には、<line-break>なんの利益にもならない話だ。　　　　<line-break>忘れてくれ。<line-break>死の羽音はしばらく預けておくよ。<line-break>処分が必要になったら、そのときは<line-break>また出向くことにする…。<end>"
EN: "That story is of no benefit to you adventurers. Forget it. I'll leave Death's Wingbeat with you for the time being. If he needs to be disposed of, I'll come again when the time comes.<end>"
KO: "…너희 모험자들에게는 아무 이득도 없는 이야기야. 잊어 줘. 죽음의 날갯소리는 당분간 맡아 두지. 처분할 필요가 생기면 그때 다시 오겠어….<end>"

## 1420059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その後、姉さんがどうなったか、　<line-break>だって？<line-break>　<line-break>知らないのか？<line-break>絶世の美女イズは、<line-break>皇帝エリュマルクに殺されたんだ。<end>"
EN: "You want to know what happened to my sister afterward? You don't know? Iz, the woman whose beauty was without equal, was killed by Emperor Elmark.<end>"
KO: "그 뒤에 누나가 어떻게 됐냐고? 몰라? 절세의 미녀 이즈는 황제 엘류마르크에게 살해당했어.<end>"

## 1420060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イズの心を奪ったネメアへの<line-break>嫉妬に狂ったエリュマルクにね。<end>"
EN: "He was driven mad with jealousy of Nemea, who had won Iz's heart.<end>"
KO: "이즈의 마음을 빼앗은 네메아를 질투한 엘류마르크에게 말이지.<end>"

## 1420061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、予備メッセージだ。<line-break>ノエル２／Ｖ４、その１。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "후후, 예비 메시지다. 노엘2/V4, 그 1.<end>"

## 1420070

## 1420071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…これでよかったのですか？<end>"
EN: "Was this... really for the best?<end>"
KO: "…이걸로 괜찮았던 건가요?<end>"

## 1420072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "夢だってあったのでしょう？<line-break>欲しいものだってあったのでしょう？<line-break>あなたが望みさえすれば…<line-break>富も名誉も…それこそ全てが<line-break>手に入ったのに。<end>"
EN: "You had dreams, didn't you? Things you wanted? Had you wished for them... wealth, honor―anything at all could have been yours.<end>"
KO: "꿈도 있었겠죠? 갖고 싶은 것도 있었겠죠? 당신이 바라기만 했다면… 부도 명예도… 정말 모든 걸 손에 넣을 수 있었는데.<end>"

## 1420073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでも…<line-break>あなたは、このけがれた抜け殻を<line-break>望むのですか？<end>"
EN: "And still... you want this tainted husk?<end>"
KO: "그래도… 당신은 이 더럽혀진 빈 껍데기를 원하십니까?<end>"

## 1420074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたしさえいなければ…<line-break>この呪われた腕輪に<line-break>縛られるものはなかったのに。<end>"
EN: "If not for me... no one would ever have been bound to this accursed bracelet.<end>"
KO: "나만 없었더라면…이 저주받은 팔찌에속박될 사람은 없었을 텐데.<end>"

## 1420075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "後ろ、向いてください。<end>"
EN: "Turn around, please.<end>"
KO: "뒤돌아 주세요.<end>"

## 1420076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう…逃げられません。<line-break>私も貴方も…。<end>"
EN: "There's no escape now... for either of us.<end>"
KO: "이제… 도망칠 수 없습니다. 저도, 당신도….<end>"

## 1420077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴方の指も…貴方のまなざしも…<line-break>貴方のささやきも私のもの。<line-break> <line-break>私の髪も…私の鼓動も…<line-break>私の全てが貴方のもの。<end>"
EN: "Your fingers... your gaze... your whispers are mine. My hair... my heartbeat... all that I am is yours.<end>"
KO: "당신의 손가락도… 시선도… 속삭임도 내 것. 내 머리카락도… 심장박동도… 나의 모든 것이 당신 것.<end>"

## 1420078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "囚われたのは貴方？<line-break>それとも私？<end>"
EN: "Which of us is the captive? You... or me?<end>"
KO: "붙잡힌 건 당신? 아니면 나?<end>"

## 1420079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1420080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃい。何にしやすかね？<end>"
EN: "Welcome. What can I get you?<end>"
KO: "어서 와. 뭘 찾으시나?<end>"

## 1420081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…量はいらぬ。<line-break>精霊神プレシオーネに<line-break>ささげるにふさわしい、よい酒を。<end>"
EN: "I do not need much. Just a fine wine, worthy of an offering to the Spirit God Pressione.<end>"
KO: "…양은 필요 없다. 정령신 프레시오네께 바치기에 어울리는 좋은 술을.<end>"

## 1420082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は？　冗談言っちゃいけねえ。<line-break>子供に出す酒はねえよ。<end>"
EN: "What? Don't be ridiculous. I don't serve liquor to children.<end>"
KO: "뭐? 농담하지 마. 애한테 줄 술은 없어.<end>"

## 1420083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なに？　親父、<line-break>わらわの言うことが聞けぬというのか。<end>"
EN: "What? Old man, are you saying you refuse to do as I ask?<end>"
KO: "뭐냐? 주인장, 내 말을 듣지 않겠다는 것이냐.<end>"

## 1420084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、聞けねえね。<line-break>俺あ、こんなとこで<line-break>酒場なんてやってるが、<line-break>子供らの未来は、<line-break>健全であってほしいからな。<end>"
EN: "That's right, I do. I may run a tavern in a place like this, but I still want children to have a wholesome future.<end>"
KO: "그래, 못 들어주지. 내가 이런 데서 술집이나 하고 있지만 아이들의 미래만큼은 건전했으면 하거든.<end>"

## 1420085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未来。未来といったな、親父。<line-break>ならば、<line-break>そなたの未来を見てしんぜようか。<line-break>役目を離れたとは言え、<line-break>風の読み手としては<line-break>まだまだ超一流じゃぞ。フフフ…。<end>"
EN: "Future. You said “future,” old man. Then shall I show you yours? I may have left my post, but as a reader of the winds, I remain second to none. Heh heh heh...<end>"
KO: "미래. 미래라고 했지, 영감. 그렇다면 네 미래를 봐 줄까. 역할에서 물러났다고 해도 바람을 읽는 자로서는 아직 일류 중의 일류라고. 후후후….<end>"

## 1420086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、子供がなにスゴんでやがる。<line-break>やれるもんなら、やってみろってんだ。<end>"
EN: "What's a child doing trying to threaten me? Go on, then. Let's see you do it.<end>"
KO: "그러니까 애가 뭘 으름장을 놓고 있어. 할 수 있으면 해 보라고.<end>"

## 1420087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう、わらわに挑戦するか。<line-break>面白い。<line-break> <line-break>…見えるぞ。<line-break>親父、そなたの未来には…。<end>"
EN: "Oh? You challenge me? How amusing. ...I can see it. Old man, in your future...<end>"
KO: "호오, 나에게 도전하겠다는 거냐. 재미있군. …보인다. 아저씨, 그대의 미래에는….<end>"

## 1420088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、<value:$28>じゃねえか。<line-break>まさか、<line-break>あんたがこの子供の保護者なのか？<end>"
EN: "Hey, isn't that <value:$28>? Don't tell me you're this child's guardian.<end>"
KO: "어, <value:$28> 아니야. 설마 네가 이 아이 보호자냐?<end>"

## 1420089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほほほほ。のう、親父。<line-break>これでわかったろうが。<line-break>わらわは子供ではないぞ。<line-break>エアの名ならば、<line-break>そなたも知っておろうが？<line-break>ん？<end>"
EN: "Ho ho ho ho! Well, old man? Now do you understand? I am no child. Surely you know the name Er. Hmm?<end>"
KO: "호호호호. 이보게, 아저씨. 이제 알겠지? 나는 어린애가 아니다. 에아라는 이름이라면 자네도 알고 있겠지? 응?<end>"

## 1420090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エア…？　エアってえと…<line-break>ま、まさかあんた、<line-break>あの、エルズのエアか！<line-break>ひっ！<line-break>こ、こいつは失礼しましたっ！<end>"
EN: "Er...? You mean... N-no, you can't be Er of Elz! Eek! P-please forgive my rudeness!<end>"
KO: "에아…? 에아라면… 서, 설마 당신, 그 엘즈의 에아인가! 힉! 시, 실례했습니다!<end>"

## 1420091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うむ、苦しゅうない。<line-break>ま、それに、わらわは、<line-break>今では<value:$28>と旅する身。<line-break>世襲せしエアの名も、<line-break>あってなきようなものよ。<line-break>親父、酒を。<end>"
EN: "Mm. Think nothing of it. Besides, I now travel with <value:$28>. The hereditary name Er is all but meaningless now. Old man, the wine.<end>"
KO: "음, 괜찮다. 뭐, 지금의 나는 <value:$28>과 여행하는 몸이니 세습되는 에아라는 이름도 있으나 마나 한 셈이지. 주인장, 술을.<end>"

## 1420092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へええ、こいつはこいつは。<line-break>…で、どこまで行かれる旅で？<end>"
EN: "Well, well! So, where are you headed?<end>"
KO: "호오, 이거 이거. …그래서 어디까지 가는 여행이신가?<end>"

## 1420093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "決めてはおらん。<line-break>ただ、これだけはと思うておることは<line-break>ひとつある。<end>"
EN: "I have not decided. But there is one thing I mean to do.<end>"
KO: "정하진 않았다. 다만 이것만은 해야겠다고 생각하는 게 하나 있어.<end>"

## 1420094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "定めや運命などという魔物じみた存在に<line-break>とことん逆らい、<line-break>魔法などに頼らず子を宿し、<line-break>子孫を残すことじゃ…。<line-break>のう、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "I shall defy those monstrous things called fate and destiny to the bitter end, conceive a child without relying on magic, and leave descendants behind... Is that not so, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "정해진 운명 같은 마물 같은 존재에 끝까지 거역하고, 마법에 기대지 않고 아이를 품어 후손을 남기는 것이지…. 그렇지, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1420095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…無理が、ないですかね？<end>"
EN: "Isn't that... asking a bit much?<end>"
KO: "…무리하는 건, 아닌가요?<end>"

## 1420096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜじゃ？<line-break>わらわと<value:$28>は、<line-break>これほど気が合うておるのに？<end>"
EN: "Why? <value:$28> and I get along splendidly, do we not?<end>"
KO: "왜지? 나와 <value:$28>는 이렇게나 마음이 잘 맞는데?<end>"

## 1420097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break><value:$28>、あれだな、<line-break>お前さんもなかなか大変だな…。<end>"
EN: "...<value:$28>, you've got your work cut out for you, don't you?<end>"
KO: "………………. <value:$28>, 너도 참 고생이 많구나….<end>"

## 1420098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親父、酒はまだか。<end>"
EN: "Tavernkeeper, is that wine not ready yet?<end>"
KO: "아저씨, 술은 아직이야?<end>"

## 1420099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…時にエアさん、<line-break>その御神眼で、<line-break>ご自分の行く先は見えねえんで？<end>"
EN: "By the way, Er... can't that divine sight of yours show you where your own path leads?<end>"
KO: "…그런데 에아 씨, 그 신의 눈으로 자신의 앞날은 보이지 않나?<end>"

## 1420100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何を言っておるか。<line-break>わからぬからこそ旅は楽しい。<line-break>…そうではないか？<end>"
EN: "What are you talking about? Not knowing is what makes a journey fun. Don't you agree?<end>"
KO: "무슨 소리냐. 모르기 때문에 여행은 즐거운 것이다. …그렇지 않느냐?<end>"

## 1420101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あらかじめ知ってしまっては、<line-break>不運もないが運もない。<line-break>不幸もないが幸もない…。<line-break>じゃろう、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "If all is known beforehand, there can be no ill fortune, but neither can there be good fortune. No unhappiness, but no happiness either... Would you not agree, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "미리 알아 버리면 불운도 없지만 행운도 없고, 불행도 없지만 행복도 없지…. 그렇지, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1420102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "みな、そなたがわらわに<line-break>教えてくれたことじゃ…。<end>"
EN: "You taught me all of that...<end>"
KO: "모두 네가 내게 가르쳐 준 것들이다….<end>"

## 1420103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆこう、<value:$28>。<line-break>新たな土地へ。新たな未来へ…。<end>"
EN: "Come, <value:$28>. On to new lands and a new future...<end>"
KO: "가자, <value:$28>. 새로운 땅으로. 새로운 미래로….<end>"

## 1420104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…親父、酒はまた今度じゃ。<line-break>また、ここへは来る気がするのでな。<end>"
EN: "Old man, the wine can wait until next time. I have a feeling I will return here.<end>"
KO: "…영감, 술은 다음에 하지. 다시 여기 오게 될 것 같으니까.<end>"

## 1420105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気がする…か。<line-break>エルズのエアが、<line-break>そんな言葉を使うようになるとは…。<line-break>世の中、面白いもんだね、まったく。<end>"
EN: "A feeling, eh? To think Er of Elz would ever speak that way... The world really is an interesting place.<end>"
KO: "느낌이라…. 엘즈의 에아가 그런 말을 쓰게 되다니…. 세상 참 재미있군.<end>"

## 1420106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…これより、ロストールは<line-break>ロセン及びリベルダムの復興にも、<line-break>積極的に助力を開始します。<line-break>よろしいですね？<end>"
EN: "From this day forward, Rostorl will take an active role in rebuilding Rocen and Liberdam as well. I trust there are no objections?<end>"
KO: "…이제부터 로스톨은 로센과 리벨덤의 부흥에도 적극적으로 협력하겠습니다. 괜찮겠죠?<end>"

## 1420107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "し、しかし、<line-break>そんなことをすれば、両国は…<end>"
EN: "B-but if Rostorl does that, the two countries...<end>"
KO: "하, 하지만 그런 짓을 하면 두 나라는….<end>"

## 1420108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それを資金に武力を増強し、<line-break>ロストールを攻めはしないか？<line-break>というのでしょう？<end>"
EN: "You fear they might use the funds to strengthen their armies and attack Rostorl. Is that it?<end>"
KO: "그걸 자금으로 무력을 강화해 로스톨을 공격하지 않겠느냐? 그런 말씀이시죠?<end>"

## 1420109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かに、わが国の財政に<line-break>余裕がないのも確かです。<line-break> <line-break>でも、そんな時だからこそ、<line-break>他国との交流を欠いてはなりません。<end>"
EN: "It is true that our own finances are strained. But that is precisely why we must maintain ties with other countries.<end>"
KO: "확실히 우리 나라 재정에 여유가 없는 것도 사실입니다. 하지만 이런 때일수록 다른 나라와의 교류를 끊어선 안 됩니다.<end>"

## 1420110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　来たか、<value:$28>…。<line-break><line-break><line-break><end>"
EN: "So you have come, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "왔나, <value:$28>….<end>"

## 1420111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　ノエル…？<line-break>　　　　ノエルは、もうこの世の存在ではない。<line-break>　　　　　…貴様がそうしたのだろうが。<line-break>　　　　　我…この竜王と同じように…。<end>"
EN: "Noel...? Noel is no longer of this world. You saw to that, I suppose. Just as you did to me... the Dragon King.<end>"
KO: " 노엘…? 노엘은 이제 이 세상의 존재가 아니다. …네가 그렇게 만들었잖느냐. 나… 이 용왕과 마찬가지로….<end>"

## 1420112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　ノエルのソウルは、<line-break>　　　巨大であるはずの我の精神をも、<line-break>　　　　　無理なく収めた…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Noel's Soul held even my own supposedly vast mind with ease...<end>"
KO: "노엘의 소울은 거대할 터인 나의 정신조차 무리 없이 받아들였다….<end>"

## 1420113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　何のための復活か？<line-break>　　　…むろん、新たな身体を得、力を蓄え、<line-break>　　世の秩序を乱す者に対して、こちらから<line-break>　　　打って出ようとの心よりだったが…<end>"
EN: "Why did I return? Why, of course, to gain a new body, gather my strength, and take the offensive against any who would disrupt the order of the world. That had been my intention...<end>"
KO: "무엇을 위해 부활했는가? …물론 새로운 몸을 얻고 힘을 길러 세상의 질서를 어지럽히는 자들을 먼저 치기 위해서였지만….<end>"

## 1420114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　ククク…そうだ、貴様のことだ、<line-break>　　　　　　<value:$28>。<line-break><line-break><end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... Yes, I mean you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "크크크… 그래, 네놈 말이다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1420115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　…かまわぬ、やればよい。<line-break>　　　　　我は逆らわぬ。<end><line-break><end>"
EN: "...No matter. Do as you will. I shall not resist.<end><end>"
KO: "…상관없다, 하면 된다. 나는 거스르지 않겠다.<end><end>"

## 1420116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　聞け、<value:$28>。<line-break>　もはや、我に神の代弁者たる<line-break>　　　自覚はありはせぬ。<line-break><end>"
EN: "Listen, <value:$28>. I no longer see myself as the voice of God.<end>"
KO: "들어라, <value:$28>. 이제 나는 스스로를 신의 대변자라 여기지 않는다.<end>"

## 1420117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　我は悟ったのだ。<line-break>　　　　　本当に愚かだったのは、<line-break>　　　実は我自身ではなかったかとな。<line-break><end>"
EN: "I have come to understand. Perhaps the true fool was none other than I.<end>"
KO: "나는 깨달았다. 정말 어리석었던 건 사실 나 자신이 아니었을까 하고.<end>"

## 1420118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　貴様に我が倒されたも、それゆえよ。<line-break>　　人の世が、我を必要としなくなったからに<line-break>　　　　　　　　　他ならん…。<line-break><end>"
EN: "That is why you defeated me. It can only be that the human world no longer had need of me...<end>"
KO: "네놈에게 내가 쓰러진 것도 그 때문이다. 인간의 세상이 더는 나를 필요로 하지 않게 되었기 때문이지….<end>"

## 1420119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　そう気付かせてくれたのも、この娘…<line-break>　　　死してなお、息づきつづけるノエルの心…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>　　　　　　　　我は今、安らかだ。<line-break>　　　　これまで感じえなかったほどにな…。<end>"
EN: "It was this girl who brought me to that realization... Noel's heart, still alive even in death. I am at peace now, more deeply than ever before...<end>"
KO: "그 사실을 깨닫게 해 준 것도 이 아이… 죽어서도 여전히 살아 숨 쉬는 노엘의 마음…. 나는 지금 평온하다. 지금껏 느껴 본 적 없을 만큼….<end>"

## 1420120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　これも…無限のソウルの力か…。<line-break><line-break><line-break><end>"
EN: "Is this, too... the power of the Infinite Soul...?<end>"
KO: "이것도… 무한한 소울의 힘인가….<end>"

## 1420121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、<line-break>さっきうなずいてくれたばかりなのに、<line-break>もうそんなことをおっしゃるの？<end>"
EN: "Oh? You nodded just a moment ago, and now you're saying that?<end>"
KO: "어머, 아까 방금 끄덕여 주셨으면서 벌써 그런 말씀을 하시는 건가요?<end>"

## 1420122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ、行きましょ<value:$28>様。<line-break>いつか、<line-break>一緒にお城を抜け出した時みたいに<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. Let's go, <value:$28>―just like that time we slipped out of the castle together.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 가요 <value:$28>님. 언젠가 함께 성에서 빠져나왔던 때처럼.<end>"

## 1420123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…以上、指示したとおり<line-break>ロセン及びリベルダムへの援助は、<line-break>それぞれの要望どおりに実施します。<line-break>よしなに…<end>"
EN: "That is all. As I instructed, aid to Rocen and Liberdam will be provided according to their respective requests. See to it.<end>"
KO: "…이상, 지시한 대로 로센과 리벨덤에 대한 지원은 각각의 요청대로 시행하겠습니다. 잘 부탁드립니다….<end>"

## 1420124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かしこまりました。女王様。<end>"
EN: "As you command, Your Majesty.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 여왕님.<end>"

## 1420125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう…なんだ、旅にでるのか？<end>"
EN: "Hey... What, you're setting off on a journey?<end>"
KO: "어이… 뭐야, 여행을 떠나는 건가?<end>"

## 1420126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "物好きなこったな。<end>"
EN: "You really are an odd one.<end>"
KO: "별난 사람이네.<end>"

## 1420127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で？　今度は何しにいくんだ？<end>"
EN: "So? What are you off to do this time?<end>"
KO: "그래서? 이번엔 뭘 하러 가는 거야?<end>"

## 1420128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "新しい世界を見に行く、だ？<line-break>はっ、お笑いだな。<line-break>　<line-break>世界なんてどこ行ったって<line-break>変わりゃしねえよ。<line-break>つまんねぇもんだぜ。<end>"
EN: "You're going to see a new world? Hah. What a joke. The world's the same wherever you go. It's a dull place.<end>"
KO: "새로운 세계를 보러 간다고? 하, 웃기는군. 세상은 어딜 가도 변하지 않아. 재미없는 곳이지.<end>"

## 1420129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネモ。<value:$28>は、<line-break>すべての種族が手を取り合って<line-break>生きている世界を探しにいくんですよ。<end>"
EN: "Nemo. <value:$28> is going in search of a world where all races live hand in hand.<end>"
KO: "네모. <value:$28>은 모든 종족이 손을 맞잡고 살아가는 세계를 찾으러 가는 겁니다.<end>"

## 1420130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すべての種族？<end>"
EN: "All races?<end>"
KO: "모든 종족?<end>"

## 1420131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。人間も、エルフも、<line-break>ドワーフも…そしてもちろん魔人もね。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Humans, Elves, Dwarves... and demonfolk too, of course.<end>"
KO: "그래. 인간도, 엘프도, 드워프도… 그리고 물론 마인도.<end>"

## 1420132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…だから、お前は<line-break>甘ちゃんだって言ってんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>そんな世界が<line-break>本当にあると思ってんのか？<end>"
EN: "That's why I keep telling you you're too damn naive. You really think a world like that exists?<end>"
KO: "…그러니까 넌 물러터졌다고 하는 거야. 그런 세상이 정말 있다고 생각해?<end>"

## 1420133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "信じているから旅立つのですよ、ネモ。<end>"
EN: "That belief is the very reason for this journey, Nemo.<end>"
KO: "믿고 있으니 떠나는 겁니다, 네모.<end>"

## 1420134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふん、ご苦労なこった。<line-break>じゃあな、あばよ。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Well, good luck with that. See you around.<end>"
KO: "흥, 수고가 많군. 그럼 간다.<end>"

## 1420135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て。<end>"
EN: "Wait.<end>"
KO: "기다려.<end>"

## 1420136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前はまだひよっ子だからな。<line-break>忠告してやる。<end>"
EN: "You're still a greenhorn, so listen up. I've got some advice for you.<end>"
KO: "넌 아직 풋내기니까. 충고 하나 해 주지.<end>"

## 1420137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生水は飲むな。たくさん食って、<line-break>たくさん寝ろ。<line-break>　<line-break>ヤバイと思ったらすぐ逃げろ。<line-break>たやすく他人を信じるな。<end>"
EN: "Don't drink water unless it's been boiled. Eat plenty and get plenty of sleep. If things look bad, run straight away. And don't be too quick to trust anyone.<end>"
KO: "생수는 마시지 마. 많이 먹고 많이 자. 위험하다 싶으면 바로 도망쳐. 쉽게 남을 믿지 마.<end>"

## 1420138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それから、それから…。<end>"
EN: "And... and another thing...<end>"
KO: "그리고, 그리고….<end>"

## 1420139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすくす…ネモ、<line-break>そんなに心配ならついていきますか？<end>"
EN: "Heh... Nemo, if you're that worried, why not go along?<end>"
KO: "후후… 네모, 그렇게 걱정되면 따라갈래요?<end>"

## 1420140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰が！<line-break>心配なんかしてねーよ、こんな奴！<end>"
EN: "Who, me?! I'm not worried about this fool!<end>"
KO: "누가! 이런 녀석 걱정 같은 건 안 하거든!<end>"

## 1420141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残念ですが私たちは、<line-break>いい子でお留守番です。<line-break>　<line-break>これは<value:$28>の旅ですからね。<end>"
EN: "I'm afraid we'll have to be good and stay behind. This is <value:$28>'s journey, after all.<end>"
KO: "아쉽지만 우리는 얌전히 집을 지켜야겠네요. 이건 <value:$28>의 여행이니까요.<end>"

## 1420142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたりめーだ！<end>"
EN: "Damn right!<end>"
KO: "당연하지!<end>"

## 1420143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気をつけて、<value:$28>。<line-break>また会える日を<line-break>楽しみにしていますよ。<end>"
EN: "Take care, <value:$28>. I look forward to the day we meet again.<end>"
KO: "조심하세요, <value:$28>. 다시 만날 날을 기대하고 있을게요.<end>"

## 1420144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…行ってしまいましたか。<line-break>どこまで行っても世界は変わらない。<line-break>　<line-break>平等な世界なんて<line-break>ないのかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "So, you've gone. No matter how far one travels, the world does not change. Perhaps there is no world where all are equal.<end>"
KO: "…가 버렸군요. 어디까지 가도 세상은 변하지 않아. 평등한 세상 같은 건 없을지도 모르겠습니다.<end>"

## 1420145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、そこに生きる人の…<line-break>そこに生きる者たちの<line-break>気持ちは変わります。<line-break>残虐な者もやさしく、<line-break>怒れる者も穏やかに。<end>"
EN: "But the hearts of the people who live in it... of all those who live in it can change. The cruel may grow kind, and the wrathful, gentle.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그곳에 사는 사람들의… 그곳에 사는 자들의 마음은 변합니다. 잔혹한 자도 다정하게, 분노한 자도 온화하게.<end>"

## 1420146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界が変わらなくても、<line-break>そこに生きる者の気持ちが変われば…。<line-break>　<line-break>あなたの夢見た世界は<line-break>そこに生まれるのかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "Even if the world itself does not change, if the hearts of those who live in it do... perhaps the world you dreamed of can be born there.<end>"
KO: "세계가 변하지 않아도 그곳에 사는 이들의 마음이 변한다면…. 당신이 꿈꾼 세계가 거기에서 태어날지도 모릅니다.<end>"

## 1420147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは困難なことかも知れません。<line-break>でも、あなたならできるはず。<line-break>　<line-break>一歩ずつ、ゆっくりと、<line-break>あなたらしく進んでください。<end>"
EN: "It may be a difficult path. But I know you can walk it. One step at a time, slowly, in your own way.<end>"
KO: "그건 어려운 일일지도 모릅니다. 하지만 당신이라면 할 수 있을 겁니다. 한 걸음씩 천천히, 당신답게 나아가세요.<end>"

## 1420148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが魔人の心を変えた時のように。<end>"
EN: "Just as you once changed the heart of a demon.<end>"
KO: "당신이 마인의 마음을 바꿨던 때처럼.<end>"

## 1420149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、わかったわかった。<line-break>おまえはいつもそうだ。<line-break>　<line-break>できもしねえことを<line-break>夢見て突っ走っていくんだからな。<end>"
EN: "All right, all right, I get it. You're always like this―charging off after some dream you haven't got a hope of realizing.<end>"
KO: "그래, 알았다 알았어. 넌 늘 그렇지. 할 수도 없는 일을 꿈꾸며 앞만 보고 달려가니까.<end>"

## 1420150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "てめえのような闇落ちが、<line-break>前の戦乱を<line-break>起こしやがったんじゃねえか。<line-break>バケモノめ、生かしちゃおけるか。<line-break>やれっ！<end>"
EN: "It was darkfallen scum like you who started the last war! You're a monster. We can't let you live. Do it!<end>"
KO: "너 같은 타락한 놈들이 지난 전란을 일으켰잖아. 괴물 놈, 살려 둘 수 없다. 해치워!<end>"

## 1420151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめろ！俺は、何もしちゃいない！<end>"
EN: "Stop! I haven't done anything!<end>"
KO: "그만둬! 난 아무것도 안 했어!<end>"

## 1420152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふざけるな、ダークエルフめ！<line-break>顔を白く塗れば、<line-break>吟遊詩人としてまかり通るってのかよ！<end>"
EN: "Don't give me that, Dark Elf! You think painting your face white is enough to pass yourself off as a bard?<end>"
KO: "웃기지 마, 다크엘프! 얼굴을 하얗게 칠하면 음유시인 행세가 통할 줄 알아!?<end>"

## 1420153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだぜ。<line-break>どの道、エルフに見つかりゃ、<line-break>命はねえのさ。<line-break>てめえらダークエルフは、<line-break>エルフ族の恥だからなあ！<end>"
EN: "That's right. If the Elves find you, you're dead anyway. You Dark Elves are a disgrace to all Elfkind!<end>"
KO: "그래. 어차피 엘프에게 들키면 목숨은 없어. 너희 다크엘프는 엘프족의 수치니까!<end>"

## 1420154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "てめえのような闇落ちが、<line-break>前の戦乱を起こしやがったんじゃねえか。<line-break><line-break>バケモノめ、生かしちゃおけるか。<line-break>やれっ！<end>"
EN: "It was darkfallen scum like you who started the last war! You're a monster. We can't let you live. Do it!<end>"
KO: "너 같은 타락한 놈들이 지난 전란을 일으켰잖아. 괴물 놈, 살려 둘 수 없다. 해치워!<end>"

## 1420155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うっ…！？<end>"
EN: "Urgh...!?<end>"
KO: "윽…!?<end>"

## 1420156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "て、てめえは…！<end>"
EN: "Y-you...!<end>"
KO: "너, 너는…!<end>"

## 1420157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…立ち去れ。<end>"
EN: "...Leave.<end>"
KO: "…물러가라.<end>"

## 1420158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞こえないのか？<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>我が閃光の弓は、<line-break>七つの山を越えた先から、<line-break>貴様の眉間を貫くことができるぞ。<end>"
EN: "Did you not hear me? My Flash Bow can pierce you between the eyes from beyond seven mountains.<end>"
KO: "안 들리나? 내 섬광의 활은 일곱 산 너머에서도 네 미간을 꿰뚫을 수 있다.<end>"

## 1420159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まま、まさかあいつ…<line-break>鉄火姫オイフェか！？<end>"
EN: "C-could that be... Aoife, the Ironfire Princess!?<end>"
KO: "서, 설마 저 녀석… 철화공주 오이페인가!?<end>"

## 1420160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やべえ、逃げるぞっ！<end>"
EN: "Damn it, run!<end>"
KO: "젠장, 도망가자!<end>"

## 1420161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "た、助かりました。<line-break>もう少しでアキュリュースに<line-break>たどりつけるというところで、<line-break>ボロが出るとは<line-break>思っていなかったもので…。<end>"
EN: "Th-thank you. I was almost to Aqyurius. I never thought I'd give myself away when I was so close...<end>"
KO: "사, 살았습니다. 아큐류스에 거의 도착한 곳에서 허점이 드러날 줄은 몰랐습니다….<end>"

## 1420162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アキュリュースに、何をしに行く？<end>"
EN: "What are you going to Aqyurius for?<end>"
KO: "아큐류스에 뭘 하러 가는 거지?<end>"

## 1420164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "答えられないようなことか。<line-break>ならば、やめておいた方がいい。<line-break>本当に、エルフ族の恥になる。<line-break>…行くわよ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Is it something you can't tell me? Then you'd be better off not going. You truly would be a disgrace to the Elves. Let's go, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "대답할 수 없는 일이야? 그럼 그만두는 게 좋아. 정말 엘프족의 수치가 될 거야. …가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1420165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…軽々しく言ってくれる。<line-break>あんたに何がわかる？<line-break>人間のあんたに…！<end>"
EN: "...That's easy for you to say. What could you possibly understand? A human like you...!<end>"
KO: "…쉽게 말하는군. 네가 뭘 알아? 인간인 네가…!<end>"

## 1420166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その答え、自分で考えるといいわ。<line-break>考えている間は、生きることができる。<line-break> <line-break>真剣に考えている間は、<line-break>憎しみを忘れることができる。<end>"
EN: "You should work out the answer for yourself. As long as you keep thinking, you can go on living. As long as you think about it in earnest, you can forget your hatred.<end>"
KO: "그 답은 스스로 생각해 봐. 생각하는 동안은 살아갈 수 있어. 진지하게 생각하는 동안은 증오를 잊을 수 있으니까.<end>"

## 1420167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、その答えが見つかった時、<line-break>私たちは、<line-break>あなたの味方になることができる…。<end>"
EN: "And when you find that answer, we can become your allies...<end>"
KO: "그리고 그 답을 찾았을 때, 우리는 당신의 편이 될 수 있어….<end>"

## 1420169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇に落ちても、<line-break>人は孤独になったわけではないわ。<line-break>大事なことは、生きること。<line-break>そして、探すこと。<line-break>理解者は、あなたが考えているより、<line-break>すぐそばにいるものよ。<end>"
EN: "Falling into darkness does not mean one is alone. What matters is to live―and to keep searching. Someone who understands you may be closer than you think.<end>"
KO: "어둠에 떨어져도 사람은 혼자가 된 게 아니야. 중요한 건 살아가는 것. 그리고 찾는 것. 이해해 줄 사람은 네가 생각하는 것보다 가까이에 있는 법이야.<end>"

## 1420170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1420171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…これより、ロストールは<line-break>ロセン及びリベルダムの復興にも、<line-break>積極的に助力を開始します。<line-break>よろしいですね？<end>"
EN: "From this day forward, Rostorl will take an active role in rebuilding Rocen and Liberdam as well. I trust there are no objections?<end>"
KO: "…이제부터 로스톨은 로센과 리벨덤의 부흥에도 적극적으로 협력하겠습니다. 괜찮겠죠?<end>"

## 1420172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "し、しかし、<line-break>そんなことをすれば、両国は…。<end>"
EN: "B-but if we do that, those two nations may...<end>"
KO: "하, 하지만 그런 짓을 하면 양국은….<end>"

## 1420173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それを資金に武力を増強し、<line-break>ロストールを攻めはしないか？<line-break>というのでしょう？<end>"
EN: "You fear they might use the funds to strengthen their armies and attack Rostorl. Is that it?<end>"
KO: "그걸 자금으로 무력을 강화해 로스톨을 공격하지 않겠느냐? 그런 말씀이시죠?<end>"

## 1420174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わが国の財政に<line-break>余裕がないのも確かです。<line-break> <line-break>でも、そんな時だからこそ、<line-break>他国との交流を欠いてはなりません。<end>"
EN: "It is true that our own treasury can ill afford it. But that is precisely why we must not neglect our ties with other nations.<end>"
KO: "우리 나라 재정에 여유가 없는 것도 사실입니다. 하지만 이런 때일수록 다른 나라와의 교류를 끊어선 안 됩니다.<end>"

## 1420175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンやリベルダムが<line-break>復興することによって出現する<line-break>協調的な国際世界…。<line-break>その渦中において、<line-break>ロストールが発言権を持つための、<line-break>いわばこれは布石なのです。<end>"
EN: "As Rocen and Liberdam recover, a cooperative international order will emerge. This, so to speak, lays the groundwork for Rostorl to have a voice within that order.<end>"
KO: "로센과 리벨덤이 부흥하며 나타날 협조적인 국제 세계…. 그 소용돌이 속에서 로스톨이 발언권을 갖기 위한, 말하자면 이것은 포석입니다.<end>"

## 1420176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そこまでお考えとは…<line-break>余計な詮索をしてしまって<line-break>お恥ずかしい。<line-break>我ら貴族も、<line-break>微力ながら力をお貸しいたします。<end>"
EN: "I-I had no idea you had considered matters so deeply. I am ashamed to have questioned you needlessly. We nobles will also lend what little aid we can.<end>"
KO: "그, 그렇게까지 생각하고 계셨다니…. 괜한 참견을 해 부끄럽습니다. 우리 귀족들도 미력하나마 힘을 보태겠습니다.<end>"

## 1420177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ、お疲れのようね、<line-break><value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. You look tired, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 피곤해 보이시네요, <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 1420178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様が<line-break>いらしてくださったおかげで、<line-break>場がぐっとしまりました。<line-break>きっと、ティアナだけでは、<line-break>まだ説得力に不安がありますもの…。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you being here, <value:$28>, the whole gathering felt much more focused. Tiana alone still wouldn't have been convincing enough...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님께서 와 주신 덕분에 분위기가 훨씬 단단해졌어요. 티아나 혼자였다면 아직 설득력이 부족했을 테니까요….<end>"

## 1420179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ、わかっていますわ。<line-break>まだまだ女王としては力不足…。<line-break> <line-break>でも、やるしかない。<line-break>やらねば、知識も判断力も度胸も<line-break>育ちはしないもの…。<end>"
EN: "No, I understand. I still have much to learn as queen. But I have no choice but to try. If I do not, I will never gain the knowledge, judgment, or courage I need.<end>"
KO: "아니요, 알고 있습니다. 아직 여왕으로서는 역부족…. 하지만 해야 해요. 하지 않으면 지식도 판단력도 배짱도 자라지 않으니까….<end>"

## 1420180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここまで、いろいろなことがあった…。<line-break>そして、いろいろな方々が、<line-break>私のそばからいなくなってしまった。<end>"
EN: "So much has happened along the way. And so many people are no longer by my side.<end>"
KO: "여기까지 여러 일이 있었어요…. 그리고 여러 분이 제 곁에서 사라져 버렸습니다.<end>"

## 1420181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様、<line-break>私は、かわいそうな女でしょうか？<end>"
EN: "Tell me, <value:$28>... am I a pitiable woman?<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>님, 저는 불쌍한 여자일까요?<end>"

## 1420182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あ、忘れていましたわ。<line-break>私には、<value:$28>様が<line-break>いらっしゃるというのに…。<line-break>それなのに、かわいそうだなんて、<line-break><value:$28>様に失礼でしたわ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I had forgotten. I still have you, <value:$28>. To call myself pitiable despite that would be an insult to you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…아, 잊고 있었네요. 제겐 <value:$28>님이 계신데…. 그런데도 불쌍하다고 하다니 <value:$28>님께 실례였어요.<end>"

## 1420183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は、私。<line-break>そうよ、私のやりかたで<line-break>行こうと思います。<line-break>ありがとう、<value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "I am who I am. Yes, I think I shall do things my own way. Thank you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "나는 나. 그래요, 내 방식대로 가려고 합니다. 고마워요, <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 1420184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "安心したら、何だか、<line-break>すっと体が軽くなりましたわ。<end>"
EN: "Now that my mind is at ease, I suddenly feel so much lighter.<end>"
KO: "안심하고 나니 왠지 몸이 가벼워졌어요.<end>"

## 1420185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、<value:$28>様は、<line-break>湖はお好きかしら？<line-break> <line-break>今年も渡り鳥の群れが山の向こうの湖に<line-break>舞い降りたそうですわ。<line-break>これから見に行きませんか？<end>"
EN: "Tell me, <value:$28>, do you like lakes? I hear the flocks of migratory birds have returned to the lake beyond the mountains. Shall we go and see them?<end>"
KO: "저기, <value:$28>님은 호수를 좋아하시나요? 올해도 철새 떼가 산 너머 호수에 내려앉았다던데, 지금 보러 가지 않으실래요?<end>"

## 1420186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、<line-break>さっきうなずいてくれたばかりなのに、<line-break>もうそんなことをおっしゃるの？<end>"
EN: "Oh? You nodded just a moment ago, and now you're saying that?<end>"
KO: "어머, 아까 방금 끄덕여 주셨으면서 벌써 그런 말씀을 하시는 건가요?<end>"

## 1420187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ、行きましょ<value:$28>様。<line-break>いつか、<line-break>一緒にお城を抜け出した時みたいに！<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. Let's go, <value:$28>. Just like that time we slipped out of the castle together!<end>"
KO: "후후후, 가요 <value:$28>님. 언젠가 함께 성을 빠져나갔을 때처럼!<end>"

## 1420188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はもういいよ、とっつあん。<end>"
EN: "I'm all right now, old man.<end>"
KO: "난 이제 됐어, 아저씨.<end>"

## 1420189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、ま、<line-break><value:$28>も大変だな。<end>"
EN: "Still, <value:$28> has a hard time of it too.<end>"
KO: "하지만 뭐, <value:$28>도 고생이 많군.<end>"

## 1420191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…、<line-break>僕だ…エルファスだ…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... It's me. Elfas.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>… 나야… 엘파스야….<end>"

## 1420192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなに不思議かい？<line-break>僕の声が聞こえるのが。<line-break>作り物とはいえ、一応神様だからね。<line-break>これくらいのことはできる。<line-break>最後に君に話したいことがあるんだ。<line-break>聞いてもらえるかい？<end>"
EN: "Is it so strange to hear my voice? I may be artificial, but I'm still a god of sorts. I can manage this much. There's one last thing I want to tell you. Will you hear me out?<end>"
KO: "그렇게 신기해? 내 목소리가 들리는 게. 만들어진 존재라 해도 일단 신이니까. 이 정도는 할 수 있어. 마지막으로 네게 하고 싶은 말이 있어. 들어줄래?<end>"

## 1420193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は世界中のソウルと一体になって、<line-break>色々なことを知った…。<line-break>悲しみ…、苦しみ…。<line-break>そして、人の抱える痛みについて…。　<end>"
EN: "I became one with the Souls throughout the world, and learned so much. Sorrow. Suffering. The pain people carry with them...<end>"
KO: "나는 전 세계의 소울과 하나가 되어 많은 것을 알게 됐어…. 슬픔… 고통…. 그리고 사람이 품고 사는 아픔에 대해서….<end>"

## 1420194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして何より、生きる喜びや…<line-break>誰かを愛する心の大事さについて…<line-break>僕は…知ることができた。　<end>"
EN: "Most of all, I came to know the joy of being alive... and how precious it is to love someone.<end>"
KO: "그리고 무엇보다 살아가는 기쁨과… 누군가를 사랑하는 마음의 소중함을… 나는 알게 됐어.<end>"

## 1420195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…人の心の色は、<line-break>無垢な純白ではない。<line-break>けれど、それらに触れて僕は感じた…。<line-break>美しい、と…。<end>"
EN: "Human hearts aren't a pure and innocent white. Yet when I touched them, I felt it... They were beautiful.<end>"
KO: "…사람 마음의 색은 티 없는 순백이 아니야. 하지만 그 모든 것에 닿고 나는 느꼈어…. 아름답다고….<end>"

## 1420196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、世界は、人の心は、<line-break>こんなにも美しいものだったんだ…。<end>"
EN: "Yes. The world and the human heart were both so beautiful.<end>"
KO: "그래, 세계는, 사람의 마음은 이렇게나 아름다운 것이었구나…<end>"

## 1420197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は今まで、そんな当たり前のことに<line-break>気付きはしなかった…。<end>"
EN: "Until now, I had never noticed something so obvious.<end>"
KO: "나는 지금까지 그런 당연한 사실조차 깨닫지 못했어….<end>"

## 1420198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰かのために生きること、<line-break>誰かを愛するということとは、<line-break>どういうことなのかを、<line-break>君が教えてくれたおかげだ…。<end>"
EN: "You taught me what it means to live for someone, and to love someone.<end>"
KO: "누군가를 위해 살아가는 것, 누군가를 사랑한다는 게 무엇인지 네가 가르쳐 준 덕분이야….<end>"

## 1420199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君と、もっと親しくなりたかった…。<line-break>だけど、僕はもう旅立たねばならない…。<line-break>最後まで言えなかったことを、<line-break>今、言うよ。<end>"
EN: "I wanted us to grow closer... But now I must depart. There's something I couldn't say until the very end. I'll say it now.<end>"
KO: "너와 좀 더 친해지고 싶었어…. 하지만 나는 이제 떠나야 해…. 끝까지 말하지 못했던 걸 지금 말할게.<end>"

## 1420200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう…。<line-break><value:$28>…<line-break>そして…僕は、君のことを愛している。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>... And... I love you.<end>"
KO: "고마워…. <value:$28>… 그리고… 나는 널 사랑해.<end>"

## 1420202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだね…。<line-break><line-break><line-break><value:$28>と、冒険の旅かあ。<line-break>いいなあ！　ボクも行きたいなあ！<end>"
EN: "That's true... Going on adventures with <value:$28>. That sounds wonderful! I want to come too!<end>"
KO: "그러게…. <value:$28>과 모험 여행이라. 좋겠다! 나도 가고 싶어!<end>"

## 1420203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私とおんなじですね、エステル様。<line-break><line-break><line-break>英雄<value:$28>様と<line-break>凄腕女魔法使いと<line-break>男装の麗人のパーティ。<end>"
EN: "Just like me, Lady Esther. A party with the great hero <value:$28>, a brilliant sorceress, and a beautiful woman dressed as a man.<end>"
KO: "저랑 똑같네요, 에스텔 님. 영웅 <value:$28>님과 실력 좋은 여자 마법사, 남장한 미인의 파티.<end>"

## 1420204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きっと依頼がワンサカきて…<line-break>世界一の冒険者も夢じゃありませんよ！<line-break>きゃっ！<end>"
EN: "We'll be flooded with requests... We might even become the greatest adventurers in the world! Eee!<end>"
KO: "분명 의뢰가 잔뜩 들어올 거예요… 세계 제일의 모험가도 꿈이 아니에요! 꺄악!<end>"

## 1420205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…分断の山脈における兵数削減は<line-break>予定どおり進行中である。<line-break> <line-break>…レムオン卿。<line-break>何か不満があるようだな。<end>"
EN: "Troop reductions in the Dividing Mountains are proceeding as planned. Lord Lemghon, you seem dissatisfied.<end>"
KO: "…분단의 산맥에서 병력 감축은 예정대로 진행 중이다. …레무온 경. 불만이 있는 모양이군.<end>"

## 1420206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勿論だ…。<line-break>こちらは国家主席である<line-break>ティアナ王女が出席している。<line-break>そちらはいつになっても皇帝代理だ。<line-break>誠意を持って和平交渉に<line-break>当たっているのか疑ってみたくもなる。<end>"
EN: "Of course I am. Princess Tiana, our head of state, is here in person, while your side is still represented by an imperial proxy. It makes me question whether you are negotiating for peace in good faith.<end>"
KO: "물론이다…. 이쪽은 국가수반인 티아나 왕녀가 참석하고 있다. 그런데 너희 쪽은 언제까지고 황제 대리뿐이지. 성의를 가지고 평화 협상에 임하고 있는지 의심하고 싶어질 정도다.<end>"

## 1420208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "玄武将軍はディンガル王家の<line-break>正当な後継者である。<line-break>感情的な発言は控えてもらいたい。<line-break>それに卿は、<line-break>本来ならここには存在しては<line-break>いけない人間なのだからな…。<end>"
EN: "The Black Tortoise General is the rightful heir to Dyneskal's royal house. I ask that you refrain from such emotional outbursts. Besides, my lord, you are someone who, by rights, should not even be here.<end>"
KO: "현무장군은 딩갈 왕가의 정당한 후계자다. 감정적인 발언은 삼가 주길 바란다. 게다가 경은 원래라면 이곳에 존재해서는 안 되는 인간이니까…<end>"

## 1420209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そちらがご不快になられるのも<line-break>分からない訳ではありません。<line-break>この問題は近日中に解決いたしますわ。<end>"
EN: "I cannot say I do not understand your displeasure. This matter will be resolved soon.<end>"
KO: "…그쪽이 불쾌해하는 것도 이해 못 할 일은 아닙니다. 이 문제는 가까운 시일 안에 해결하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1420210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どういうことですか？<line-break>説明を求めます。<end>"
EN: "What do you mean? I demand an explanation.<end>"
KO: "무슨 뜻입니까? 설명을 요구합니다.<end>"

## 1420211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我々ディンガル帝国は<line-break>新たなる皇帝を擁立するのです。<line-break> <line-break>人類の革新を進めるに相応しい人物を<line-break>…ね。<end>"
EN: "We of the Dyneskal Empire intend to enthrone a new emperor―someone fit to lead Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "우리 딘갈 제국은 새로운 황제를 옹립할 겁니다. 인류의 혁신을 이끌기에 걸맞은 인물을 말이죠….<end>"

## 1420212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まさか！？<line-break>そのような無法が<line-break>許されるとでも思っているのか？<end>"
EN: "Surely not! Do you really think such lawlessness will be tolerated?<end>"
KO: "…설마!? 그런 무법이 허용될 거라 생각하는 건가?<end>"

## 1420213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そちらでは無法と<line-break>見えるのかもしれません。<line-break> <line-break>しかし、我が国は<line-break>血筋よりも能力を尊びます。<end>"
EN: "It may appear lawless to you. But our nation prizes ability above bloodline.<end>"
KO: "그쪽에서는 무법으로 보일지 모르겠습니다. 하지만 우리나라는 혈통보다 능력을 중시합니다.<end>"

## 1420214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "血筋など王家の人間と契りを結べば<line-break>手に入るもの。<line-break> <line-break>そんなものより、人間として、<line-break>英雄として偉大なソウルを持つものが<line-break>必要なのです。<end>"
EN: "A bloodline can be acquired simply by joining oneself to a member of the royal family. More than that, we need someone who possesses a great Soul, both as a human being and as a hero.<end>"
KO: "혈통 따윈 왕가의 사람과 인연을 맺으면 얻을 수 있는 것. 그런 것보다 인간으로서, 영웅으로서 위대한 소울을 가진 자가 필요합니다.<end>"

## 1420215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "……！<line-break>認められません。<line-break>あの方の力がどれほど大きいのか。<line-break>あなた方だって分かっているはずです。<line-break>あなた方は血塗られた覇道を<line-break>再び歩むつもりなのですか！<end>"
EN: "No! I cannot accept this. You know how great that person's power is. Do you intend to tread the bloodstained path of conquest once more?<end>"
KO: "……! 인정할 수 없습니다. 그분의 힘이 얼마나 큰지 당신들도 알고 있을 겁니다. 당신들은 피로 물든 패도를 다시 걸으려는 겁니까!<end>"

## 1420216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何用だ…セバスチャン。<end>"
EN: "What is it, Sebastian?<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이지… 세바스찬.<end>"

## 1420217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "会議中、失礼いたします。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>レムオン様…お耳を…。<end>"
EN: "Forgive the interruption, Lord Lemghon. A word in private...<end>"
KO: "회의 중에 실례하겠습니다. 레무온 님… 잠시 귀를….<end>"

## 1420218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何！？<end>"
EN: "What?!<end>"
KO: "뭐!?<end>"

## 1420219

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まさか！？<line-break>いえ…あの方なら…。<end>"
EN: "Surely not! No... but knowing that person...<end>"
KO: "…설마!? 아니… 그분이라면….<end>"

## 1420220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "伝令を飛ばせ！<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>新たなる皇帝<line-break><value:$28>の<line-break>捜索を開始せよ。<end>"

## 1420228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まさか！？<line-break>そのような無法が<line-break>許されるとでも思っているのか？<end>"
EN: "Surely not! Do you really think such lawlessness will be tolerated?<end>"
KO: "…설마!? 그런 무법이 허용될 거라 생각하는 건가?<end>"

## 1420229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そちらでは無法と<line-break>見えるのかもしれません。<line-break> <line-break>しかし、我が国は<line-break>血筋よりも能力を尊びます。<end>"
EN: "It may appear lawless to you. But our nation prizes ability above bloodline.<end>"
KO: "그쪽에서는 무법으로 보일지 모르겠습니다. 하지만 우리나라는 혈통보다 능력을 중시합니다.<end>"

## 1420230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "血筋など王家の人間と契りを結べば<line-break>手に入るもの。<line-break> <line-break>そんなものより、人間として、<line-break>英雄として偉大なソウルを持つものが<line-break>必要なのです。<end>"
EN: "A bloodline can be acquired simply by joining oneself to a member of the royal family. More than that, we need someone who possesses a great Soul, both as a human being and as a hero.<end>"
KO: "혈통 따윈 왕가의 사람과 인연을 맺으면 얻을 수 있는 것. 그런 것보다 인간으로서, 영웅으로서 위대한 소울을 가진 자가 필요합니다.<end>"

## 1420231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの、それは…。<end>"
EN: "But that...<end>"
KO: "저기, 그건….<end>"

## 1420233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1420234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "당신들에게 필요한 것처럼 우리도 그분의 힘이 필요합니다.<end>"

## 1420235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、それは…。<end>"

KO: "그, 그건….<end>"

## 1420236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1420237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私と結婚なんて言っては<line-break>いませんけど…アトレイア王女。<line-break> <line-break>でも、それはいい考えだわ。<line-break>私はそれでも構わないわ。<end>"

## 1420238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1420246

## 1420247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1420248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えーと、思い出話もたっくさん<line-break>出てると思うんだけど、<line-break> <line-break>何か肝心なものが足りない…<line-break>なんて思ってるのは、ボクだけかな？<end>"

## 1420249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1420254

## 1420255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1420276

## 1420277

## 1420278

## 1420281

## 1420282
