KO: "바이아시온 대륙뿐 아니라 세상 끝까지 보는 여행을.<end>"

## 1420283
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…乗った。<line-break>…それならば、奴といつでも<line-break>剣を交えることができる。<end>"
EN: "I'm in. Then I could cross swords with that one whenever I wished.<end>"
KO: "…탔다. …그렇다면 녀석과 언제든 검을 맞댈 수 있어.<end>"

KO: "가자!<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1420286
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1420287
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1420290
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんともまあ、<line-break>考えようによっては、<line-break>ぜいたくなことじゃな。<line-break>救世の英雄に、見送って<line-break>もらえるのじゃからな。<end>"
EN: "Well, now. Looked at one way, it is quite a luxury to have the hero who saved the world see us off.<end>"
KO: "…뭐랄까, 생각하기에 따라서는 호사스러운 일이군. 구세의 영웅에게 배웅받는 것이니.<end>"

## 1420291
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃんに見送ってもらえるのが、<line-break>ぜいたくか…。<line-break>なんか、わかんねえな。<end>"
EN: "Having Sis see me off is a luxury? I don't really get it.<end>"
KO: "누나가 배웅해 주는 게 사치인가…. 뭔가 잘 모르겠네.<end>"

## 1420292
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なあ、<value:$28>、<line-break>これからどうすんだよ？<line-break> <line-break>ホントに俺んちに遊びに<line-break>来ねえのか？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>, what are you going to do now? Are you really not coming to visit my place?<end>"
KO: "야, <value:$28>, 이제 어떻게 할 거야? 정말 우리 집에 놀러 안 올 거냐?<end>"

## 1420293
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァンはよほど自分の家に<line-break>自信があるんだな。<end>"
EN: "You must have great confidence in your home, Van.<end>"
KO: "반은 자기 집에 꽤 자신이 있나 보군.<end>"

## 1420294
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いーや、その真逆だぜ！<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>最強の冒険者を、<line-break>親父に会わせてやりてえんだ。<line-break>な？　サ、イキョー！<end>"
EN: "No, just the opposite! I want my old man to meet the best adventurer there is. Well? Best be going!<end>"
KO: "아니, 정반대야! 최강의 모험자를 아버지께 보여 드리고 싶은 거라고. 그렇지? 최, 강!<end>"

## 1420295
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最強と、さあ行こう…を<line-break>かけてるんだ。<end>"
EN: "He's playing “the best” off “best be going.”<end>"
KO: "최강과 '자, 가자'를 엮은 말장난이야.<end>"

## 1420296
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ…。<end>"
EN: "Hmph.<end>"
KO: "훗….<end>"

## 1420297
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "テメ、今、鼻で笑ったな！<line-break>うぉぉぉぉっ！<end>"
EN: "You! You just snorted at me! Raaagh!<end>"
KO: "너, 방금 코웃음 쳤지! 우오오오옷!<end>"

## 1420298
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっかましいわい！<end>"
EN: "Pipe down!<end>"
KO: "시끄럽다!<end>"

## 1420299
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐお！！<end>"
EN: "Guh!<end>"
KO: "크오!!<end>"

## 1420300
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ…。<line-break>こうしていると、すぐに<line-break>日が暮れてしまいそうだな。<end>"
EN: "Heh. At this rate, the sun will be down before we know it.<end>"
KO: "후후후…. 이러고 있으면 금세 날이 저물 것 같군.<end>"

## 1420301
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まったく、名残惜しい。<line-break>いつかまた、この面々で<line-break>冒険ができるとよいのじゃがな。<end>"
EN: "It is hard indeed to part. I hope this company may adventure together again someday.<end>"
KO: "정말 아쉽군. 언젠가 다시 이 멤버로 모험할 수 있으면 좋겠네.<end>"

## 1420302
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "不可能だろうな。<end>"
EN: "That will likely be impossible.<end>"
KO: "불가능하겠지.<end>"

## 1420303
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラ…。<end>"
EN: "Sera...<end>"
KO: "세라….<end>"

## 1420304
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜ俺たちがここにいるか、<line-break>考えろ。<line-break> <line-break>俺たちは、それぞれが帰る理由と<line-break>場所を抱えているからこそ、<line-break>今、ここにいる。<end>"
EN: "Think about why we're here. Each of us has a reason to return and a place to return to. That is precisely why we are all here now.<end>"
KO: "우리가 왜 여기 있는지 생각해라. 우리는 각자 돌아갈 이유와 장소가 있기 때문에 지금 여기 있는 거다.<end>"

## 1420305
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そりゃそうだけどよお…。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, I know, but...<end>"
KO: "그야 그렇긴 하지만….<end>"

## 1420306
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、そこは、天運の導きに<line-break>従うまでよ。<line-break> <line-break>考えてみれば、これだけの面々が<line-break>集ったのも、ほとんど偶然のような<line-break>もんじゃからな。<end>"
EN: "Then we can only follow where fortune leads. When you think of it, the gathering of such a company was almost sheer chance to begin with.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그건 천운의 이끄심을 따르면 될 일이지. 생각해 보면 이렇게 많은 사람들이 모인 것도 거의 우연 같은 일이니까.<end>"

## 1420307
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど、<line-break>そう言われればそうだ。<line-break> <line-break>みなずいぶん方々から、<line-break><value:$28>に惹かれて<line-break>やってきたのじゃな。<end>"
EN: "True, now that you mention it. We came from far and wide, all drawn here by <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그렇군, 듣고 보니 그래. 모두 꽤 먼 곳에서 <value:$28>에게 이끌려 온 거구나.<end>"

## 1420308
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、そろそろ<line-break>お別れとしようか。<line-break> <line-break>皆、それぞれの方向へと、<line-break>帰らねばならないのだからな。<end>"
EN: "Then perhaps it is time to say our farewells. We must each return by a different road.<end>"
KO: "그럼 슬슬 헤어질 때인가. 모두 각자의 길로, 돌아가야 하니까.<end>"

## 1420309
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、俺とナッジは<line-break>テラネだ。<end>"
EN: "Right. Nadge and I are heading to Terane.<end>"
KO: "그래, 나와 나지는 테라네야.<end>"

## 1420310
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>近いうち、遊びにきてよ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Come and visit soon, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "응. <value:$28>, 조만간 놀러 와.<end>"

## 1420311
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしはアルノートゥンだ。<line-break>イズキヤルの墓を作ってやらねばな。<line-break> <line-break><value:$28>も、<line-break>墓参りに来てやってくれい。<end>"
EN: "I am bound for Alnortun. I must build a grave for Izkiyal. Come and pay your respects too, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "나는 알노툰으로 간다. 이즈키얄의 무덤을 만들어 줘야지. <value:$28>도 성묘하러 와 주게.<end>"

## 1420312
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私とセラは、ともかく一度、<line-break>ミイスへ向かおうと思う。<end>"
EN: "Sera and I intend to make for Miis first.<end>"
KO: "나와 세라는 일단 미이스로 향하려고 한다.<end>"

## 1420313
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "面倒だがな。<end>"
EN: "Troublesome though it is.<end>"
KO: "귀찮긴 하지만.<end>"

## 1420314
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしはもちろん、<line-break>ドワーフ王国じゃわい。<line-break> <line-break>訪ね来てくれるのであれば、<line-break>でかい炉があるからの、<line-break>いい仕事ができよう。<end>"
EN: "And I return to the Dwarf Kingdom, of course. If you come to visit, we have a great forge there. We could do some fine work.<end>"
KO: "나는 물론 드워프 왕국이지. 찾아온다면 큰 화로가 있으니 좋은 일을 할 수 있을 게야.<end>"

## 1420315
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、俺はおっさんたちと<line-break>途中まで行くぜ。<line-break> <line-break>姉ちゃんがいつか帰ってくるまで<line-break>ノーブルを守るのは、俺だからな。<end>"
EN: "Then I'll travel part of the way with the old fellows. Until Sis comes home someday, I'm the one who'll protect Noble.<end>"
KO: "그럼 난 아저씨들과 중간까지 같이 갈게. 누나가 언젠가 돌아올 때까지 노블을 지키는 건 나니까.<end>"

## 1420316
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "出発だな。<end>"
EN: "Time to depart.<end>"
KO: "출발이군.<end>"

## 1420317
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうした？　行かないのか？<end>"
EN: "What's wrong? Aren't you going?<end>"
KO: "왜 그래? 안 가는 거야?<end>"

## 1420318
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、わしが代表として言おう。<end>"
EN: "Well, I shall speak for us all.<end>"
KO: "아, 내가 대표로 말하지.<end>"

## 1420319
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>おぬしがいては、<line-break>解散できぬではないか！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, we cannot disband while you are still here!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 자네가 있으면 해산할 수가 없지 않나!<end>"

## 1420320
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イオンズ殿の言うとおりじゃ。<line-break>ついつい、パーティとして、<line-break>くっついて行きたくなるわい。<end>"
EN: "Sir Ions is right. We cannot help wanting to follow along as a party.<end>"
KO: "이온즈 공 말대로구먼. 자꾸 파티 일원으로서 따라가고 싶어지는군.<end>"

## 1420321
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "<value:$28>! 해산해도 집에는 안 돌려보낼 셈이냐!<end>"

## 1420322
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "同感だ…。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>ここは、私たちが<line-break><value:$28>を見送った方が<line-break>話がうまく行きそうだな。<end>"

KO: "동감이야…. 여기서는 우리가 <value:$28>을 배웅하는 편이 일이 잘 풀릴 것 같군.<end>"

## 1420323
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1420324

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "아아….<end>"

## 1420332
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1420333
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん、なるほどね。<line-break>で、何にも知らない<line-break><value:$28>が通ったら、<line-break>みんなで一気に身柄を確保しようって<line-break>寸法、ねえ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, I get it. When an unsuspecting <value:$28> comes along, we all pounce and take our quarry into custody.<end>"
KO: "음, 그렇군. 아무것도 모르는 <value:$28>이 지나가면 다 같이 단숨에 붙잡겠다는 계획이네.<end>"

## 1420334
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "知ってるぅ！<line-break>そういうのって、<line-break>まちぶせ、って言うんだよぉ。<end>"
EN: "I know what that's called! It's an ambush!<end>"
KO: "알아아! 그런 걸 매복이라고 하는 거지.<end>"

## 1420335
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まっ、待ち伏せだなんて、<line-break>人聞きの悪い。<line-break> <line-break>アタクシを、そんな悪事の巻きぞえに<line-break>しないでくれるかしら？<end>"
EN: "A-an ambush? How dreadful that sounds. Kindly do not implicate me in such villainy.<end>"
KO: "매, 매복이라니 듣기 거북하네요. 저를 그런 나쁜 일에 끌어들이지 말아 주실래요?<end>"

## 1420336
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그렇다면 좀 좁으니까 비켜 줬으면 하는데.<end>"

## 1420337
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1420340

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1420341

## 1420342
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと！<line-break>大きな声出すんじゃないわよ、<line-break>馬のシッポ女！<line-break>それで<value:$28>が<line-break>気付いたらどうするのよ！<end>"
EN: "Hey! Keep your voice down, horse-tail woman! What if <value:$28> hears you?<end>"
KO: "잠깐! 큰 소리 내지 마, 말꼬리 여자! 그러다 <value:$28>이 눈치채면 어떡해!<end>"

## 1420343
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぶち壊しってんでしょ？<line-break>あのねー、ぶち壊しも何も、こんなトコ、<line-break><value:$28>は通んないっての。<line-break>どこの誰？<line-break>こんなトコで張ろうって言ったのはあ。<end>"
EN: "You mean I'd ruin it? There's nothing to ruin―<value:$28> won't come anywhere near this place. Whose idea was it to stake out this spot, anyway?<end>"
KO: "망친다고? 있잖아, 망치고 말고 할 것도 없이 이런 데를 <value:$28>이 지나갈 리가 없잖아. 대체 누가 여기서 기다리자고 한 거야?<end>"

## 1420344
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フェティだよぉ。<end>"
EN: "Fety's.<end>"
KO: "페티야아.<end>"

## 1420345
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、こらっ！<end>"
EN: "H-hey!<end>"
KO: "이, 이봐!<end>"

## 1420346
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーダメダメ。全くダメよ。<line-break><value:$28>の頭が<line-break>どれだけ切れるか知らないの？<line-break><value:$28>だったら、とっくに<line-break>敵…つまり、あたしらの存在を<line-break>かぎつけてるはずよん。<end>"
EN: "Oh, this is hopeless. Do you have any idea how sharp <value:$28> is? By now, <value:$28> must have sniffed out the enemy's presence―and by that I mean us.<end>"
KO: "아, 안 돼 안 돼. 전혀 안 돼. <value:$28> 머리가 얼마나 잘 돌아가는지 몰라? <value:$28>라면 벌써 적… 그러니까 우리 존재를 눈치챘을걸.<end>"

## 1420347
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そしておそらく、<line-break>裏をついて、可能な限り<line-break>戦闘を避けようとしてる。<end>"
EN: "And probably turned the tables, avoiding a fight wherever possible.<end>"
KO: "그리고 아마 빈틈을 찔러 가능한 한 전투를 피하려는 거야.<end>"

## 1420348
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど。<value:$28>なら、<line-break>それくらいはやってきそう…。<line-break> <line-break>この森には、未開な部分が多いし、<line-break>そこをかいくぐられると…。<end>"
EN: "That makes sense. <value:$28> might well do that. Much of this forest remains unexplored, so if another route is taken through it...<end>"
KO: "그렇군. <value:$28>이라면 그 정도는 해낼 것 같아…. 이 숲은 미개척지가 많으니 그곳으로 빠져나가면….<end>"

## 1420349
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところで、私たち、<line-break>どうしてここで<line-break><value:$28>を待ってるの？<end>"
EN: "By the way, why are we waiting here for <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "그런데 우리, 왜 여기서 <value:$28>를 기다리고 있는 거야?<end>"

## 1420350
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしてって、それは…<end>"
EN: "Why? Well, because...<end>"
KO: "왜냐고? 그건….<end>"

## 1420351
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様のそばに<line-break>お仕えしたいからです。<end>"
EN: "Because we wish to serve at <value:$28>'s side.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님 곁에서 모시고 싶기 때문입니다.<end>"

## 1420352
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お仕えしたい？<line-break>冗談言わないでちょうだい。<end>"
EN: "Serve? Don't be absurd.<end>"
KO: "모시고 싶다고? 농담하지 마.<end>"

## 1420353
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>が、<line-break>どっかのご主人様なんかに<line-break>なるはずないじゃない。<line-break><value:$28>は、<line-break>アタクシとまた冒険に出るのよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> would never become anybody's master. <value:$28> is going adventuring with me again.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>이 어디의 주인님 같은 게 될 리 없잖아. <value:$28>은 나랑 다시 모험을 떠날 거야.<end>"

## 1420354
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだまだ、<line-break>アタクシは驚き足りなくってよー！<end>"
EN: "I have not had nearly enough surprises yet!<end>"
KO: "아직 멀었어요, 저는 놀라움이 부족하답니다!<end>"

## 1420355
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタも行きたいよぉ。<line-break>ヴァイとヴィアも<line-break>一緒にくればいいと思うよぉ。<end>"
EN: "Lulluantha wants to go too. Vai and Via should come with us.<end>"
KO: "루루안타도 가고 싶어. 바이랑 비아도 같이 오면 좋겠어.<end>"

## 1420356
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、でも、私たちは、<line-break>冒険に出たいわけではなく…。<end>"
EN: "B-but we do not wish to go adventuring...<end>"
KO: "하, 하지만 저희는 모험을 떠나고 싶은 게 아니라….<end>"

## 1420357
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ア、アイリーンこそ！<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>何の目的もないのに、<line-break>ここにいるのは、<line-break>おかしいじゃない！？<end>"
EN: "W-what about Irene? It's strange for you to be here when you don't even have a reason!<end>"
KO: "아, 아이린이야말로! 아무 목적도 없으면서 여기 있는 게 이상하잖아!?<end>"

## 1420358
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うっ…。<end>"
EN: "Ngh...<end>"
KO: "윽….<end>"

## 1420359
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかく、落ち着きなさいよー！<line-break>そんなこと言ってる今が、<line-break>一番危険なのよー！<end>"
EN: "Just calm down! We're in the most danger while we're standing here arguing!<end>"
KO: "아무튼 진정하라니까! 그런 소리 하고 있는 지금이 제일 위험하다고!<end>"

## 1420360
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アタクシが考えるに、向こうには<line-break>馬鹿でかい蜂の巣があったから、<line-break> <line-break><value:$28>は、<line-break>ここを通るはずなのよー！<end>"
EN: "By my reasoning, there was an enormous beehive over that way, so <value:$28> is bound to come through here!<end>"
KO: "내 생각에는 저쪽에 엄청나게 큰 벌집이 있었으니까, <value:$28>은 반드시 여길 지나갈 거야!<end>"

## 1420361
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へーへー、ご明察。<line-break>その通りでー。<end>"
EN: "Yes, yes. Brilliant deduction. Absolutely.<end>"
KO: "그래그래, 정확하네. 그 말이 맞아.<end>"

## 1420362
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、何よあんた！　態度でかいわね！<line-break>すでに高貴な私と同じラインに<line-break>立っててよ！<end>"
EN: "W-what is with that attitude? You already speak to me as though you stood on the same lofty plane!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐야 너! 태도가 건방지네! 이미 고귀한 나와 같은 선에 서 있잖아!<end>"

## 1420363
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だってカルラちゃんも<line-break>高貴なんだもんさ！<line-break>しょーがなーいじゃん。<line-break>ねえ、アイリーン？<end>"
EN: "But little Karula is highborn too! Can't be helped. Right, Irene?<end>"
KO: "카를라도 귀한 신분이잖아! 어쩔 수 없지 뭐. 그렇지, 아이린?<end>"

## 1420364
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンは、何か、<line-break>まじめに考えてるよぉ。<end>"
EN: "Irene is thinking seriously about something.<end>"
KO: "아이린은 뭔가 진지하게 생각하고 있어.<end>"

## 1420365
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…一理ある。蜂の巣があって、<line-break>危なくて通れそうにない。<line-break> <line-break>しかし、相手がその一般的な認識すら<line-break>利用するとしたら…？<end>"
EN: "There is some logic to it. The beehive makes that route look too dangerous to use. But what if our quarry is exploiting that very assumption?<end>"
KO: "…일리가 있어. 벌집이 있어서 위험해 지나갈 수 없을 것 같아. 하지만 상대가 그 일반적인 인식까지 이용한다면…?<end>"

## 1420366
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ、もし、すでに私たちの思惑が、<line-break>筒抜けだとしたら…！<end>"
EN: "No―what if our entire plan has already been exposed?<end>"
KO: "아니, 만약 이미 우리의 의도가 전부 새고 있다면…!<end>"

## 1420367
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………！<line-break>ま、まさか、そんなことって…！<end>"
EN: "N-no... surely that can't be...!<end>"
KO: "………………! 서, 설마, 그럴 수가…!<end>"

## 1420368
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は、犯人が、この中にいる…！？<end>"
EN: "I-is the culprit among us?!<end>"
KO: "버, 범인이 이 안에 있다고…!?<end>"

## 1420369
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まー、ンなこと、<line-break>このかれんな乙女たちが<line-break>たくらむわきゃないわねー。<end>"
EN: "Oh, come on. None of us sweet young maidens would plot such a thing.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그런 걸 이 가련한 소녀들이 꾸밀 리 없지.<end>"

## 1420370
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シッポ女と同じなのは<line-break>気に食わないけど、<line-break>アタクシもそう思ってよ。<end>"
EN: "I dislike agreeing with Horse-Tail, but I think so too.<end>"
KO: "꼬리 여자랑 같은 건 마음에 안 들지만 나도 그렇게 생각해.<end>"

## 1420371
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、もう少し待ってみましょう。<line-break>実は正面から現れるかも。<end>"
EN: "Then let us wait a little longer. Perhaps our quarry will simply appear from the front.<end>"
KO: "그럼 조금 더 기다려 보죠. 어쩌면 정면에서 나타날지도 몰라요.<end>"

## 1420372
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来ません…。<end>"
EN: "No one is coming...<end>"
KO: "오지 않습니다….<end>"

## 1420373
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来ないわね…。<end>"
EN: "No one is coming...<end>"
KO: "안 오네….<end>"

KO: "(무사히 지나가서 다행이네, <value:$28>.)<end>"

## 1420380
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行くのか…<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Are you leaving, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "가는 건가… <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1420381
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私は多くの血を流し過ぎた。<line-break>運命と戦うために全てを犠牲にした…。<line-break>だが…お前は私とは違う。<line-break>お前は救うために戦ってきたはずだ。<line-break>そんな、お前の力を<line-break>必要としている人がいる。<end>"
EN: "I have spilled too much blood. I sacrificed everything to defy fate. But you are not like me. You have fought to save others. There are people who need your strength.<end>"
KO: "…나는 너무 많은 피를 흘렸다. 운명과 싸우기 위해 모든 것을 희생했지…. 하지만… 넌 나와 달라. 넌 구하기 위해 싸워 왔을 것이다. 그런 네 힘을 필요로 하는 사람이 있다.<end>"

## 1420382
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前はそれでも行くのか。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Will you still leave, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "그래도 갈 셈인가, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1420383
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "意外と度胸がないのね。<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break> <line-break>自分の力が平和を乱すかもしれない。<line-break>それが恐ろしいのかしら…。<end>"
EN: "You are less courageous than I expected, <value:$28>. Are you afraid your power might disturb the peace?<end>"
KO: "의외로 배짱이 없네, <value:$28>. 자신의 힘이 평화를 어지럽힐지도 모른다. 그게 두려운 거야…?<end>"

## 1420384
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。<line-break>あなたの力は大きいわ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Your power is great.<end>"
KO: "그래. 네 힘은 커.<end>"

## 1420385
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたがその気になれば…<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>この大陸に再び戦渦を引き起こすことも<line-break>できるのでしょうね…。<end>"
EN: "If you chose to, you could likely plunge this continent into the flames of war once more.<end>"
KO: "당신이 마음만 먹는다면… 이 대륙에 다시 전쟁을 일으킬 수도 있겠죠….<end>"

## 1420386
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、お前には仲間がいる。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>それはお前が正しいことに力を<line-break>振るってきた証であり、<line-break>お前の力を抑える枷でもある。<end>"
EN: "But you have companions. Their presence proves that you have used your strength for what is right, and it is also a fetter that restrains that strength.<end>"
KO: "하지만 너에겐 동료가 있다. 그건 네가 옳은 일을 위해 힘을 써 왔다는 증거이자 네 힘을 억누르는 족쇄이기도 하지.<end>"

## 1420387
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこに友がいる限り、その力は破滅を<line-break>招くことはない。<line-break>自分と仲間を信じるがいい。<end>"
EN: "As long as you have friends beside you, your power will not bring ruin. Have faith in yourself and your companions.<end>"
KO: "그곳에 친구가 있는 한 그 힘이 파멸을 부르는 일은 없다. 자신과 동료를 믿어라.<end>"

## 1420388
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その人たちに何も告げずに行くなんて、<line-break>許されないわ。<line-break> <line-break>残される方にも、それぞれの思いが<line-break>あるんだから。<end>"
EN: "You cannot simply leave without saying anything to those people. Each person left behind has feelings of their own.<end>"
KO: "그 사람들에게 아무 말도 하지 않고 가는 건 용납할 수 없어. 남겨지는 쪽에도 저마다의 마음이 있으니까.<end>"

## 1420389
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その辺にしておきなさい。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>ここから先は<line-break><value:$28>自身が<line-break>決めることです。<end>"
EN: "That is enough. What comes next is for <value:$28> to decide.<end>"
KO: "그쯤 해 두세요. 여기서부터는 <value:$28> 본인이 결정할 일입니다.<end>"

## 1420390
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我々に口を挟む権利はありません。<end>"
EN: "We have no right to interfere.<end>"
KO: "우리에게 참견할 권리는 없습니다.<end>"

## 1420391
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、それでは、<line-break>そろそろあなたの望むところへ<line-break>お連れしましょう。<end>"
EN: "Come, then. It is time I took you where you wish to go.<end>"
KO: "자, 그럼 슬슬 당신이 원하는 곳으로 모시겠습니다.<end>"

## 1420392
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仲間の元に戻るのも、<line-break>新たな世界を求めるのも<line-break>それはあなたの自由なのですから…。<end>"
EN: "You are free to return to your companions or to seek a new world.<end>"
KO: "동료들에게 돌아가든 새로운 세계를 찾든 그건 당신의 자유니까요….<end>"

## 1420393
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…さあ、どうぞ。<line-break>あなたが開いた未来への扉は<line-break>一つではありません。<end>"
EN: "Go on. The future you opened has more than one door.<end>"
KO: "…자, 가세요. 당신이 연 미래의 문은 하나뿐이 아닙니다.<end>"

## 1420394
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界は再び、あなたに様々な顔を<line-break>見せてくれるでしょう。<line-break><line-break>自由な旅を！<end>"
EN: "The world will once again show you its many faces. Travel freely!<end>"
KO: "세계는 다시 당신에게 여러 모습을 보여 줄 겁니다. 자유로운 여행을!<end>"

## 1420400
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひっ、ひぃいいい。<line-break>か、金なら好きなだけやる。<line-break>だ、だから見逃してくれ。<end>"
EN: "E-eek! I'll give you as much money as you want! S-so please, let me go!<end>"
KO: "히, 히이이익. 도, 돈이라면 원하는 만큼 줄게. 그, 그러니 살려 줘.<end>"

## 1420401
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "し、しらん。<line-break>わしはしらんぞ！<line-break> <line-break>きょ、強制労働なぞ<line-break>どこでもやっているではないか。<line-break>わ、わしは悪くない！<end>"
EN: "I-I know nothing! Nothing, I tell you! F-forced labor happens everywhere, does it not? I-I've done nothing wrong!<end>"
KO: "모, 몰라. 난 모른다고! 가, 강제 노동 따위 어디서나 하잖아. 나, 난 잘못 없어!<end>"

## 1420402
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "領主様！<end>"
EN: "My lord!<end>"
KO: "영주님!<end>"

## 1420403
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いっ、いいところに来た、お前達。<line-break>こ、こいつを倒したら、褒美を出す。<line-break>りゅ、竜騎士にしてやってもいい！<end>"
EN: "Y-you've come at just the right time! Defeat this one and I'll reward you. I-I might even make you Dragon Knights!<end>"
KO: "마, 마침 잘 왔다, 너희들. 이, 이 녀석을 쓰러뜨리면 상을 주겠다. 용, 용기사로 만들어 줘도 좋아!<end>"

## 1420404
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞いたか！<line-break>…いくぞ！<end>"
EN: "You heard that! Let's go!<end>"
KO: "들었지! …간다!<end>"

## 1420405
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　…そうはさせん。<end>"
EN: "…I won't let that happen.<end>"
KO: "…그렇게는 못 한다.<end>"

## 1420406
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐわぁ！<end>"
EN: "Gwaaah!<end>"
KO: "크와악!<end>"

## 1420407
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う、うわぁあああ！！<end>"
EN: "A-aaaaagh!!<end>"
KO: "우, 우와아아아!!<end>"

## 1420408
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無茶をするなと言ったはずだ…。<end>"
EN: "I told you not to do anything reckless...<end>"
KO: "무리하지 말라고 했을 텐데….<end>"

## 1420409
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前が手を汚す必要はない。<end>"
EN: "There's no need for you to dirty your hands.<end>"
KO: "네가 손을 더럽힐 필요는 없어.<end>"

## 1420410
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "汚れるのは…<line-break>俺だけで十分だ。<end>"
EN: "I alone need to get my hands dirty.<end>"
KO: "더러워지는 건… 나 하나면 충분해.<end>"

## 1420411
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ともに歩んでいこうというのか？<line-break>ダルケニスであるこの俺と…。<line-break> <line-break>後悔するぞ…。<end>"
EN: "You would walk beside me? Beside a Darkenith...? You will regret it.<end>"
KO: "함께 걸어가겠다는 건가? 다르케니스인 이 나와…. 후회할 거다….<end>"

## 1420412
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>…すまない。<line-break>馬鹿なことを聞いた。<end>"
EN: "... I'm sorry. That was a foolish question.<end>"
KO: "………………. …미안하다. 바보 같은 걸 물었군.<end>"

## 1420413
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この身体が血と闇で汚されようとも、<line-break>お前を明けない夜に<line-break>閉じ込めることになろうとも…<line-break>俺はお前を放さない…決して…。<end>"
EN: "Even if this body is stained with blood and darkness, even if it means imprisoning you in a night that will never end... I will not let you go. Never.<end>"
KO: "이 몸이 피와 어둠으로 더럽혀지더라도, 너를 끝나지 않는 밤에 가두게 되더라도… 나는 너를 놓지 않아… 절대로….<end>"

## 1420420
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "起きなさい、<value:$28>。<line-break>もう朝よ。<end>"
EN: "Wake up, <value:$28>. It's morning.<end>"
KO: "일어나, <value:$28>. 벌써 아침이야.<end>"

## 1420421
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "朝ご飯が冷めてしまうわよ。<line-break>さあ、早くしなさい、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Your breakfast is getting cold. Come on, hurry up, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아침밥 다 식겠어. 자, 서둘러, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1420422
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、行くのね？<end>"
EN: "So, you're leaving?<end>"
KO: "그럼, 가는 거지?<end>"

## 1420424
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかっていると思うけれど…<line-break>道中、気をつけなさい。<end>"
EN: "I'm sure you know this already, but... be careful on the road.<end>"
KO: "알고 있겠지만… 가는 길 조심해.<end>"

## 1420425
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "支度が済んだら<line-break>すぐに出て行ってしまうのね…。<line-break>あの子も変わらないわ。<end>"
EN: "Off as soon as everything's ready... That child hasn't changed either.<end>"
KO: "준비가 끝나면 바로 떠나 버리는구나…. 그 아이도 변함없네.<end>"

## 1420426
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いつまで気配を消している気？<end>"
EN: "How long do you intend to keep hiding your presence?<end>"
KO: "…언제까지 기척을 숨길 셈이야?<end>"

## 1420427
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幸せそうだな。<line-break>…アスティア。<end>"
EN: "You look happy... Astia.<end>"
KO: "행복해 보이는군. …아스티아.<end>"

## 1420428
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうね。<line-break>バルザー。<end>"
EN: "Yes... Baltzer.<end>"
KO: "…그러네. 발자.<end>"

## 1420429
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我らの使命は終わった。<line-break>次の眠りはおそらく<line-break>永遠のものとなるだろう。<line-break>それでも、幸せといえるのか？<end>"
EN: "Our mission is over. Our next sleep will likely be eternal. Can you still call yourself happy?<end>"
KO: "우리의 사명은 끝났다. 다음 잠은 아마 영원한 것이 되겠지. 그래도 행복이라 할 수 있을까?<end>"

## 1420430
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。<end>"
EN: "Yes.<end>"
KO: "네.<end>"

## 1420431
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "暖かい日の光…<line-break>愛すべき隣人達…<line-break>豊かな大地の恵み…<line-break>そして、<line-break>世界で一番誇らしくて…<line-break>世界で一番愛しいあの子…。<end>"
EN: "Warm sunlight... beloved neighbors... the bounty of this fertile land... and that child, the one I'm proudest of in all the world... the one I love most dearly in all the world...<end>"
KO: "따뜻한 햇빛… 사랑스러운 이웃들… 풍요로운 대지의 은혜… 그리고 세상에서 가장 자랑스럽고 가장 사랑스러운 그 아이….<end>"

## 1420432
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それより他に何もない永遠と<line-break>引き換えになんてできないわ。<end>"
EN: "I could never trade them for an eternity with nothing else in it.<end>"
KO: "그것 외엔 아무것도 없는 영원과 맞바꿀 순 없어.<end>"

## 1420433
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "宿命も運命も全てを捨て…<line-break>新しい時代がくる。<line-break> <line-break>私はその到来を告げるため、<line-break>この世界に生まれたのかもしれない。<end>"
EN: "Destiny, fate, all of it cast aside... a new age is coming. Perhaps I was born into this world to herald its arrival.<end>"
KO: "숙명도 운명도 모두 버리고… 새로운 시대가 온다. 나는 그 도래를 알리기 위해 이 세상에 태어난 걸지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1420434
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうだな。<end>"
EN: "Yes... I suppose so.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군.<end>"

## 1420435
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行くの？<end>"
EN: "Are you leaving?<end>"
KO: "가는 거야?<end>"

## 1420436
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ…。<line-break>エスリンに会う前に<line-break>するべきことがあってな。<end>"
EN: "Yes... There's something I must do before I meet Ethlinn.<end>"
KO: "아아… 에스린을 만나기 전에 해야 할 일이 있어서 말이지.<end>"

## 1420437
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さらばだ、告げる者よ。<line-break>新たな時代に幸多からんことを<line-break>祈っている。<end>"
EN: "Farewell, herald. I pray the new age brings you great happiness.<end>"
KO: "잘 가라, 전하는 자여. 새로운 시대에 많은 행복이 있기를 빈다.<end>"

## 1420438
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫よ。<line-break>あの子のいる世界、<line-break>あの子達の築く世界だもの…<end>"
EN: "It'll be all right. It's a world with that child in it, a world those children will build...<end>"
KO: "괜찮아. 그 아이가 있는 세계, 그 아이들이 만들어 갈 세계니까….<end>"

## 1420439
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幾千万の意思がその幸せを祈る<line-break>新しい時代なんですもの…。<end>"
EN: "It will be a new age, with tens of millions of wills praying for those children's happiness...<end>"
KO: "수천만의 의지가 그 행복을 바라는 새로운 시대니까요….<end>"

## 1420470
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様。<line-break>ご無事でなによりです。<line-break>ゆっくりしていってくださいね。<end>"
EN: "I'm so glad you're safe, <value:$28>. Please, stay and rest awhile.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님. 무사하셔서 다행입니다. 편히 쉬다 가세요.<end>"

## 1420471
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここにいると　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>心が安まるんです。<line-break> 　<line-break>つらかったことや<line-break>悲しかったことを<line-break>みんな忘れられるような気がして…。<end>"
EN: "I feel at peace here. It's as though I can forget all the pain and sadness...<end>"
KO: "여기 있으면 마음이 편안해져요. 힘들었던 일도 슬펐던 일도 전부 잊을 수 있을 것 같아서….<end>"

## 1420472
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネモさんってとても<line-break>やさしい方なんですよ。<end>"
EN: "Nemo is such a kind man.<end>"
KO: "네모 씨는 정말 상냥한 분이에요.<end>"

## 1420473
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当です。<line-break>親切にいろいろと<line-break>教えてくださるんです。<end>"
EN: "He really is. He's been kind enough to teach me all sorts of things.<end>"
KO: "정말이에요. 친절하게 여러 가지를 가르쳐 주세요.<end>"

## 1420474
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日もいい天気ですね。<line-break>たまには庭で昼寝でもどうです？<line-break>…ムリは、よくありませんよ。<end>"
EN: "Lovely weather again today. How about a nap in the garden for a change? You really shouldn't overdo it.<end>"
KO: "오늘도 날씨가 좋네요. 가끔은 정원에서 낮잠이라도 어떠세요? …무리는 좋지 않아요.<end>"

## 1420475
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>また背が伸びたようですね。<end>"
EN: "It looks as though you've grown again, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 또 키가 큰 것 같네요.<end>"

## 1420476
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私が縮んだから？<line-break>…なるほど、そうかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "Or is it simply that I've shrunk? Ah, yes. That may be it.<end>"
KO: "제가 작아져서요? …그렇군요, 그럴지도 모르겠네요.<end>"

## 1420477
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ケリュネイアは<line-break>よくやってくれてますよ。<line-break>わがままな猫２匹の世話をね。<line-break>ああ見えても<line-break>やさしい子なんです。<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia has been doing a fine job, looking after two demanding cats. She may not seem it, but she's a kind girl.<end>"
KO: "케류네이아는 잘해 주고 있어요. 제멋대로인 고양이 두 마리를 돌보느라 말이죠. 저래 보여도 상냥한 아이예요.<end>"

## 1420478
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%2でも、また…、<line-break>いつ闇に飲まれるかわからない。<line-break>今度もマゴスに<line-break>支配されずにいられるかわからない…。<line-break>私、こわい…。<end>もう…限界かも知れない。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>それに<line-break>これ以上、みんなに迷惑…<line-break>かけられない…。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%2But... I don't know when the darkness might swallow me again, or whether I'll be able to stay free of Magos's control next time... I'm scared...<end>I may have reached my limit... And I can't keep burdening everyone like this...<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%2하지만 또… 언제 어둠에 삼켜질지 몰라. 이번에도 마고스에게 지배당하지 않을 수 있을지 모르겠어…. 나, 무서워….<end>이제… 한계일지도 몰라. 게다가 더 이상 모두에게 폐를 끼칠 수도 없어….<end>"

## 1420479
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あまりムリを<line-break>なさらないでくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Please don't push yourself too hard.<end>"
KO: "너무 무리하지 마세요.<end>"

## 1420480
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、昼寝でもしましょうかね。<end>"
EN: "Well then, perhaps it's time for a nap.<end>"
KO: "자, 낮잠이나 자 볼까.<end>"

## 1420482

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様。<line-break>ご無事でなによりです。<line-break>ゆっくりしていってくださいね。<end>"

KO: "<value:$28> 님. 무사하셔서 다행입니다. 편히 쉬다 가세요.<end>"

## 1420486
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の門の島に向かえば、<line-break>もうあとに引き返すことは<line-break>ままならないでしょう。<line-break>充分に力をつけてから<line-break>向かってくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Once you set out for the Island of the Dark Gate, turning back will likely no longer be an option. Make sure you're strong enough before you go.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 문 섬으로 향하면 이제 되돌아오기가 쉽지 않을 겁니다. 충분히 힘을 기른 뒤에 가 주세요.<end>"

## 1420487

## 1420490
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ケリュネイアは<line-break>よくやってくれてますよ。<line-break>わがままな猫２匹の世話をね。<line-break>ああ見えても<line-break>やさしい子なんです。<end>"

## 1420491
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "昔の仲間の気配があるんだよな。<line-break>エンシャントから漂ってくる。<line-break>気をつけた方がいいぜ。<line-break>あそこで何者かが<line-break>なんかやろうとしてやがる。<line-break>ま、ただの俺のカンだけどよ。<end>"
EN: "I sense the presence of an old comrade drifting from Ancient. You'd better be careful. Someone over there is up to something. Well, that's just my gut talking.<end>"
KO: "옛 동료의 기척이 느껴져. 엔샨트 쪽에서 흘러오고 있어. 조심하는 게 좋겠어. 거기서 누군가 뭔가 꾸미고 있는 것 같아. 뭐, 그냥 내 감이지만.<end>"

## 1420492
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1420506
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長！お祝いするゴブ！ごちそうゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Captain! We have to celebrate, gob! With a feast!<end>"
KO: "단장! 축하한다 고브! 잔치다 고브.<end>"

## 1420507
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "雨の日も風の日も<line-break>ゴブリン質になった日も<line-break>諦めずにがんばってきてよかったゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Through rain and wind, even when we were taken Goblin hostage, we never gave up. It was all worth it, gob!<end>"
KO: "비 오는 날도 바람 부는 날도 고블린 인질이 된 날도 포기하지 않고 버텨 온 보람이 있는 고브!<end>"

## 1420508
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マルーン！<line-break>さっそく宿屋の手配を命じるゴブ！<line-break>最高の食事を揃えるゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Maroon! I order you to make arrangements with the inn at once, gob! Have them prepare the finest meal they can!<end>"
KO: "마룬! 당장 여관 준비를 명령하는 고브! 최고의 음식을 마련하는 고브!<end>"

## 1420509
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長！　いってくるゴ…<end>"
EN: "Captain! I'm off, go―<end>"
KO: "단장! 다녀올게고….<end>"

## 1420510
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Not yet, gob.<end>"
KO: "아직 고브.<end>"

## 1420511
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なにをするゴブ？<line-break>急がないと晩ごはんに<line-break>間に合わないゴブ。<end>"
EN: "What are you doing, gob? Hurry, or it'll be too late for dinner, gob.<end>"
KO: "뭐 하는 고브? 서두르지 않으면 저녁밥에 늦는다 고브.<end>"

## 1420512
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごちそうは食べたいゴブ。<line-break>でも、リベルダムにはおなかを<line-break>すかせた人が一杯いるゴブ。<end>"
EN: "I want a feast too, gob. But there are so many hungry people in Liberdam.<end>"
KO: "맛있는 건 먹고 싶은 고브. 하지만 리벨덤에는 배고픈 사람이 잔뜩 있는 고브.<end>"

## 1420513
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごちそうでなくても、<line-break>おなかは一杯にできるゴブ。<line-break> <line-break>ごちそうを食べるのに使うお金を<line-break>分ければ、おなかをすかせた人が<line-break>何人か救えるゴブ。<end>"
EN: "It doesn't have to be a feast to fill an empty belly, gob. If we shared the money we'd spend on one, we could save some of those hungry people.<end>"
KO: "진수성찬이 아니어도, 배는 채울 수 있다 고브. 진수성찬에 쓸 돈을나눠 주면, 굶주린 사람을몇 명은 구할 수 있다 고브.<end>"

## 1420514
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おなかをすかせた人が<line-break>いなくなってからでも<line-break>ごちそうはきっと食べれるゴブ。<end>"
EN: "We can still have a feast once no one is going hungry, gob.<end>"
KO: "배고픈 사람이 없어지고 나서도 진수성찬은 분명 먹을 수 있다 고브.<end>"

## 1420515
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…す、凄いゴブ。<line-break>オルナットがごちそうを<line-break>あきらめたゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Th-That's incredible, gob. Ornut gave up a feast!<end>"
KO: "…대, 대단하다 고브. 오르나트가 잔치를 포기했다 고브!<end>"

## 1420516
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…オルナットの言うことが正しいゴブ。<line-break>我ら誇り高いゴブゴブ団は、<line-break>飢えと貧困と戦ってきたゴブが、<line-break>まだ、完全に勝利していないゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Ornut is right, gob. Our proud Gob-Gob Gang has fought hunger and poverty, but we have yet to win a complete victory!<end>"
KO: "…오르나트 말이 맞다 고브. 우리 자랑스러운 고브고브단은 굶주림과 가난에 맞서 싸워 왔지만 아직 완전히 이기진 못했다 고브!<end>"

## 1420517
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マルーン！　ごちそうを変更して<line-break>炊き出しの準備をするゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Maroon! Change of plans―prepare meals for the hungry instead of a feast, gob!<end>"
KO: "마룬! 잔치를 변경해서 배식 준비를 하는 고브!<end>"

## 1420518
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、団長…<line-break>自分は今ほどゴブゴブ団に入って<line-break>誇らしいと思ったことはないゴブ！<line-break>行ってくるゴブ。<end>"
EN: "C-Captain... I've never been prouder to belong to the Gob-Gob Gang, gob! I'm on my way!<end>"
KO: "다, 단장… 지금만큼 고브고브단에 들어온 걸 자랑스럽게 생각한 적이 없다고브! 다녀오겠다고브.<end>"

## 1420519
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フゴー殿、ダイダロ殿。<line-break>というワケで、<line-break>ゴブゴブ団は前進するゴブ。<line-break>ゴブゴブ団を認めてくれたことは<line-break>嬉しかったゴブ。<line-break>では、さらばゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Lord Hugo, Lord Daedalo. And so, the Gob-Gob Gang marches onward, gob. We were glad to have your recognition. Farewell, gob!<end>"
KO: "휴고 님, 다이다로 님. 그런 이유로 고브고브단은 전진한다 고브. 고브고브단을 인정해 줘서 기뻤다 고브. 그럼 안녕이다 고브!<end>"

## 1420520
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　愚かな者たちよ…　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　死こそ、汝らにとって<line-break>　　　　　最高の結末であったろうに…<end>"
EN: "Fools... Death would have been the finest end you could have hoped for...<end>"
KO: "어리석은 자들이여… 죽음이야말로 너희에게 최고의 결말이었을 텐데….<end>"

## 1420521
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　我は竜王…　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　世界の牧者、神の代理なり…。　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　<value:$28>よ…<line-break>　　　我の息吹にかかって消えるがよい…！　　<end>"
EN: "I am the Dragon King... shepherd of the world and God's representative. <value:$28>... Let my breath sweep you away!<end>"
KO: "나는 용왕…. 세계의 목자, 신의 대리자다…. <value:$28>여… 나의 숨결에 휩쓸려 사라져라…!<end>"

## 1420522
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　汝は、その力をどうするつもりなのだ…　　<line-break>　　　世界すら滅ぼす可能性のある力を…　　　<line-break>　　生ある限り、そこには葛藤あるのみぞ…　　<line-break>　　　　　　愚かなり…、愚かなり…　　　　　<end>"
EN: "What do you intend to do with that power... power that could destroy even the world? Where there is life, there can only be conflict... How foolish... How foolish...<end>"
KO: "그대는 그 힘을 어찌할 셈인가…. 세계조차 멸망시킬 가능성이 있는 힘을…. 살아 있는 한 그곳에는 갈등뿐…. 어리석구나…, 어리석구나….<end>"

## 1420523
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　我は、うつつの身を持つ最後の神…　　　<line-break>　　　　人の子よ…、すべての神を殺し、　　　<line-break>　　　人はおのが足で歩めると言えるのか？　　<line-break>　　　　　　愚かなり…、愚かなり…　　　　　<end>"
EN: "I am the last god embodied in this world... Child of man... having slain all the gods, can humankind claim to walk on its own two feet? How foolish... How foolish...<end>"
KO: "나는 현세의 육신을 지닌 마지막 신…. 인간의 아이여…, 모든 신을 죽이고 인간이 자기 발로 걸을 수 있다고 말할 수 있느냐? 어리석구나…, 어리석구나….<end>"

## 1420524
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "終わったな。<end>"
EN: "It's over.<end>"
KO: "끝났군.<end>"

## 1420525
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、戻ろう。<line-break>戦いが終わっても、<line-break>やらねばならぬことは残っている。<end>"
EN: "Come. Let's go back. The battle may be over, but there is still work to be done.<end>"
KO: "자, 돌아가자. 싸움이 끝나도 해야 할 일은 남아 있다.<end>"

## 1420526
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王が倒れましたか…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>うつしよの最後の神が消え、<line-break>人は自分たちの力だけで<line-break>歩まねばならなくなりましたね。<end>"
EN: "The Dragon King has fallen, then... The last god in the mortal world is gone, and humankind must now walk forward alone, under its own power.<end>"
KO: "용왕이 쓰러졌습니까…. 현세의 마지막 신이 사라지고 인간은 자기 힘만으로 걸어가야 하게 됐군요.<end>"

## 1420527
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この日が訪れたことを<line-break>喜ぶべきか悲しむべきか…。<end>"
EN: "Should we rejoice that this day has come, or mourn it...?<end>"
KO: "이날이 찾아온 것을 기뻐해야 할지 슬퍼해야 할지….<end>"

## 1420528
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですね。<line-break>今は、人の可能性を信じて、<line-break>時の流れに身を任せましょうか。<line-break>あなたが開いた新たな未来の扉は、<line-break>無限の可能性に満ちていますものね。<end>"
EN: "I suppose, for now, we should place our faith in humanity's potential and let time run its course. The door you opened onto a new future is filled with limitless possibilities.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 지금은 인간의 가능성을 믿고 시간의 흐름에 몸을 맡겨 보죠. 당신이 연 새로운 미래의 문은 무한한 가능성으로 가득하니까요.<end>"

## 1420530
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうかしましたか？<end>"
EN: "Is something wrong?<end>"
KO: "무슨 일인가요?<end>"

## 1420531
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんでもない。<end>"
EN: "It's nothing.<end>"
KO: "아무것도 아니야.<end>"

## 1420532
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだやることがある。<end>"
EN: "There's still something I have to do.<end>"
KO: "아직 할 일이 있어.<end>"

## 1420533
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですか。<end>"
EN: "...I see.<end>"
KO: "…그렇습니까.<end>"

## 1420534
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、先に行ってますね。<end>"
EN: "Then I'll go on ahead.<end>"
KO: "그럼 먼저 가 있을게요.<end>"

## 1420535
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、戻りましょうか。<line-break>無事な姿をみんなに見せないと。<line-break>さあ、行きましょう。<end>"
EN: "Shall we go back, then? Everyone needs to see that you're safe. Come, let's go.<end>"
KO: "그럼 돌아갈까요. 무사한 모습을 모두에게 보여 줘야죠. 자, 갑시다.<end>"

## 1420536
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　神聖暦<value:$24>年<value:$25>月　　　　<line-break>後に多くの吟遊詩人に歌われるこの騒乱の後<line-break>　　ディンガル帝国とロストール王国は　　<line-break>　　　　　和平条約を締結した。　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Sacred Era, Year <value:$24>, Month <value:$25>. After this upheaval, which many bards would later sing of, the Dyneskal Empire and the Kingdom of Rostorl concluded a peace treaty.<end>"
KO: "신성력 <value:$24>년 <value:$25>월 후에 수많은 음유시인이 노래하게 될 이 소란 뒤 딩갈 제국과 로스톨 왕국은 평화조약을 체결했다.<end>"

## 1420537

## 1420539
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来たか…。<line-break>アスティアの子よ。<line-break>我を倒さぬかぎり、闇の門は閉じぬ。<end>"
EN: "So you have come... Child of Astia. The Dark Gate will not close unless you defeat me.<end>"
KO: "왔는가…. 아스티아의 아이여. 나를 쓰러뜨리지 않는 한 어둠의 문은 닫히지 않는다.<end>"

## 1420540
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "母も戻っては来ぬ。<line-break>お前がここに来るのも必然というワケか。<line-break> <line-break>よかろう。<line-break>このヴァシュタールを滅ぼしてみよ。<end>"
EN: "Nor will Mother return. So it was inevitable that you would come here. Very well. Destroy Vashtar, if you can.<end>"
KO: "어머니도 돌아오지 않는다. 네가 여기 오는 것도 필연이라는 건가. 좋다. 이 바슈타르를 멸해 보아라.<end>"

## 1420541
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…無駄だ。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>いかなる力を持ってしても<line-break>我を滅することはできん。<line-break>我は不滅の存在。<end>"
EN: "It is futile. No power can destroy me. I am immortal.<end>"
KO: "…소용없다. 어떤 힘으로도 나를 멸할 수 없다. 나는 불멸의 존재다.<end>"

## 1420542
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "永遠の終わりを探し求める<line-break>朽ち果てぬ骸。<line-break>お前の憎しみも良い暇つぶしとなる。<line-break>また、会おう。<line-break>アスティアの子よ。<end>"
EN: "A corpse that will never decay, seeking an end to eternity. Your hatred will make a pleasant diversion. We shall meet again, child of Astia.<end>"
KO: "영원의 끝을 찾아 헤매는 썩지 않는 시체. 네 증오도 좋은 심심풀이가 되겠군. 또 만나자, 아스티아의 아이여.<end>"

## 1420543
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、これはウルグ様の力…。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>お前は一体！<line-break>まあ…よい。<line-break>お前こそ我が求めていた存在。<end>"
EN: "Th-this is the power of Ulugh... What are you? No matter. You are the one I have sought.<end>"
KO: "이, 이건 울그 님의 힘…. 너는 대체! 뭐… 됐다. 네가 바로 내가 찾던 존재다.<end>"

## 1420544
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、その力で我を撃て。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>闇の中の闇。<line-break>ウルグ様の力であれば我も<line-break>眠ることができよう。<end>"
EN: "Now strike me with that power. Darkness within darkness... With the power of Ulugh, perhaps even I may sleep.<end>"
KO: "자, 그 힘으로 나를 쏴라. 어둠 속의 어둠. 울그 님의 힘이라면 나도 잠들 수 있겠지.<end>"

## 1420545
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さらばだ…アスティアの子よ。<line-break>礼を言うぞ。<end>"
EN: "Farewell... child of Astia. You have my thanks.<end>"
KO: "잘 있어라… 아스티아의 아이여. 감사한다.<end>"

## 1420546
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　神聖暦<value:$24>年<value:$25>月　　　　<line-break>後に多くの吟遊詩人に歌われるこの騒乱の後<line-break>　　ディンガル帝国とロストール王国は　　<line-break>　　　　　和平条約を締結した。　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Sacred Era, Year <value:$24>, Month <value:$25>. After this upheaval, which many bards would later sing of, the Dyneskal Empire and the Kingdom of Rostorl concluded a peace treaty.<end>"
KO: "신성력 <value:$24>년 <value:$25>월 후에 수많은 음유시인이 노래하게 될 이 소란 뒤 딩갈 제국과 로스톨 왕국은 평화조약을 체결했다.<end>"

## 1420547
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪熱く燃える　正しい魂～<end>"
EN: "♪A righteous soul, blazing hot―<end>"
KO: "♪뜨겁게 타오르는 올바른 영혼~<end>"

## 1420548
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冗談じゃないぜ…。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>いくらギルドの依頼とはいえ<line-break>なんで、俺が<line-break>ガルドランの護衛なんか…。<end>"
EN: "You've got to be kidding me... Guild job or not, why do I have to escort Girldlan...?<end>"
KO: "농담하지 마…. 아무리 길드 의뢰라 해도 왜 내가 갈드란의 호위 같은 걸….<end>"

## 1420549
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、いいんじゃないかな。<line-break>ガルドランも改心したみたいだし。<line-break>少しでも悪い事する人が減るなら、<line-break>目立つガルドランを<line-break>防犯のマスコットに<line-break>するのも一つの手だと思うよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, maybe it's not such a bad idea. Girldlan does seem to have changed his ways. If it means fewer people doing wrong, making someone as conspicuous as Girldlan a crime-prevention mascot might just work.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 괜찮지 않을까. 갈드란도 개심한 것 같고. 조금이라도 나쁜 일을 하는 사람이 줄어든다면 눈에 띄는 갈드란을 방범 마스코트로 쓰는 것도 한 방법이라고 생각해.<end>"

## 1420550
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねぇ。<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "있잖아. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1420551
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪天を切り裂く　正義の光～<end>"
EN: "♪The light of justice, rending the heavens―<end>"
KO: "♪하늘을 가르는 정의의 빛~<end>"

## 1420552
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うおうおー。俺様伝説絶好調！<line-break>俺様の熱い魂が燃えている！<end>"
EN: "Woooaah! My legend's on a roll! My fiery soul is burning bright!<end>"
KO: "우오오~. 이 몸의 전설 절정! 이 몸의 뜨거운 영혼이 타오른다!<end>"

## 1420553
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…分断の山脈に置ける兵数削減は<line-break>予定どおり進行中である。<line-break> <line-break>…レムオン卿。<line-break>何か不満があるようだな<end>"
EN: "The reduction of troop strength in the Dividing Mountains is proceeding as planned. Lord Lemghon, you appear dissatisfied.<end>"
KO: "…분단의 산맥에 배치한 병력 감축은 예정대로 진행 중이다. …레무온 경. 뭔가 불만이 있는 모양이군.<end>"

## 1420554
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勿論だ…。<line-break>こちらは国家主席である<line-break>ティアナ王女が出席している。<line-break>そちらはいつになっても皇帝代理だ。<line-break>誠意を持って和平交渉に<line-break>当たっているのか疑ってみたくもなる。<end>"
EN: "Of course I am. Princess Tiana, our head of state, is here in person, while your side is still represented by an imperial proxy. One cannot help questioning your sincerity in these peace negotiations.<end>"
KO: "물론이다…. 이쪽은 국가수반인 티아나 왕녀가 참석하고 있다. 그런데 너희 쪽은 언제까지고 황제 대리뿐이지. 성의를 가지고 평화 협상에 임하고 있는지 의심하고 싶어질 정도다.<end>"

## 1420556
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "玄武将軍はディンガル王家の<line-break>正当な後継者である<line-break>感情的な発言は控えてもらいたい。<line-break>それに卿は本来なら<line-break>ここには存在しては<line-break>いけない人間なのだからな…。<end>"
EN: "General Genbu is the rightful heir to the Dyneskal royal house. I must ask you to refrain from emotional outbursts. Besides, my lord, you are a man who by rights should not even exist...<end>"
KO: "현무장군은 딩갈 왕가의 정당한 후계자다. 감정적인 발언은 삼가 주길 바란다. 게다가 경은 본래라면 여기에 존재해선 안 될 인간이니까….<end>"

## 1420557
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そちらがご不快になられるのも<line-break>分からない訳ではありません。<line-break>この問題は近日中に解決いたしますわ。<end>"
EN: "I can understand why you are displeased. This matter will be resolved in the coming days.<end>"
KO: "…그쪽이 불쾌해하는 것도 이해 못 할 일은 아닙니다. 이 문제는 가까운 시일 안에 해결하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1420558
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どういうことですか？<line-break>説明を求めます。<end>"
EN: "What do you mean? I demand an explanation.<end>"
KO: "무슨 뜻입니까? 설명을 요구합니다.<end>"

## 1420559
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我々ディンガル帝国は<line-break>新たなる皇帝を擁立するのです。<line-break> <line-break>人類の革新を進めるに相応しい人物を<line-break>…ね<end>"
EN: "We of the Dyneskal Empire intend to install a new emperor―someone worthy of furthering Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "우리 딘갈 제국은 새로운 황제를 세울 겁니다. 인류의 혁신을 추진하기에 걸맞은 인물을… 말이죠.<end>"

## 1420560
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まさか！？<line-break>そのような無法が<line-break>許されるとでも思っているのか？<end>"
EN: "You cannot mean it! Do you truly think such lawlessness will be tolerated?<end>"
KO: "…설마!? 그런 무법이 허용될 거라 생각하는 건가?<end>"

## 1420561
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そちらでは無法と<line-break>見えるのかもしれません。<line-break> <line-break>しかし、我が国は<line-break>血筋よりも能力を尊びます。<end>"
EN: "It may appear lawless to you. But our nation prizes ability above bloodline.<end>"
KO: "그쪽에서는 무법으로 보일지 모르겠습니다. 하지만 우리나라는 혈통보다 능력을 중시합니다.<end>"

## 1420562
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "血筋など王家の人間と契りを結べば<line-break>手に入るもの。<line-break> <line-break>そんなものより、人間として、<line-break>英雄として偉大なソウルを持つものが<line-break>必要なのです。<end>"
EN: "A bloodline can be acquired simply by joining oneself to a member of the royal house. What we need is something greater: one who possesses a great Soul, both as a person and as a hero.<end>"
KO: "혈통 따윈 왕가의 사람과 인연을 맺으면 얻을 수 있는 것. 그런 것보다 인간으로서, 영웅으로서 위대한 소울을 가진 자가 필요합니다.<end>"

## 1420563
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "……！<line-break>認められません。<line-break>あの方の力がどれほど大きいのか。<line-break>あなた方だって分かっているはずです。<line-break>あなた方は血塗られた覇道を<line-break>再び歩むつもりなのですか！<end>"
EN: "I will not accept this! You know as well as we do how great that person's power is. Do you intend to tread the bloodstained path of conquest once again?<end>"
KO: "……! 인정할 수 없습니다. 그분의 힘이 얼마나 큰지 당신들도 알고 있을 겁니다. 당신들은 피로 물든 패도를 다시 걸으려는 겁니까!<end>"

## 1420564
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何用だ…セバスチャン<end>"
EN: "What is it, Sebastian?<end>"
KO: "무슨 용건이냐… 세바스찬.<end>"

## 1420565
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Forgive the interruption, Lord Lemghon. A word in private...<end>"
KO: "회의 중에 실례하겠습니다. 레무온 님… 잠시 귀를….<end>"

## 1420582

## 1420584
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ダメです。そんなの絶対認めません！<line-break>あの方はロストールには<line-break>無くてはならない方です。<line-break>そ、それにザギヴさんと結婚なんて、<line-break>絶対に認めません。<end>"

## 1420585

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
